      waterDrop(ctx,x,y,.075*(1-.2*q),1-clamp((q-.7)/.3,0,1));
    }
    // nervous cheek lines
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(90,140,200,.7)';ctx.lineWidth=.04;ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const a=.5+Math.sin(t*5+i)*.1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.62+i*.12,-.82+i*.02);ctx.lineTo(-.6+i*.12,-.82+.22+a*.05);ctx.stroke();}
  };

  FX.dizzy=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const n=5,items=[];
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=t*2.6+i*TAU/n;items.push({a,s:Math.sin(a),c:Math.cos(a),i});}
    items.sort((a,b)=>a.s-b.s);
    items.forEach(o=>{
      const depth=.72+.28*(o.s+1)/2,x=o.c*1.0,y=-1.02+o.s*.3;
      ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=.82+.18*(o.s+1)/2;
      ctx.fillStyle='#FFD21F';ctx.strokeStyle='#E5890A';ctx.lineWidth=.045;ctx.lineJoin='round';
      starPath(ctx,x,y,.21*depth,t*3+o.i,5,.46);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    });
    // swirl above the head
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,210,31,.55)';ctx.lineWidth=.03;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();
    for(let a=0;a<TAU*1.6;a+=.2){const r=.12+a*.05,x=Math.cos(a+t*4)*r,y=-1.28+Math.sin(a+t*4)*r*.45;a?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y);}
    ctx.stroke();
  };

  FX.hearts=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    softDisc(ctx,-H.face.cx,.3,.42,[255,96,140],.72);softDisc(ctx,H.face.cx,.3,.42,[255,96,140],.72);
    const n=Math.round(7*clamp(k,.4,2)),cols=['#FF4D79','#FF86AE','#E8224F'];
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.55+i/n),x=(hs(i)-.5)*1.6+Math.sin(t*2.3+i*2)*.18,y=-.95-p*2.1;
      const pop=p<.14?ease(p/.14)*1.25:1,s=(.12+.17*hs(i+3))*pop,a=1-clamp((p-.7)/.3,0,1);
      ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=a;ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(Math.sin(t*2+i)*.35);
      ctx.fillStyle=cols[i%3];ctx.strokeStyle='#A8143A';ctx.lineWidth=s*.16;heartPath(ctx,0,0,s);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.75)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(-s*.5,-s*.45,s*.2,s*.12,-.6,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  };

  function zPath(ctx,s){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-s,-s);ctx.lineTo(s,-s);ctx.lineTo(-s,s);ctx.lineTo(s,s);}
  FX.sleep=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.42+i/3),x=.72+p*.95+Math.sin(p*6+i)*.07,y=-.75-p*1.55,s=.1+p*.2,a=Math.pow(Math.sin(p*Math.PI),.7);
      ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=a;ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(-.22);ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineCap='round';
      ctx.strokeStyle='#4E78CF';ctx.lineWidth=s*.62;zPath(ctx,s);ctx.stroke();
      ctx.strokeStyle='#CFE0FF';ctx.lineWidth=s*.34;zPath(ctx,s);ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // snot bubble breathing in and out
    const br=.09+.1*(.5+.5*Math.sin(t*1.7));
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(190,228,255,.7)';ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(110,165,230,.95)';ctx.lineWidth=.03;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(.1,.34+br*.5,br,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.85)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(.1-br*.35,.34+br*.5-br*.4,br*.22,br*.12,-.6,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
  };

  FX.electric=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const step=Math.floor(t*14);
    softDisc(ctx,0,0,1.7,[110,190,255],.5);
    ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.shadowColor='#7DD3FF';ctx.shadowBlur=.25*H.R;
    const bolts=Math.round(7*clamp(k,.5,1.6));
    for(let i=0;i<bolts;i++){
      if(hs(step*3.1+i*1.7)<.22)continue;
      const base=i*TAU/bolts+hs(step+i)*.8,r0=1.02,r1=1.55+hs(step*2+i)*.55,segs=5;
      const pts=[];
      for(let s=0;s<=segs;s++){
        const r=lerp(r0,r1,s/segs),off=(hs(step*5+i*7+s)-.5)*.34*(s>0&&s<segs?1:0);
        pts.push([Math.cos(base)*r-Math.sin(base)*off,Math.sin(base)*r+Math.cos(base)*off]);
      }
      const draw=()=>{ctx.beginPath();pts.forEach((p,j)=>j?ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1]):ctx.moveTo(p[0],p[1]));ctx.stroke();};
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(90,175,255,.75)';ctx.lineWidth=.11;draw();
      ctx.strokeStyle='#FFFFFF';ctx.lineWidth=.04;draw();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    for(let i=0;i<10;i++){
      const a=hs(step+i*3)*TAU,r=1.05+hs(step*2+i)*.8;
      ctx.fillStyle=rgba(255,245,160,.9);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r,.035,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
  };

  FX.sparkle=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const n=Math.round(8*clamp(k,.5,2)),cols=['#FFFFFF','#FFF2A8','#CDEBFF','#FFD6F2'];
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const a=hs(i)*TAU,r=1.05+hs(i+7)*.75,x=Math.cos(a)*r,y=Math.sin(a)*r*.95-.1;
      const tw=.5+.5*Math.sin(t*4.2+i*2.3),s=(.1+.2*hs(i+2))*(.25+.75*tw*tw);
      ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#FFF2A8';ctx.shadowBlur=.2*H.R;
      ctx.fillStyle=cols[i%4];ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(230,170,40,.65)';ctx.lineWidth=.018;
      starPath(ctx,x,y,s*2.3,t*.6+i,4,.2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  };

  /* ---------- public API ---------- */
  function draw(ctx,frame,type,tt,cfg){
    const fn=FX[type];if(!fn||!frame||!(frame.r>0))return;
    cfg=cfg||{};
    const k=clamp(cfg.k==null?1:cfg.k,0,2.5),size=clamp(cfg.size==null?1:cfg.size,.25,2.5);
    const R=frame.r*size;
    const hand=fn.atHand&&frame.hand?frame.hand:null;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha*=clamp(k,0,1);
    if(hand){ctx.translate(hand.x+(cfg.ox||0)*frame.r,hand.y+(cfg.oy||0)*frame.r);ctx.scale(R*.85,R*.85);}
    else{
      ctx.translate(frame.x+(cfg.ox||0)*frame.r,frame.y+(cfg.oy||0)*frame.r);
      ctx.rotate(frame.rot||0);ctx.scale(frame.flip?-1:1,1);ctx.scale(R,R);
    }
    ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
    try{fn(ctx,Number(tt)||0,k,{R:R,face:frame.face||F0});}catch(e){try{console.warn('Character effect',type,e);}catch(_e){}}
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* Tiny thumbnails: the real effect, drawn over a stylised head, at several moments in time.
     They fill the timeline effect clip so it reads as a frame-by-frame strip. */
  const thumbCache=Object.create(null);
  /* v338: the frame artwork is centred exactly in the middle of the thumbnail. Effects such as
     Anger/Fire/Hearts sit far above the head, Tears/Blood far below it, so the old fixed head
     position pushed the visible art up or down inside the timeline frame. We measure the union of
     the real painted pixels over all 8 phases (same offset for every frame, so the strip does not
     jitter) and translate that box to the centre of the 56x62 thumbnail. */
  const thumbBoxCache=Object.create(null);
  function thumbPaint(x,cx,cy,type,phase){
    const r=11;
    x.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.14)';x.fillRect(cx-4,cy+r-2,8,8);
    const g=x.createRadialGradient(cx-3,cy-4,2,cx,cy,r);g.addColorStop(0,'#FFE0C6');g.addColorStop(1,'#E8B08A');
    x.fillStyle=g;x.beginPath();x.arc(cx,cy,r,0,TAU);x.fill();
    x.fillStyle='#3A2A3E';x.beginPath();x.arc(cx-4.4,cy+.8,1.3,0,TAU);x.arc(cx+4.4,cy+.8,1.3,0,TAU);x.fill();
    x.strokeStyle='#7A4A44';x.lineWidth=1;x.beginPath();x.arc(cx,cy+4,3,.2,Math.PI-.2);x.stroke();
    const frame={x:cx,y:cy,r:r,rot:0,flip:false,hand:{x:cx+17,y:cy+7}};
    draw(x,frame,type,.12+phase*.17,{k:1,size:FX[type]&&FX[type].atHand?.8:1});
  }
  function thumbBox(type){
    if(thumbBoxCache[type]!==undefined)return thumbBoxCache[type];
    let box=null;
    try{
      const W=168,H=186,OX=56,OY=58;           /* roomy work canvas so tall effects are never clipped */
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=W;c.height=H;
      const x=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
      let x0=1e9,y0=1e9,x1=-1,y1=-1;
      for(let p=0;p<8;p++){
        x.clearRect(0,0,W,H);thumbPaint(x,OX+28,OY+29,type,p);
        const d=x.getImageData(0,0,W,H).data;
        for(let yy=0;yy<H;yy++){for(let xx=0;xx<W;xx++){
          if(d[(yy*W+xx)*4+3]>12){if(xx<x0)x0=xx;if(xx>x1)x1=xx;if(yy<y0)y0=yy;if(yy>y1)y1=yy;}
        }}
      }
      if(x1>=x0&&y1>=y0)box={x0:x0-OX,y0:y0-OY,x1:x1+1-OX,y1:y1+1-OY};
    }catch(_e){box=null;}
    thumbBoxCache[type]=box;return box;
  }
  function thumb(type,i){
    const phase=((Number(i)||0)%8+8)%8,key=type+'|'+phase;
    if(thumbCache[key])return thumbCache[key];
    let url='';
    try{
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=56;c.height=62;const x=c.getContext('2d');
      x.clearRect(0,0,56,62);
      const box=thumbBox(type);
      x.save();
      if(box){
        const bw=Math.max(1,box.x1-box.x0),bh=Math.max(1,box.y1-box.y0);
        const s=Math.min(1,52/bw,58/bh);                      /* shrink only if it would not fit */
        x.translate(28,31);x.scale(s,s);x.translate(-(box.x0+box.x1)/2,-(box.y0+box.y1)/2);
        thumbPaint(x,28,29,type,phase);
      }else{
        thumbPaint(x,28,29,type,phase);
      }
      x.restore();
      url=c.toDataURL('image/png');
    }catch(_e){url='';}
    thumbCache[key]=url;return url;
  }
  function frameInner(type,i,fallbackSvg){
    if(!FX[type])return fallbackSvg||'';
    const u=thumb(type,i);
    return u?'<img class="ev-v334-frame-img" alt="" draggable="false" src="'+u+'">':(fallbackSvg||'');
  }

  window.ethvelleCharFxLib={types:TYPES,ids:TYPES.map(x=>x[0]),draw,thumb,frameInner,has:t=>!!FX[t],_fx:FX};
})();

</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v411-character-effects-pack">
/* Ethvelle v411 - extra CHARACTER effects.
   * Crying / eye-drop family drawn as transparent water-blue (gradient, rim light, white glints).
   * Emotion, aura and fighting effects (blush, question, idea, music, surprise, frozen, poison,
     power aura, dark aura, punch hit, round 2D smoke).
   Positions come from H.face (eye / cheek / nose / temple anchors measured from the character's own
   head art when it is available), so the art lands on the face of every model, not on a fixed spot.
   All coordinates are head units: origin = head centre, 1 = head radius, up = -y. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const lib=window.ethvelleCharFxLib;
  if(!lib||!lib._fx||window.__evCharFxPack)return;
  const FX=lib._fx,TAU=Math.PI*2;
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const fr=x=>x-Math.floor(x);
  const hs=i=>fr(Math.sin((i+1)*127.1+37.7)*43758.5453);
  const ease=t=>{t=clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t);};
  const lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t;
  const rgba=(r,g,b,a)=>'rgba('+r+','+g+','+b+','+clamp(a,0,1)+')';
  const F0={fw:1,ex:.4,cx:.52,ey:.08,ny:.34,my:.5,sideX:.92};
  const face=H=>(H&&H.face)||F0;

  /* ---------- shared water look: transparent blue, rim-lit, white glints ---------- */
  function dropShape(ctx,x,y,s){
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y-s*1.75);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-s*.2,y-s*.78,x-s,y-s*.15,x-s,y+s*.38);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-s,y+s*1.02,x+s,y+s*1.02,x+s,y+s*.38);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+s,y-s*.15,x+s*.2,y-s*.78,x,y-s*1.75);ctx.closePath();
  }
  function waterDrop(ctx,x,y,s,a){
    if(a<=.01)return;
    ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=a;
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(x,y-s*1.75,x,y+s);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(215,242,255,.78)');g.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(110,196,255,.6)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(36,128,230,.72)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,98,196,.8)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.012,s*.22);
    dropShape(ctx,x,y,s);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.95)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x-s*.38,y+s*.1,s*.17,s*.34,.25,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.65)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x+s*.36,y+s*.58,s*.1,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  /* a flowing ribbon of water along a quadratic path with moving highlights */
  function waterStream(ctx,x0,y0,cx,cy,x1,y1,w,t,seed){
    ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(22,104,204,.5)';ctx.lineWidth=w*1.28;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x0,y0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx,cy,x1,y1);ctx.stroke();
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(x0,y0,x1,y1);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(170,226,255,.7)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(70,162,250,.62)');
    ctx.strokeStyle=g;ctx.lineWidth=w;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x0,y0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx,cy,x1,y1);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([w*1.5,w*2.3]);ctx.lineDashOffset=-t*w*5+seed;
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.92)';ctx.lineWidth=w*.3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x0-w*.16,y0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx-w*.16,cy,x1-w*.16,y1);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function wetEye(ctx,x,y,s,t,k){
    // glassy, tear-filled lower lid with a shimmering surface
    const sh=.5+.5*Math.sin(t*5+k);
    ctx.save();
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x-.04,y-.04,.01,x,y,.3);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(220,244,255,.75)');g.addColorStop(.6,'rgba(110,196,255,.52)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(70,160,250,.0)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y+.02,.3,.2,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(26,112,214,.62)';ctx.lineWidth=.035;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y+.05,.24,.14,0,.1,Math.PI-.1);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(.78+.2*sh)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x-s*.09,y-.05,.05,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x+s*.06,y+.01,.025,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function ripple(ctx,x,y,p,a){
    if(p<=0||p>=1)return;
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(120,205,255,'+((1-p)*.8*a)+')';ctx.lineWidth=.03;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,.06+p*.22,.025+p*.08,0,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();
  }

  /* ---------- crying family ---------- */
  FX.teary=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H);
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){
      const x=s*f.ex,y=f.ey;
      wetEye(ctx,x,y,s,t,s);
      // a heavy drop swelling on the lower lid, then sliding off
      const p=fr(t*.5+(s>0?.3:0)),sw=ease(p/.7),sz=.045+.06*sw;
      if(p<.72)waterDrop(ctx,x+s*.03,y+.2+sw*.12,sz,1);
      else{const q=(p-.72)/.28;waterDrop(ctx,x+s*.05,y+.32+q*q*1.05,sz*(1-.2*q),1-q);}
    }
  };
  FX.cryStream=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H),chinY=.98;
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){
      const x=s*f.ex,y=f.ey;
      wetEye(ctx,x,y,s,t,s);
      waterStream(ctx,x,y+.12,s*(f.ex+.14),y+.55,s*(f.ex+.06),chinY,.11,t,s*3);
      // drips running off the chin
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
        const p=fr(t*.62+i/3+(s>0?.17:0)),yy=chinY-.02+p*p*2.05,sz=.075+.03*Math.sin(p*Math.PI);
        waterDrop(ctx,s*(f.ex+.06)+Math.sin(p*5+i)*.025,yy,sz,1-clamp((p-.74)/.26,0,1));
      }
      // beads sliding down the cheek
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
        const p=fr(t*.7+i*.5+(s>0?.23:0)),yy=y+.2+p*(chinY-y-.2),xx=lerp(x,s*(f.ex+.06),p)+Math.sin(p*3)*s*.03;
        waterDrop(ctx,xx,yy,.04+.015*Math.sin(p*Math.PI),Math.sin(p*Math.PI));
      }
    }
  };
  FX.eyeDrops=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H);
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){
      const x=s*f.ex,y=f.ey;
      wetEye(ctx,x,y,s,t,s);
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
        const per=.62+i*.07,p=fr(t/per+i/3+(s>0?.19:0));
        if(p<.3){const g=ease(p/.3);waterDrop(ctx,x+Math.sin(i*2)*.03,y+.17+g*.04,.03+.065*g,.85+.15*g);}
        else{const q=(p-.3)/.7,yy=y+.22+q*q*2.1,sz=.095-.02*q;waterDrop(ctx,x+Math.sin(i*2)*.03,yy,sz,1-clamp((q-.78)/.22,0,1));}
      }
    }
    // a splash ring where the falling drops end
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){ripple(ctx,s*f.ex,2.15,fr(t*.9+(s>0?.4:0)),.8);}
  };
  FX.tearFountain=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H);
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){
      const x=s*f.ex,y=f.ey+.08,pulse=.82+.18*Math.sin(t*9+s);
      wetEye(ctx,x,y-.04,s,t,s);
      // two thick arcs of water gushing outward then falling
      for(let a=0;a<2;a++){
        const spread=a?1.5:1.0,len=(a?1.0:.82)*pulse;
        const ex=x+s*len*spread*.55,ey=y+len*.3+.4,tx=x+s*len*spread*1.1,ty=y+1.35+a*.3;
        waterStream(ctx,x,y+.1,x+s*len*spread*.5,y-.2+a*.1,tx,ty,.12-.02*a,t*1.4,s*4+a*7);
      }
      // spray droplets flung outward
      for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
        const p=fr(t*.9+i/9+(s>0?.11:0)),sp=.8+hs(i)*.9,xx=x+s*(.1+p*sp),yy=y+.1-.55*Math.sin(p*Math.PI*.9)*(.6+hs(i+4)*.6)+p*p*1.6;
        waterDrop(ctx,xx,yy,.04+.04*hs(i+2),1-clamp((p-.72)/.28,0,1));
      }
    }
  };

  /* ---------- emotion ---------- */
  FX.blush=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H),th=.5+.5*Math.sin(t*3);
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){
      const x=s*f.cx,y=.3;
      const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,.02,x,y,.46);g.addColorStop(0,rgba(255,92,128,.78+.14*th));g.addColorStop(.6,rgba(255,110,150,.42));g.addColorStop(1,rgba(255,120,160,0));
      ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,.46,.3,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(224,48,96,.88)';ctx.lineWidth=.04;ctx.lineCap='round';
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-.18+i*.14,y+.1);ctx.lineTo(x-.08+i*.14,y-.08);ctx.stroke();}
    }
    // little floating hearts of embarrassment
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.5+i*.5),x=(i?.9:-.9)*f.fw+Math.sin(t*2+i)*.06,y=-.2-p*.9;
      ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=Math.sin(p*Math.PI);ctx.fillStyle='#FF4F8A';ctx.strokeStyle='#B01E57';ctx.lineWidth=.03;
      ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,.12);ctx.bezierCurveTo(-.26,-.04,-.14,-.22,0,-.08);ctx.bezierCurveTo(.14,-.22,.26,-.04,0,.12);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
    }
  };
  function textMark(ctx,ch,x,y,size,fill,line,rot){
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(rot||0);ctx.scale(.01,.01);
    ctx.font='900 '+Math.round(size*100)+'px "Arial Black",Impact,Arial,sans-serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineWidth=size*100*.2;ctx.strokeStyle=line;ctx.strokeText(ch,0,0);ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fillText(ch,0,0);ctx.restore();
  }
  FX.question=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.55+i/3),pop=p<.15?ease(p/.15)*1.25:1,x=(i-1)*.62+Math.sin(t*2+i)*.06,y=-1.15-p*.7,a=Math.sin(Math.min(1,p*1.2)*Math.PI);
      ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=clamp(a*1.5,0,1);
      textMark(ctx,'?',x,y,.62*pop*(.8+.2*(i===1)),'#FFE24A','#7A4A00',Math.sin(t*3+i)*.2);ctx.restore();
    }
  };
  FX.idea=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const bob=Math.sin(t*3)*.05,flick=.85+.15*Math.sin(t*17);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(0,-1.55+bob);
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,.05,0,0,1.05);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,240,140,'+(.85*flick)+')');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,220,60,0)');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,1.05,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const a=-Math.PI+(i+.5)*Math.PI/9,fl=.6+.4*Math.sin(t*12+i*1.7);ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,226,60,'+(.95*fl)+')';ctx.lineWidth=.06;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*.52,Math.sin(a)*.52);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*(.78+.1*fl),Math.sin(a)*(.78+.1*fl));ctx.stroke();}
    const bg=ctx.createRadialGradient(-.08,-.1,.02,0,0,.34);bg.addColorStop(0,'#FFFDE0');bg.addColorStop(.5,'#FFE04A');bg.addColorStop(1,'#F2A810');
    ctx.fillStyle=bg;ctx.strokeStyle='#8A5A00';ctx.lineWidth=.04;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,-.04,.32,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='#B9B9C6';ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(-.15,.26,.3,.17);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.8)';ctx.lineWidth=.035;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-.04,-.06,.17,Math.PI*1.1,Math.PI*1.6);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  };
  function note(ctx,x,y,s,rot,col,dbl){
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(rot);ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.strokeStyle='#2B2150';ctx.lineWidth=s*.12;ctx.lineCap='round';
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,s*.34,s*.24,-.4,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(s*.3,-s*.05);ctx.lineTo(s*.3,-s*1.2);ctx.stroke();
    if(dbl){ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(s*.8,-s*.2,s*.34,s*.24,-.4,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(s*1.1,-s*.25);ctx.lineTo(s*1.1,-s*1.3);ctx.moveTo(s*.3,-s*1.2);ctx.lineTo(s*1.1,-s*1.3);ctx.stroke();}
    else{ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(s*.3,-s*1.2);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(s*.85,-s*1.0,s*.7,-s*.55);ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  FX.music=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const cols=['#7CF2FF','#FF8AD8','#FFE14A','#9DFF8A'];
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.42+i/5),side=i%2?1:-1,x=side*(1.0+p*.7)+Math.sin(p*7+i)*.12,y=-.2-p*1.9;
      ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=Math.sin(p*Math.PI)*1.1;note(ctx,x,y,.26+.1*hs(i),Math.sin(t*2+i)*.3,cols[i%4],i%2===0);ctx.restore();
    }
  };
  FX.surprise=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const flick=Math.floor(t*14);
    ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(let i=0;i<20;i++){
      const a=i*TAU/20+hs(flick+i)*.08,r0=1.3+.2*hs(i),r1=r0+.5+.5*hs(i+flick*.3);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.95)';ctx.lineWidth=.045;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*r0,Math.sin(a)*r0);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r1,Math.sin(a)*r1);ctx.stroke();
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(60,60,90,.6)';ctx.lineWidth=.015;ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    const c=t%1.2,pop=c<.12?ease(c/.12)*1.4:c<.28?lerp(1.4,1,ease((c-.12)/.16)):1+.05*Math.sin(t*12);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(0,-1.55);ctx.scale(pop,pop);ctx.lineJoin='round';
    ctx.fillStyle='#FF3A3A';ctx.strokeStyle='#6E0C0C';ctx.lineWidth=.07;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.16,-.5);ctx.lineTo(.16,-.5);ctx.lineTo(.1,.14);ctx.lineTo(-.1,.14);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,.34,.1,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.75)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(-.05,-.28,.025,.14,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  };

  /* ---------- status / elemental ---------- */
  FX.frozen=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H);
    ctx.save();
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,.1,0,0,1.05);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(170,230,255,.12)');g.addColorStop(.7,'rgba(150,222,255,.4)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(220,248,255,.62)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,.98*f.fw+.08,1.0,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(235,252,255,.95)';ctx.lineWidth=.045;ctx.stroke();
    // ice shards around the head
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
      const a=-Math.PI*.95+i*Math.PI*1.9/8,r=.95+.14*hs(i),len=.38+.34*hs(i+5),x=Math.cos(a)*r*f.fw,y=Math.sin(a)*r;
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(a+Math.PI/2);
      const sg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,-len);sg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(120,200,255,.85)');sg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(240,252,255,.98)');
      ctx.fillStyle=sg;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(60,140,220,.95)';ctx.lineWidth=.03;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.08,0);ctx.lineTo(0,-len);ctx.lineTo(.08,0);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // sparkling frost + cold breath
    for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const p=fr(t*.5+i/8),x=(hs(i)-.5)*2.2,y=-1.2+p*2.2,a=Math.sin(p*Math.PI);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(a*.95)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,.035+.025*hs(i+3),0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const p=fr(t*.6+i/3),x=.1+p*.6,y=.52-p*.3,r=.1+p*.26;ctx.fillStyle='rgba(235,250,255,'+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI)*.65)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
  };
  FX.poison=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H);
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,.1,.1,0,.1,1.15);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(120,255,90,0)');g.addColorStop(.75,'rgba(90,230,70,.28)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(40,150,40,.0)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,.1,1.2,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    // sick green cheeks
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){const x=s*f.cx,y=.3,gg=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,.02,x,y,.42);gg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(120,230,60,.7)');gg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(120,230,60,0)');ctx.fillStyle=gg;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,.42,.28,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
    // toxic bubbles rising
    for(let i=0;i<10;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.42+i/10),x=(hs(i)-.5)*1.9+Math.sin(t*2+i*2)*.1,y=.7-p*2.5,r=(.07+.12*hs(i+4))*(.6+p*.7),a=Math.sin(p*Math.PI);
      const bg=ctx.createRadialGradient(x-r*.3,y-r*.3,r*.1,x,y,r);bg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(220,255,200,'+(a*.9)+')');bg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(70,200,50,'+(a*.6)+')');
      ctx.fillStyle=bg;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(30,120,30,'+(a*.9)+')';ctx.lineWidth=.025;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(a*.85)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x-r*.35,y-r*.35,r*.2,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
    // skull-and-swirl dizziness above the head
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(120,230,60,.9)';ctx.lineWidth=.045;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();
    for(let a=0;a<TAU*1.5;a+=.2){const r=.1+a*.06,x=Math.cos(a-t*4)*r,y=-1.3+Math.sin(a-t*4)*r*.45;a?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y);}ctx.stroke();
  };
  function auraFlame(ctx,x,by,w,h,t,seed){
    const sw=Math.sin(t*7+seed*4)*w*.45+Math.sin(t*13+seed*2)*w*.2,hh=h*(.86+.14*Math.sin(t*9+seed*5));
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-w,by);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-w*1.2,by-hh*.34,x-w*.3+sw*.5,by-hh*.64,x+sw,by-hh);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+w*.3+sw*.5,by-hh*.6,x+w*1.2,by-hh*.34,x+w,by);ctx.closePath();
  }
  FX.powerAura=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,-.1,.3,0,-.1,2.1);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,230,120,.0)');g.addColorStop(.45,'rgba(255,210,70,.5)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,170,30,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,-.1,2.1,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    const spec=[[-1.15,.38,1.7],[-.78,.42,2.3],[-.38,.46,2.75],[0,.5,3.15],[.38,.46,2.75],[.78,.42,2.3],[1.15,.38,1.7]];
    [['rgba(255,150,20,.8)',1],['rgba(255,214,70,.85)',.72],['rgba(255,252,210,.9)',.4]].forEach((pass,pi)=>{
      ctx.fillStyle=pass[0];
      spec.forEach((s,i)=>{auraFlame(ctx,s[0],.85,s[1]*pass[1],s[2]*(pi===2?.62:pi===1?.8:1),t*1.2+pi*.08,i+pi*3);ctx.fill();});
    });
    for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const p=fr(t*.8+hs(i)),x=(hs(i+3)-.5)*2.4,y=.8-p*3.2;ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,236,150,'+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI)*.95)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,.04+.04*hs(i+8),0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
    ctx.restore();
  };
  FX.darkAura=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H);
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,.3,0,0,2.0);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(70,0,110,0)');g.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(70,10,120,.5)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(20,0,40,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,2.0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    for(let i=0;i<11;i++){
      const p=fr(t*.34+i/11),a=hs(i)*TAU,x=Math.cos(a)*(.75+.35*hs(i+2))+Math.sin(t+i)*.1,y=.7-p*2.6,r=.2+.36*p,al=Math.sin(p*Math.PI);
      const sg=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,.02,x,y,r);sg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(34,8,56,'+(al*.88)+')');sg.addColorStop(.6,'rgba(70,20,110,'+(al*.55)+')');sg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(90,30,150,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=sg;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
    // glowing red eyes
    for(let s=-1;s<=1;s+=2){const x=s*f.ex,y=f.ey,gg=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,.26);gg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,60,60,.95)');gg.addColorStop(.4,'rgba(255,30,60,.55)');gg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,30,60,0)');ctx.fillStyle=gg;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,.26,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
  };

  /* ---------- fighting ---------- */
  function puff(ctx,x,y,r,fill,line,lw){
    ctx.beginPath();
    for(let q=0;q<5;q++){const a=q*TAU/5+.3,px=x+Math.cos(a)*r*.55,py=y+Math.sin(a)*r*.55;ctx.moveTo(px+r*.5,py);ctx.arc(px,py,r*.5,0,TAU);}
    ctx.moveTo(x+r*.7,y);ctx.arc(x,y,r*.7,0,TAU);
    ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fill();ctx.lineWidth=lw;ctx.strokeStyle=line;ctx.stroke();
  }
  function star(ctx,x,y,r,rot,n,inner){ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<n*2;i++){const a=rot-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/n,rr=i%2?r*inner:r;ctx.lineTo(x+Math.cos(a)*rr,y+Math.sin(a)*rr);}ctx.closePath();}
  FX.punchHit=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const f=face(H),cx=-f.cx-.12,cy=.16,c=t%1.05,pop=c<.1?ease(c/.1)*1.4:c<.26?lerp(1.4,1,ease((c-.1)/.16)):1,fade=c>.8?1-(c-.8)/.2:1;
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(cx,cy);ctx.globalAlpha*=clamp(fade,0,1);
    ctx.lineJoin='miter';
    star(ctx,0,0,.78*pop,.2,9,.52);ctx.fillStyle='#FFD21F';ctx.fill();ctx.lineWidth=.06;ctx.strokeStyle='#7A1A0C';ctx.stroke();
    star(ctx,0,0,.46*pop,.2,9,.55);ctx.fillStyle='#FFF7C8';ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=i*TAU/10+.1,r0=.95*pop,r1=r0+.3+.2*hs(i);ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.9)';ctx.lineWidth=.04;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*r0,Math.sin(a)*r0);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r1,Math.sin(a)*r1);ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
    // dust ring puffs flying off the cheek
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
      const p=clamp((c-.05)/.7,0,1),a=-Math.PI*.9+i*.5,d=.5+p*.9,al=1-p;
      if(p<=0||p>=1)continue;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=al;puff(ctx,cx+Math.cos(a)*d,cy+Math.sin(a)*d*.8,.16+.12*p,'rgba(246,246,252,.96)','rgba(70,66,88,.9)',.035);ctx.restore();
    }
  };
  FX.roundSmoke=function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const cnt=9,R=1.18;
    ctx.save();ctx.lineJoin='round';
    for(let layer=0;layer<2;layer++){
      for(let i=0;i<cnt;i++){
        const a=i*TAU/cnt+t*(layer?-.9:.7)+layer*.3,w=1+.12*Math.sin(t*6+i*2.1+layer),r=(.34-layer*.06)*w,rad=R*(layer?.62:1);
        puff(ctx,Math.cos(a)*rad,Math.sin(a)*rad*.92+.05,r,layer?'rgba(255,255,255,.97)':'rgba(222,226,238,.96)','rgba(58,56,76,.95)',.045);
      }
    }
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const p=fr(t*.8+i/5),a=hs(i+2)*TAU,d=.6+1.0*p;ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,214,40,'+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI))+')';ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(170,90,10,'+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI))+')';ctx.lineWidth=.03;star(ctx,Math.cos(a)*d,Math.sin(a)*d,.2*(1-p*.3),t*3+i,5,.46);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
  };

  /* ---------- registration ---------- */
  const NEW=[
    ['teary','Teary Eyes'],['cryStream','Crying Streams'],['eyeDrops','Eye Drops'],['tearFountain','Tear Fountain'],
    ['blush','Blush'],['question','Question'],['idea','Idea Bulb'],['music','Music Notes'],['surprise','Surprise'],
    ['frozen','Frozen'],['poison','Poison'],['powerAura','Power Aura'],['darkAura','Dark Aura'],
    ['punchHit','Punch Hit'],['roundSmoke','Round Smoke 2D']
  ];
  NEW.forEach(([id,label])=>{
    if(!lib.types.some(x=>x[0]===id))lib.types.push([id,label]);
    if(Array.isArray(lib.ids)&&!lib.ids.includes(id))lib.ids.push(id);
  });
  const BOUNDS={
    teary:{x0:-1.0,x1:1.0,y0:-.2,y1:1.35},cryStream:{x0:-1.0,x1:1.0,y0:-.2,y1:3.1},eyeDrops:{x0:-.9,x1:.9,y0:-.1,y1:2.4},
    tearFountain:{x0:-2.0,x1:2.0,y0:-.3,y1:1.9},blush:{x0:-1.3,x1:1.3,y0:-1.3,y1:.65},question:{x0:-1.2,x1:1.2,y0:-2.4,y1:-.7},
    idea:{x0:-1.1,x1:1.1,y0:-2.6,y1:-.5},music:{x0:-2.1,x1:2.1,y0:-2.3,y1:.3},surprise:{x0:-2.0,x1:2.0,y0:-2.5,y1:1.9},
    frozen:{x0:-1.5,x1:1.5,y0:-1.9,y1:1.5},poison:{x0:-1.4,x1:1.4,y0:-2.0,y1:1.3},powerAura:{x0:-2.1,x1:2.1,y0:-2.4,y1:1.0},
    darkAura:{x0:-2.0,x1:2.0,y0:-2.2,y1:1.2},punchHit:{x0:-1.8,x1:.2,y0:-.9,y1:1.2},roundSmoke:{x0:-1.8,x1:1.8,y0:-1.7,y1:1.8},
    tearOne:{x0:.12,x1:.82,y0:-.05,y1:2.55},tearTwo:{x0:-.86,x1:.86,y0:-.05,y1:2.55}
  };
  const GROUPS=[
    ['Face & feelings',['anger','sweat','dizzy','hearts','sleep','blush','question','idea','surprise','music']],
    ['Crying & water',['tears','tearOne','tearTwo','teary','cryStream','eyeDrops','tearFountain']],
    ['Fire, aura & power',['fire','chi','electric','powerAura','darkAura','sparkle']],
    ['Fighting & injuries',['punchHit','roundSmoke','blood','bloodHalo','bruise','nosebleed','bandage','bandagePatch','frozen','poison']]
  ];
  window.__evCharFxPack={ids:NEW.map(x=>x[0]),bounds:BOUNDS,groups:GROUPS};

  /* Grouped lists for the Effects panel (characters, character-effect clips and video layers). */
  window.__evFxGroups=function(isChar,isCharClip,items){
    const label=id=>{const r=items.find(x=>x[0]===id)||lib.types.find(x=>x[0]===id);return r?r[1]:null;};
    if(isChar){return [];}
    if(false){
      const placed=new Set(),out=[];
      GROUPS.forEach(([title,ids])=>{
        const list=ids.filter(id=>lib.has(id)).map(id=>{placed.add(id);return [id,label(id)||id];});
        if(list.length)out.push([title,list]);
      });
      const rest=lib.types.filter(x=>!placed.has(x[0])&&lib.has(x[0]));
      if(rest.length)out.push(['More',rest.map(x=>[x[0],x[1]])]);
      /* v421: "None" is the very first tile of the first section (Face & feelings); no separate title block. */
      if(out.length)out[0][1].unshift(['none','None']);else out.push([null,[['none','None']]]);
      return out;
    }
    const pack=window.__evSceneFxPack,out=[];
    const weather=items.filter(x=>x[0]==='none'||['rain','storm','clouds','snow','lightning','flash','fog','sunrise','blur'].includes(x[0]));
    out.push([null,weather]); /* v435: only 'None' is left, so no group title */
    if(pack)pack.groups.forEach(([title,g])=>{const list=pack.types.filter(x=>x[2]===g).map(x=>[x[0],x[1]]);if(list.length)out.push([title,list]);});
    return out;
  };

  /* panel styling for the group titles */
  const st=document.createElement('style');st.id='ethvelle-v411-effects-panel-groups-css';
  st.textContent='.evv-fx-group-title{margin:14px 2px 8px;font-size:12px!important;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.05em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#D6D6DC!important;}'+
    '.evv-panel .evv-fx-group-title:first-of-type{margin-top:6px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v334-character-effect-clips">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const lib=window.ethvelleCharFxLib;
  if(!lib)return;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const list=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const chars=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const selId=()=>{try{return String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');}catch(_e){return '';}};
  const selOverlay=()=>list().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===selId())||null;
  const isCharEffectClip=o=>!!(o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&o.ethvelleEffectKind==='character');
  const labelOf=type=>{const row=lib.types.find(x=>x[0]===type);return row?row[1]:'Effect';};
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));

  /* Which character is selected on the timeline (same rule the Effects panel uses). */
  function selectedCharIndex(){
    const sc=selId();
    if(!(sc==='main'||sc.startsWith('seg')||sc.startsWith('char')))return -1;
    let idx=typeof activeCharIdx==='number'?activeCharIdx:0;
    if(sc.startsWith('char')){const n=parseInt(sc.slice(4),10);if(Number.isInteger(n))idx=n;}
    return chars()[idx]?idx:-1;
  }
  function charRange(ch){
    const clips=[];
    try{
      const arr=(typeof activeCharacter==='function'&&ch===activeCharacter())?getAllStickmanClips(activeClip,splitStickmanSegments):getAllStickmanClips(ch.clip,ch.segments||[]);
      (arr||[]).forEach(x=>{if(x&&x.clip)clips.push(x.clip);});
    }catch(_e){}
    if(!clips.length){if(ch.clip)clips.push(ch.clip);(ch.segments||[]).forEach(s=>clips.push(s));}
    if(!clips.length)return {start:0,end:Number(typeof timelineDuration!=='undefined'?timelineDuration:5)||5};
    return {start:Math.min(...clips.map(c=>Number(c.trimStart)||0)),end:Math.max(...clips.map(c=>Number(c.trimEnd)||0))};
  }
  function charVisibleAt(ch,t){
    if(ch&&ch.__evGhost)return true;
    try{
      const arr=(typeof activeCharacter==='function'&&ch===activeCharacter())?getAllStickmanClips(activeClip,splitStickmanSegments):getAllStickmanClips(ch.clip,ch.segments||[]);
      if(!arr||!arr.length)return true;
      return arr.some(x=>x&&x.clip&&t>=Number(x.clip.trimStart)&&t<Number(x.clip.trimEnd));
    }catch(_e){return true;}
  }

  /* ---------- drawing a character-effect clip (live canvas, still preview and export) ---------- */
  window.ethvelleDrawCharacterEffectClip=function(ctx,ov,t){
    if(!ctx||!ov||!ov.visualEffects)return;
    const type=String(ov.visualEffects.type||'');if(!lib.has(type))return;
    if(t<Number(ov.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(ov.trimEnd||0))return;
    const ch=chars().find(c=>c&&c.id===ov.ethvelleEffectCharId)||window.__ethvelleCharGhost(ov.ethvelleEffectCharId);
    const frame=typeof window.__ethvelleCharFrame==='function'?window.__ethvelleCharFrame(ch):null;if(!frame)return;
    let tr=null;
    try{tr=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',t):null;}catch(_e){}
    tr=tr||ov.transform||{};
    const cfg=window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg(ov.visualEffects);
    cfg.ox+=(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)/Math.max(1,frame.r);
    cfg.oy+=(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)/Math.max(1,frame.r);
    cfg.size*=Math.max(.1,Number(tr.scale)||1);
    lib.draw(ctx,frame,type,Math.max(0,t-Number(ov.trimStart||0))*cfg.speed,cfg);
  };

  /* ---------- creating / re-typing the effect clip ---------- */
  function addCharacterEffectClip(idx,type){
    const ch=chars()[idx];if(!ch||!lib.has(type))return null;
    const range=charRange(ch);
    const now=Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const start=(now>=range.start&&now<range.end)?now:range.start;
    const id='ovfx'+Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6);
    const blank='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="512" height="512" viewBox="0 0 512 512"><rect width="512" height="512" fill="none"/></svg>';
    const clip={
      id,name:labelOf(type)+' Effect',color:'#5578F5',type:'image',src:'data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,'+encodeURIComponent(blank),
      sourceDuration:5,sourceOffset:0,sourceWidth:512,sourceHeight:512,
      trimStart:start,trimEnd:start+5,thumbs:[lib.thumb(type,0)],peaks:[],speed:1,
      transform:{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0},
      visualEffects:{type,intensity:135,density:100,speed:100,scale:100,x:0,y:0},
      ethvelleEffectClip:true,ethvelleEffectKind:'character',ethvelleEffectType:type,ethvelleEffectLabel:labelOf(type),
      ethvelleEffectCharId:ch.id,ethvelleEffectTargetId:''
    };
    overlays.push(clip);
    timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,clip.trimEnd);
    afterClipChange(clip,true);
    try{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(labelOf(type)+' effect added · 5 seconds',{duration:1500});}catch(_e){}
    return clip;
  }
  function retypeCharacterEffectClip(clip,type){
    clip.visualEffects=Object.assign({},clip.visualEffects||{},{type});
    clip.ethvelleEffectType=type;clip.ethvelleEffectLabel=labelOf(type);clip.name=labelOf(type)+' Effect';clip.thumbs=[lib.thumb(type,0)];
    afterClipChange(clip,false);
    return clip;
  }
  function afterClipChange(clip,select){
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof syncTimelineInnerWidth==='function')syncTimelineInnerWidth();if(typeof renderRuler==='function')renderRuler();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayListPanel==='function')renderOverlayListPanel();}catch(_e){}
    if(select){try{if(typeof window.selectClipForChrome==='function')window.selectClipForChrome(clip.id);}catch(_e){}}
    try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{if(typeof window.renderVisualPanel==='function'&&$('evvVisualPanel'))window.renderVisualPanel('visualEffects');}catch(_e){}});
  }

  /* Picking a character effect (from a character OR from an existing character-effect clip). */
  const prevSet=window.setEthvelleVisualEffect;
  window.setEthvelleVisualEffect=function(type){
    type=String(type||'');
    const sel=selOverlay();
    if(isCharEffectClip(sel)){
      if(lib.has(type))return retypeCharacterEffectClip(sel,type);
      return sel;
    }
    if(lib.has(type)){
      const idx=selectedCharIndex();
      if(idx>=0)return addCharacterEffectClip(idx,type);
    }
    return typeof prevSet==='function'?prevSet.apply(this,arguments):undefined;
  };

  /* ---------- styling for the real-animation frames in the timeline clip ---------- */
  const st=document.createElement('style');st.id='ethvelle-v334-char-effect-frames-css';
  st.textContent=[
    '#timelineInner .overlay-clip.ev-v246-effect-clip .ev-v248-effect-frame{overflow:hidden!important;}',
    '#timelineInner .overlay-clip.ev-v246-effect-clip .ev-v248-effect-frame img.ev-v334-frame-img{display:block!important;width:100%!important;height:100%!important;object-fit:contain!important;pointer-events:none!important;-webkit-user-drag:none;user-select:none;}',
    '.evv-choice-grid{max-height:none;}'
  ].join('\n');
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* Re-label timeline strips already on screen once the library is available. */
  try{if(window.__ethvelleV249EffectFrames&&window.__ethvelleV249EffectFrames.schedule)window.__ethvelleV249EffectFrames.schedule();}catch(_e){}
  window.__ethvelleV334={addCharacterEffectClip,retypeCharacterEffectClip,charVisibleAt};
})();

</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v335-character-effect-selection-css">
  /* v335: the selected character-effect clip gets its own compact on-canvas white frame.
     It is separate from the general image selection box, so it hugs the effect artwork only. */

<script id="ethvelle-v335-character-effect-polish-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV335CharacterEffects)return;
  window.__ethvelleV335CharacterEffects=true;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  const lib=window.ethvelleCharFxLib;
  if(!lib||!lib._fx||!Array.isArray(lib.types))return;

  /* ---------------- Add four distinct, reusable character effects ---------------- */
  const FX=lib._fx,TAU=Math.PI*2;
  const fr=x=>x-Math.floor(x);
  const ease=t=>{t=clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t);};
  const rgba=(r,g,b,a)=>'rgba('+r+','+g+','+b+','+clamp(a,0,1)+')';
  const addType=(id,label,draw)=>{
    if(!FX[id])FX[id]=draw;
    if(!lib.types.some(x=>x[0]===id))lib.types.push([id,label]);
    if(Array.isArray(lib.ids)&&!lib.ids.includes(id))lib.ids.push(id);
  };

  /* One clear, glossy tear on a single eye. The drop travels down one cheek and loops. */
  addType('tearOne','Single Tear',function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const x=H.face.ex+.03,phase=fr(t*.62),travel=ease(phase),y=.14+travel*1.86;
    ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(112,191,255,.42)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,.12,.25,.16,-.1,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#267BC5';ctx.lineWidth=.11;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,.25);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+.03,.62,x-.05,1.03,x-.015,y+.12);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(235,249,255,.95)';ctx.lineWidth=.035;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-.045,.32);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x-.08,.8,x-.07,y-.05);ctx.stroke();
    const size=.135+.035*Math.sin(phase*Math.PI);
    ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#4CB7FF';ctx.shadowBlur=.12;
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(x-size,y-size*1.7,x+size,y+size);
    g.addColorStop(0,'#F5FCFF');g.addColorStop(.36,'#8ED4FF');g.addColorStop(1,'#247DDB');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.strokeStyle='#155A9D';ctx.lineWidth=.035;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y-size*1.7);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-size*.2,y-size*.7,x-size,y-size*.1,x-size,y+size*.35);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-size,y+size,x+size,y+size,x+size,y+size*.35);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+size,y-size*.1,x+size*.2,y-size*.7,x,y-size*1.7);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='#FFFFFF';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x-size*.36,y+size*.1,size*.17,size*.32,-.25,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
    ctx.restore();
  });

  /* Two independent tear streams, one beneath each eye, with offset timing for natural motion. */
  addType('tearTwo','Double Tears',function(ctx,t,k,H){
    for(let side of [-1,1]){
      const x=side*(H.face.ex+.03),phase=fr(t*.58+(side>0?.22:0)),travel=ease(phase),y=.18+travel*1.72;
      ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(120,198,255,.58)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,.12,.27,.17,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle='#176DB7';ctx.lineWidth=.115;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,.24);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-side*.04,.68,x+side*.06,1.08,x,y+.1);ctx.stroke();
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(245,252,255,.98)';ctx.lineWidth=.035;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-side*.05,.32);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x-side*.09,.82,x-side*.055,y-.05);ctx.stroke();
      const sz=.125+.025*Math.sin(phase*Math.PI);
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(x-sz,y-sz*1.7,x+sz,y+sz);g.addColorStop(0,'#FFFFFF');g.addColorStop(.4,'#93D8FF');g.addColorStop(1,'#287ED8');
      ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.strokeStyle='#155A9D';ctx.lineWidth=.035;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y-sz*1.7);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-sz*.18,y-sz*.7,x-sz,y-sz*.1,x-sz,y+sz*.35);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-sz,y+sz,x+sz,y+sz,x+sz,y+sz*.35);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+sz,y-sz*.1,x+sz*.18,y-sz*.7,x,y-sz*1.7);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='#FFFFFF';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x-sz*.36,y+sz*.1,sz*.17,sz*.32,-.25,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  });

  /* Strong circular blood ring with glossy crimson highlights and individual droplets. */
  addType('bloodHalo','Circular Blood',function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const pulse=1+.045*Math.sin(t*4.2),r=1.28*pulse;
    ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
    ctx.strokeStyle='#5A0710';ctx.lineWidth=.2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,r,Math.PI*.08,Math.PI*1.94);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#C81425';ctx.lineWidth=.115;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,r-.015,Math.PI*.1,Math.PI*1.92);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#FF5660';ctx.lineWidth=.035;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-.025,-.025,r-.11,Math.PI*.22,Math.PI*1.76);ctx.stroke();
    for(let i=0;i<15;i++){
      const a=i*TAU/15+t*.12, wob=.08*Math.sin(t*2.6+i*1.7),rr=r+.15+wob;
      const x=Math.cos(a)*rr,y=Math.sin(a)*rr;
      const sz=.045+.045*((Math.sin(i*2.7)+1)/2);
      ctx.fillStyle=i%3===0?'#720914':'#D7192B';ctx.strokeStyle='#690912';ctx.lineWidth=.025;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,sz,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
      if(i%4===0){ctx.strokeStyle='#B51222';ctx.lineWidth=.075;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x+.05,y+.16,x+.07,y+.29);ctx.stroke();}
    }
    /* Two short drops fall from the lower rim on alternating beats. */
    for(let side of [-1,1]){
      const p=fr(t*.72+(side>0?.35:0)),yy=.94+ease(p)*.92,alpha=1-clamp((p-.8)/.2,0,1);
      ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.strokeStyle='#700B15';ctx.lineWidth=.11;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(side*.72,.88);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(side*.82,yy*.65,side*.88,yy);ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='#D7192B';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(side*.88,yy,.075,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  });

  /* Separate cheek plaster: a padded gauze patch with visible weave, stitching and a medical cross. */
  addType('bandagePatch','Bandage Patch',function(ctx,t,k,H){
    const pop=ease(t/.28),breath=1+.018*Math.sin(t*3.2);
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(H.face.cx-.09,.28);ctx.rotate(-.18);ctx.scale(pop*breath,pop*breath);
    const round=(x,y,w,h,r)=>{ctx.beginPath();if(ctx.roundRect)ctx.roundRect(x,y,w,h,r);else ctx.rect(x,y,w,h);};
    ctx.shadowColor='rgba(35,22,12,.32)';ctx.shadowBlur=.08;
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(-.52,-.28,.5,.28);g.addColorStop(0,'#FFFDF4');g.addColorStop(.52,'#F1E5CE');g.addColorStop(1,'#DCC9A8');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.strokeStyle='#9D8060';ctx.lineWidth=.045;round(-.52,-.27,1.04,.54,.12);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    ctx.save();round(-.4,-.18,.8,.36,.07);ctx.clip();
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(147,117,83,.62)';ctx.lineWidth=.018;
    for(let x=-.5;x<.55;x+=.09){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,-.28);ctx.lineTo(x+.24,.28);ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+.24,-.28);ctx.lineTo(x,.28);ctx.stroke();}
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#B49771';ctx.lineWidth=.025;ctx.setLineDash([.04,.055]);round(-.45,-.21,.9,.42,.07);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);
    /* Small central gauze pad and muted red medical cross. */
    ctx.fillStyle='#FFFCF2';ctx.strokeStyle='#D9C6A8';ctx.lineWidth=.025;round(-.16,-.13,.32,.26,.045);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='#C43A43';ctx.fillRect(-.035,-.085,.07,.17);ctx.fillRect(-.095,-.025,.19,.055);
    ctx.restore();
  });

  /* Give every effect the same crisp, slightly richer colour treatment. */
  const oldDraw=lib.draw;
  if(typeof oldDraw==='function'&&!oldDraw.__evv335RichPaint){
    const richDraw=function(ctx,frame,type,tt,cfg){
      if(!ctx)return oldDraw.apply(this,arguments);
      ctx.save();
      try{if('filter' in ctx)ctx.filter='saturate(1.5) contrast(1.28) brightness(1.12)';}catch(_e){}
      try{return oldDraw.apply(this,arguments);}finally{ctx.restore();}
    };
    richDraw.__evv335RichPaint=true;lib.draw=richDraw;
  }

  /* ---------------- Individual on-canvas selection frame ---------------- */
  const isEffect=o=>!!(o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&o.ethvelleEffectKind==='character');
  const selectedId=()=>{try{return String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');}catch(_e){return '';}};
  const overlaysList=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const getSelectedFx=()=>overlaysList().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===selectedId()&&isEffect(o))||null;
  function ensureBox(){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage||!stage.parentElement)return null;
    let layer=$('evvCharFxSelectionLayer');
    if(!layer){
      layer=document.createElement('div');layer.id='evvCharFxSelectionLayer';
      const frame=document.createElement('div');frame.id='evvCharFxSelectionFrame';layer.appendChild(frame);
      const wrap=stage.parentElement;wrap.appendChild(layer);
    }
    return layer;
  }
  const FX_BOUNDS={
    anger:{x0:-1.55,x1:1.55,y0:-1.85,y1:.72},fire:{x0:-1.85,x1:1.85,y0:-2.65,y1:.92},
    tears:{x0:-.82,x1:.82,y0:-.1,y1:2.85},tearOne:{x0:.12,x1:.82,y0:-.05,y1:2.55},
    tearTwo:{x0:-.86,x1:.86,y0:-.05,y1:2.55},bandage:{x0:-1.5,x1:1.48,y0:-.95,y1:.72},
    bandagePatch:{x0:-.16,x1:1.04,y0:-.1,y1:.72},blood:{x0:-1.8,x1:1.2,y0:-1.15,y1:2.15},
    bloodHalo:{x0:-1.95,x1:1.95,y0:-1.95,y1:1.95},nosebleed:{x0:-.75,x1:.75,y0:.1,y1:1.55},
    sweat:{x0:-1.5,x1:1.72,y0:-1.0,y1:1.75},chi:{x0:-2.15,x1:2.15,y0:-2.15,y1:2.15},
    bruise:{x0:-1.18,x1:.42,y0:-.72,y1:.82},dizzy:{x0:-1.45,x1:1.45,y0:-1.75,y1:-.48},
    hearts:{x0:-1.8,x1:1.8,y0:-3.0,y1:.8},sleep:{x0:.35,x1:2.05,y0:-2.8,y1:.65},
    electric:{x0:-1.9,x1:1.9,y0:-1.9,y1:1.9},sparkle:{x0:-1.95,x1:1.95,y0:-1.95,y1:1.85},...(window.__evCharFxPack?window.__evCharFxPack.bounds:{})
  };
  function hideBox(){const l=$('evvCharFxSelectionLayer');if(l)l.style.display='none';}
  function paintSelectionBox(){
    const layer=ensureBox();if(!layer)return;
    const frameEl=$('evvCharFxSelectionFrame');
    const clip=getSelectedFx();
    if(!clip){hideBox();return;}
    let playing=false;try{playing=!!isPlaying;}catch(_e){}
    if(playing){hideBox();return;}
    const t=Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0))||0;
    if(t<Number(clip.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(clip.trimEnd||0)){hideBox();return;}
    const ch=(Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[]).find(c=>c&&String(c.id)===String(clip.ethvelleEffectCharId))||window.__ethvelleCharGhost(clip.ethvelleEffectCharId);
    if(!ch||!window.__ethvelleCharFrame){hideBox();return;}
    const f=window.__ethvelleCharFrame(ch);if(!f||!(Number(f.r)>0)){hideBox();return;}
    const type=String(clip.visualEffects&&clip.visualEffects.type||clip.ethvelleEffectType||'');
    if(!lib.has(type)){hideBox();return;}
    const cfg=window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg?window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg(clip.visualEffects||{}):{size:1,ox:0,oy:0};
    const tr=clip.transform||{};
    const scale=clamp(cfg.size==null?1:cfg.size,.25,2.5)*clamp(tr.scale==null?1:tr.scale,.1,6);
    const R=f.r*scale*(FX[type]&&FX[type].atHand?.85:1);
    const anchor=(FX[type]&&FX[type].atHand&&f.hand)?f.hand:{x:f.x,y:f.y};
    const cx0=anchor.x+(Number(cfg.ox)||0)*f.r+(Number(tr.offsetX)||0);
    const cy0=anchor.y+(Number(cfg.oy)||0)*f.r+(Number(tr.offsetY)||0);
    let b=FX_BOUNDS[type]||{x0:-1.8,x1:1.8,y0:-1.9,y1:1.9};
    let x0=b.x0,x1=b.x1,y0=b.y0,y1=b.y1;
    if(f.flip){const old=x0;x0=-x1;x1=-old;}
    const localX=(x0+x1)*.5*R,localY=(y0+y1)*.5*R;
    const a=Number(f.rot)||0,cs=Math.cos(a),sn=Math.sin(a);
    const cx=cx0+localX*cs-localY*sn,cy=cy0+localX*sn+localY*cs;
    const w=Math.max(8,(x1-x0)*R),h=Math.max(8,(y1-y0)*R);
    const stage=$('canvasStage'),wrap=stage&&stage.parentElement;if(!stage||!wrap){hideBox();return;}
    layer.style.left=(stage.offsetLeft||0)+'px';layer.style.top=(stage.offsetTop||0)+'px';
    layer.style.width=Math.max(1,Number(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?stageW:stage.clientWidth)||stage.clientWidth)+'px';
    layer.style.height=Math.max(1,Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:stage.clientHeight)||stage.clientHeight)+'px';
    frameEl.style.left=(cx-w/2)+'px';frameEl.style.top=(cy-h/2)+'px';
    frameEl.style.width=w+'px';frameEl.style.height=h+'px';frameEl.style.transform='rotate('+a+'rad)';
    layer.style.display='block';
  }
  let boxRaf=0;
  function scheduleBox(){if(boxRaf)return;boxRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{boxRaf=0;try{paintSelectionBox();}catch(err){console.warn('Character-effect selection frame:',err);hideBox();}});}

  /* Character effect image placeholders cover the whole stage; do not show the generic
     full-stage image transform box. The dedicated frame above hugs the actual effect instead. */
  const oldSelTarget=window.selBoxTarget;
  if(typeof oldSelTarget==='function'&&!oldSelTarget.__evv335NoFullStageFxBox){
    const wrapped=function(){if(getSelectedFx())return null;return oldSelTarget.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped.__evv335NoFullStageFxBox=true;window.selBoxTarget=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('selBoxTarget=window.selBoxTarget');}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* ---------------- Add the new buttons to the existing Character Effects panel ---------------- */
  const EXTRA=[];
  function injectExtraChoices(){
    const host=$('evvVisualPanel');if(!host)return;
    const selected=getSelectedFx();
    const id=selectedId();
    const isCharContext=!!selected||id==='main'||id.startsWith('seg')||id.startsWith('char');
    if(!isCharContext)return;
    const grid=host.querySelector('.evv-choice-grid');if(!grid)return;
    grid.querySelectorAll('[data-evv-v335-extra-fx]').forEach(n=>n.remove());
    const active=selected&&selected.visualEffects?String(selected.visualEffects.type||''):'';
    EXTRA.forEach(([type,label])=>{
      const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='evv-choice'+(active===type?' active':'');
      btn.textContent=label;btn.dataset.evvV335ExtraFx=type;btn.title=label+' character effect';
      btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(typeof window.setEthvelleVisualEffect==='function')window.setEthvelleVisualEffect(type);scheduleBox();});
      grid.appendChild(btn);
    });
  }
  const oldPanel=window.renderVisualPanel;
  if(typeof oldPanel==='function'&&!oldPanel.__evv335ExtraFx){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=oldPanel.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='visualEffects')requestAnimationFrame(injectExtraChoices);return result;};
    wrapped.__evv335ExtraFx=true;window.renderVisualPanel=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('renderVisualPanel=window.renderVisualPanel');}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* Repaint the dedicated border after selections, slider changes, canvas renders and animation poses. */
  function wrap(name,marker){

    wrapped[marker]=true;window[name]=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)(name+'=window.'+name);}catch(_e){}
  }
  ['updateSelBox','renderFrame','drawStick','renderOverlayLayers','selectClipForChrome','onClipSelected',
   'setEthvelleEffectSetting','setEthvelleVisualEffect','deselectAllClips'].forEach((n,i)=>wrap(n,'__evv335_'+i));
  window.addEventListener('resize',scheduleBox,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',scheduleBox,{passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',scheduleBox,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',scheduleBox,true);

  /* If the panel is already open while this patch loads, render its extra choices once. */
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{injectExtraChoices();scheduleBox();});
  window.__ethvelleV335={scheduleBox,paintSelectionBox,extraEffects:EXTRA.map(x=>x[0])};

<script id="ethvelle-v336-character-effect-native-selection-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV336NativeFxSelection)return;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const lib=window.ethvelleCharFxLib;
  if(!lib||!lib.has||!lib._fx)return;
  const all=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const chars=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const selectedId=()=>{try{return String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');}catch(_e){return '';}};
  const isFx=o=>!!(o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&o.ethvelleEffectKind==='character');
  const selectedFx=()=>all().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===selectedId()&&isFx(o))||null;
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  const normDeg=d=>((Number(d)||0)%360+540)%360-180;
  const bounds={
    anger:{x0:-1.55,x1:1.55,y0:-1.85,y1:.72},fire:{x0:-1.85,x1:1.85,y0:-2.65,y1:.92},
    tears:{x0:-.82,x1:.82,y0:-.1,y1:2.85},tearOne:{x0:.12,x1:.82,y0:-.05,y1:2.55},
    tearTwo:{x0:-.86,x1:.86,y0:-.05,y1:2.55},bandage:{x0:-1.5,x1:1.48,y0:-.95,y1:.72},
    bandagePatch:{x0:-.16,x1:1.04,y0:-.1,y1:.72},blood:{x0:-1.8,x1:1.2,y0:-1.15,y1:2.15},
    bloodHalo:{x0:-1.95,x1:1.95,y0:-1.95,y1:1.95},nosebleed:{x0:-.75,x1:.75,y0:.1,y1:1.55},
    sweat:{x0:-1.5,x1:1.72,y0:-1.0,y1:1.75},chi:{x0:-2.15,x1:2.15,y0:-2.15,y1:2.15},
    bruise:{x0:-1.18,x1:.42,y0:-.72,y1:.82},dizzy:{x0:-1.45,x1:1.45,y0:-1.75,y1:-.48},
    hearts:{x0:-1.8,x1:1.8,y0:-3.0,y1:.8},sleep:{x0:.35,x1:2.05,y0:-2.8,y1:.65},
    electric:{x0:-1.9,x1:1.9,y0:-1.9,y1:1.9},sparkle:{x0:-1.95,x1:1.95,y0:-1.95,y1:1.85},...(window.__evCharFxPack?window.__evCharFxPack.bounds:{})
  };

  function buildFxTarget(clip){
    if(!clip||!window.__ethvelleCharFrame||!window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg)return null;
    const t=Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0))||0;
    if(t<Number(clip.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(clip.trimEnd||0)||(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&!!isPlaying))return null;
    const ch=chars().find(c=>c&&String(c.id)===String(clip.ethvelleEffectCharId))||window.__ethvelleCharGhost(clip.ethvelleEffectCharId);
    const frame=window.__ethvelleCharFrame(ch);if(!frame||!(Number(frame.r)>0))return null;
    const type=String(clip.visualEffects&&clip.visualEffects.type||clip.ethvelleEffectType||'');
    if(!lib.has(type))return null;
    const cfg=window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg(clip.visualEffects||{});
    const meta=lib._fx[type]||{};
    const atHand=!!(meta.atHand&&frame.hand);
    const b=bounds[type]||{x0:-1.8,x1:1.8,y0:-1.9,y1:1.9};
    let x0=b.x0,x1=b.x1;
    if(!atHand&&frame.flip){const old=x0;x0=-x1;x1=-old;}
    const R=Math.max(1,Number(frame.r)*clamp(cfg.size==null?1:cfg.size,.25,2.5)*(atHand?.85:1));
    const anchor=atHand?frame.hand:{x:frame.x,y:frame.y};
    /* cfg.x/y and the transform offset move the effect's pivot, just like the drawing code. */
    const pivot={x:anchor.x+(Number(cfg.ox)||0)*frame.r,y:anchor.y+(Number(cfg.oy)||0)*frame.r};
    const baseRot=atHand?0:(Number(frame.rot)||0)*180/Math.PI;
    const cx=(x0+x1)*.5*R,cy=(b.y0+b.y1)*.5*R;
    const transform=clip.transform||(clip.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0});
    if(!Number.isFinite(Number(transform.scale)))transform.scale=1;
    if(!Number.isFinite(Number(transform.offsetX)))transform.offsetX=0;
    if(!Number.isFinite(Number(transform.offsetY)))transform.offsetY=0;
    if(!Number.isFinite(Number(transform.rotation)))transform.rotation=0;
    /* A mapped transform lets the native frame include the character's own head angle
       without writing that angle back into the effect clip's separate rotation value. */
    /* v339: when the effect follows another clip the frame shows where it is drawn (authored + tracked
       delta); edits write back only the authored part so the delta is never baked in twice. */
    const trk=(typeof window.ethvelleTrackingDelta==='function'?window.ethvelleTrackingDelta(clip,t):null)||{ds:1,dx:0,dy:0,dr:0};
    const mapped={};
    Object.defineProperties(mapped,{
      scale:{enumerable:true,get:()=>(Number(transform.scale)||1)*trk.ds,set:v=>{transform.scale=clamp((Number(v)||1)/Math.max(.0001,trk.ds),.1,6);}},
      offsetX:{enumerable:true,get:()=>(Number(transform.offsetX)||0)+trk.dx,set:v=>{transform.offsetX=(Number(v)||0)-trk.dx;}},
      offsetY:{enumerable:true,get:()=>(Number(transform.offsetY)||0)+trk.dy,set:v=>{transform.offsetY=(Number(v)||0)-trk.dy;}},
      rotation:{enumerable:true,get:()=>baseRot+(Number(transform.rotation)||0)+trk.dr,set:v=>{transform.rotation=normDeg((Number(v)||0)-baseRot-trk.dr);}}
    });
    return {
      kind:'overlay',obj:mapped,
      cB:{x:pivot.x+cx,y:pivot.y+cy},P0:pivot,
      w0:Math.max(12,(x1-x0)*R),h0:Math.max(12,(b.y1-b.y0)*R),
      canDelete:true,__evvCharacterEffectClipId:String(clip.id),
      __evvCharacterEffectSource:clip
    };
  }

  /* Install the same target contract the native selection frame already understands. */
  const oldTarget=window.selBoxTarget;
  if(typeof oldTarget==='function'&&!oldTarget.__evv336CharacterEffects){
    const wrapped=function(){const clip=selectedFx();if(clip)return buildFxTarget(clip);return oldTarget.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped.__evv336CharacterEffects=true;window.selBoxTarget=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('selBoxTarget=window.selBoxTarget');}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* The effects are painted on the dedicated FX canvas, not on the transparent placeholder
     image used to give the clip a timeline lane. So a native selection-frame drag must repaint
     that FX canvas instead of moving the placeholder DOM image. */
  function paintFxNow(){
    try{
      const api=window.__ethvelleV244Debug;
      if(api&&typeof api.paintEffectPreview==='function')api.paintEffectPreview();
      else if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();
    }catch(err){console.warn('Character effect transform preview:',err);}
  }
  const oldApply=window.selBoxApply;
  if(typeof oldApply==='function'&&!oldApply.__evv336CharacterEffects){
    const wrapped=function(tg){
      if(tg&&tg.__evvCharacterEffectClipId){
        paintFxNow();
        try{if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof window.scheduleSelBoxSliderSync==='function')window.scheduleSelBoxSliderSync();}catch(_e){}
        return;
      }
      return oldApply.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv336CharacterEffects=true;window.selBoxApply=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('selBoxApply=window.selBoxApply');}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* Add rotation to the same drawing call used by the live preview, thumbnails and export.
     The rotation pivot is the exact translated origin used by the effect renderer. */
  const baseDraw=lib.draw;
  if(typeof baseDraw==='function'&&!baseDraw.__evv336ClipRotation){
    const rotatedDraw=function(ctx,frame,type,tt,cfg){
      const deg=Number(cfg&&cfg.rotation)||0;
      if(!ctx||Math.abs(deg)<.0001)return baseDraw.apply(this,arguments);
      const meta=lib._fx[type]||{},hand=!!(meta.atHand&&frame&&frame.hand);
      const origin=hand?frame.hand:{x:frame.x,y:frame.y};
      const px=origin.x+(Number(cfg.ox)||0)*frame.r,py=origin.y+(Number(cfg.oy)||0)*frame.r;
      const clean=Object.assign({},cfg);delete clean.rotation;
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(px,py);ctx.rotate(deg*Math.PI/180);ctx.translate(-px,-py);
      try{return baseDraw.call(this,ctx,frame,type,tt,clean);}finally{ctx.restore();}
    };
    rotatedDraw.__evv336ClipRotation=true;lib.draw=rotatedDraw;
  }

  const oldDrawClip=window.ethvelleDrawCharacterEffectClip;
  if(typeof oldDrawClip==='function'&&!oldDrawClip.__evv336Transform){
    const drawClip=function(ctx,clip,t){
      if(!ctx||!isFx(clip)||!clip.visualEffects)return oldDrawClip.apply(this,arguments);
      const type=String(clip.visualEffects.type||'');if(!lib.has(type))return;
      const time=Number(t)||0;
      if(time<Number(clip.trimStart||0)||time>=Number(clip.trimEnd||0))return;
      const ch=chars().find(c=>c&&String(c.id)===String(clip.ethvelleEffectCharId))||window.__ethvelleCharGhost(clip.ethvelleEffectCharId);
      const frame=window.__ethvelleCharFrame?window.__ethvelleCharFrame(ch):null;if(!frame)return;
      let tr=null,keyedFx=null;
      try{keyedFx=window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',time):null;}catch(_e){}
      tr=keyedFx;
      /* v339: a character effect can follow any clip  -  draw it at its tracked transform. */
      try{if(clip.clipTracking&&clip.clipTracking.enabled&&clip.clipTracking.targetId&&typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function')tr=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(clip,time,keyedFx)||tr;}catch(_e){}
      tr=tr||clip.transform||{};
      const cfg=window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg(clip.visualEffects);
      cfg.ox+=(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)/Math.max(1,frame.r);
      cfg.oy+=(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)/Math.max(1,frame.r);
      cfg.size*=Math.max(.1,Number(tr.scale)||1);
      cfg.rotation=Number(tr.rotation)||0;
      lib.draw(ctx,frame,type,Math.max(0,time-Number(clip.trimStart||0))*cfg.speed,cfg);
    };
    drawClip.__evv336Transform=true;window.ethvelleDrawCharacterEffectClip=drawClip;
  }

  /* Keep the effect itself topmost and prevent transparent placeholder taps from stealing
     a drag that starts inside the visible effect's native transform frame. */
  const oldPick=window.pickCanvasItemAt;
  if(typeof oldPick==='function'&&!oldPick.__evv336CharacterEffects){
    const wrapped=function(clientX,clientY){
      const clip=selectedFx();
      if(clip){
        const tg=buildFxTarget(clip);
        if(tg&&typeof window.selBoxGeom==='function'){
          const g=window.selBoxGeom(tg),canvas=$('stickCanvas'),r=canvas&&canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
          if(r&&r.width&&r.height){
            const x=(clientX-r.left)*((typeof stageW!=='undefined'?Number(stageW):1)/r.width),y=(clientY-r.top)*((typeof stageH!=='undefined'?Number(stageH):1)/r.height);
            if(typeof window.pointInSelGeom==='function'&&window.pointInSelGeom(g,x,y))return null;
            const dx=x-g.cx,dy=y-g.cy,a=-g.rot*Math.PI/180,cs=Math.cos(a),sn=Math.sin(a);
            const lx=dx*cs-dy*sn,ly=dx*sn+dy*cs,band=12;
            if(Math.abs(lx)<=g.w/2+band&&Math.abs(ly)<=g.h/2+band)return null;
          }
        }
      }
      return oldPick.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv336CharacterEffects=true;window.pickCanvasItemAt=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('pickCanvasItemAt=window.pickCanvasItemAt');}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* Refresh the native box whenever the playhead, selected clip or character pose changes. */
  function refresh(){
    try{if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.__ethvelleV335==='object'&&window.__ethvelleV335.scheduleBox)window.__ethvelleV335.scheduleBox();}catch(_e){}
  }
  ['selectClipForChrome','onClipSelected','renderFrame','drawStick','renderOverlayLayers','setEthvelleEffectSetting','setEthvelleVisualEffect'].forEach((name,i)=>{
    const base=window[name],marker='__evv336FxRefresh'+i;

    wrapped[marker]=true;window[name]=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)(name+'=window.'+name);}catch(_e){}
  });
  window.addEventListener('resize',refresh,{passive:true});

  document.addEventListener('pointerup',refresh,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',refresh,true);
  requestAnimationFrame(refresh);
  window.__ethvelleV336NativeFxSelection={buildFxTarget,refresh,paintFxNow};
