<script id="ethvelle-v340-capcut-transform-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__evcxTransform)return;
  window.__evcxTransform=true;

  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const SP=7.4;            // CSS px between two ticks (CapCut: one tick = one unit)
  const MAJOR=10;          // brighter tick every 10 units
  const ROW_H=44;          // ruler height
  const BLUE='#2F5CE8';    // deep blue centre marker (the site's primary blue)
  const IND_TOP=12,IND_H=21,TICK_H=11,BASE_Y=IND_TOP+IND_H; // ticks sit on the same baseline as the marker

  const RULERS=[
    {key:'posX',  input:'posXSlider', label:'X axis'},
    {key:'posY',  input:'posYSlider', label:'Y axis'},
    {key:'size',  input:'sizeSlider', label:'Zoom'},
    {key:'rot',   input:'rotSlider',  label:'Rotate'}
  ];

  const CHECK_SVG='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4.5 12.6l4.8 4.8L19.5 6.8"/></svg>';
  const RESET_SVG='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4.2 12a7.8 7.8 0 1 0 2.5-5.7"/><path d="M4.4 4v4.4h4.4"/></svg>';

  function body(){
    const hid=(id,min,max,val)=>`<input type="range" class="transform-slider evcx-hidden" id="${id}" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="1" value="${val}" tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true">`;
    const ruler=k=>`<canvas class="evcx-ruler" data-evcx-ruler="${k}" tabindex="0" role="slider"></canvas>`;
    return `<div class="evcx-root" data-evcx-root="1">
      <div class="evcx-tabs" role="tablist">
        <button type="button" class="evcx-tab active" role="tab" data-evcx-tab="position">Position</button>
        <button type="button" class="evcx-tab" role="tab" data-evcx-tab="zoom">Zoom</button>
        <button type="button" class="evcx-tab" role="tab" data-evcx-tab="rotate">Rotate</button>
      </div>
      <div class="evcx-stage">
        <div class="evcx-pane active" data-evcx-pane="position">
          <span class="evcx-lab" style="grid-column:1;grid-row:1">X axis</span>
          <span class="evcx-lab" style="grid-column:1;grid-row:2">Y axis</span>
          <div class="evcx-box">${ruler('posX')}${ruler('posY')}</div>
          <span class="evcx-val" id="posXVal" style="grid-column:3;grid-row:1">0</span>
          <span class="evcx-val" id="posYVal" style="grid-column:3;grid-row:2">0</span>
        </div>
        <div class="evcx-pane" data-evcx-pane="zoom">
          <div class="evcx-box">${ruler('size')}</div>
          <span class="evcx-val" id="sizeVal">100%</span>
        </div>
        <div class="evcx-pane" data-evcx-pane="rotate">
          <div class="evcx-box">${ruler('rot')}</div>
          <span class="evcx-val" id="rotVal">0°</span>
        </div>
        ${hid('sizeSlider',10,400,100)}${hid('posXSlider',-200,200,0)}${hid('posYSlider',-200,200,0)}${hid('rotSlider',-180,180,0)}
      </div>
      <div class="evcx-foot">
        <button type="button" class="evcx-reset" data-evcx-act="reset">${RESET_SVG}<span>Reset</span></button>
        <div class="evcx-title" id="evcxTitle">Transform</div>
        <button type="button" class="evcx-ok" data-evcx-act="ok" aria-label="Done">${CHECK_SVG}</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  /* ---------- tick ruler (canvas) ---------- */
  const instances=new Map();

  function makeRuler(canvas){
    const def=RULERS.find(r=>r.key===canvas.dataset.evcxRuler);
    if(!def)return null;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    let w=0,dpr=1,pos=0,drag=null,raf=0,pending=null,anim=0,lastEmit=null;

    const inputEl=()=>$(def.input);
    const range=()=>{
      const i=inputEl();
      const min=i&&Number.isFinite(Number(i.min))?Number(i.min):-100;
      const max=i&&Number.isFinite(Number(i.max))?Number(i.max):100;
      return [min,max];
    };
    const clamp=v=>{const [a,b]=range();return Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));};

    function rr(x,y,ww,hh,r){
      ctx.beginPath();
      if(ctx.roundRect){ctx.roundRect(x,y,ww,hh,r);}
      else{ctx.moveTo(x+r,y);ctx.arcTo(x+ww,y,x+ww,y+hh,r);ctx.arcTo(x+ww,y+hh,x,y+hh,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y+hh,x,y,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+ww,y,r);ctx.closePath();}
      ctx.fill();
    }

    function resize(){
      const cw=canvas.clientWidth|0;
      if(!cw)return false;
      dpr=Math.min(3,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
      if(cw!==w||canvas.width!==Math.round(cw*dpr)){
        w=cw;canvas.width=Math.round(w*dpr);canvas.height=Math.round(ROW_H*dpr);
      }
      draw();return true;
    }

    function draw(){
      if(!w)return;
      ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
      ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,ROW_H);
      const [min,max]=range();
      const cx=w/2,half=cx/SP;
      const lo=Math.max(Math.ceil(min),Math.floor(pos-half)-1);
      const hi=Math.min(Math.floor(max),Math.ceil(pos+half)+1);
      for(let v=lo;v<=hi;v++){
        const x=cx+(v-pos)*SP;
        if(x<-2||x>w+2)continue;
        if(Math.abs(x-cx)<1.6)continue; // the blue marker takes this tick's place
        const edge=Math.min(x,w-x);
        const fade=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(edge-SP*.4)/(SP*2.6)));
        if(fade<=0)continue;
        const major=(v%MAJOR===0);
        ctx.fillStyle=major?`rgba(255,255,255,${(.93*fade).toFixed(3)})`:`rgba(255,255,255,${(.40*fade).toFixed(3)})`;
        rr(x-.8,BASE_Y-TICK_H,1.6,TICK_H,.8);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle=BLUE;
      rr(cx-1.3,IND_TOP,2.6,IND_H,1.3);
    }

    // Set the ruler position and push whole-number changes into the real slider.
    function apply(v,emit){
      pos=clamp(v);
      const iv=Math.round(pos);
      const input=inputEl();
      if(emit&&input&&Number(input.value)!==iv){
        input.value=iv;
        try{window.updateTransform();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle transform:',err);}
        try{input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));}catch(_e){}
        lastEmit=iv;
      }
      canvas.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(iv));
      draw();
    }
    function finishChange(){
      const input=inputEl();
      if(input&&lastEmit!==null){try{input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}catch(_e){}}
      lastEmit=null;
    }

    function stopAnim(){if(anim){cancelAnimationFrame(anim);anim=0;}}
    function settle(){
      stopAnim();
      const from=pos,to=Math.round(pos);
      if(Math.abs(from-to)<.001){apply(to,true);finishChange();return;}
      const t0=performance.now(),dur=130;
      const step=now=>{
        const k=Math.min(1,(now-t0)/dur),e=1-Math.pow(1-k,3);
        apply(from+(to-from)*e,true);
        if(k<1)anim=requestAnimationFrame(step);else{anim=0;apply(to,true);finishChange();}
      };
      anim=requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }
    function fling(vel){ // vel in units per ms
      stopAnim();
      let last=performance.now();
      const [min,max]=range();
      const step=now=>{
        const dt=Math.min(34,now-last);last=now;
        pos+=vel*dt;
        vel*=Math.exp(-dt/240);
        if(pos<=min||pos>=max){apply(pos<=min?min:max,true);anim=0;finishChange();return;}
        apply(pos,true);
        if(Math.abs(vel)<.0035){anim=0;settle();return;}
        anim=requestAnimationFrame(step);
      };
      anim=requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }

    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(drag||(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0))return;
      stopAnim();
      drag={id:e.pointerId,x0:e.clientX,p0:pos,s:[{t:e.timeStamp,x:e.clientX}]};
      lastEmit=null;
      try{canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:false});
    canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      drag.s.push({t:e.timeStamp,x:e.clientX});
      if(drag.s.length>8)drag.s.shift();
      // Content follows the finger: drag left -> ticks slide left -> value goes up.
      pending=drag.p0-(e.clientX-drag.x0)/SP;
      if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{raf=0;if(pending!==null){apply(pending,true);pending=null;}});
      if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:false});
    const end=e=>{
      if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      if(raf){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;}
      if(pending!==null){apply(pending,true);pending=null;}
      const s=drag.s,last=s[s.length-1];
      let vel=0;
      for(let i=s.length-2;i>=0;i--){ if(last.t-s[i].t>=70||i===0){const dt=Math.max(1,last.t-s[i].t);vel=-(last.x-s[i].x)/dt/SP;break;} }
      if(e.type==='pointercancel'||e.timeStamp-last.t>90)vel=0; // paused before lifting: no fling
      drag=null;
      try{canvas.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      if(Math.abs(vel)>.012)fling(Math.max(-2.2,Math.min(2.2,vel)));else settle();
      e.stopPropagation();
    };
    canvas.addEventListener('pointerup',end,{passive:false});
    canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',end,{passive:false});
    canvas.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
      const step=e.shiftKey?MAJOR:1;
      if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'||e.key==='ArrowDown'){apply(Math.round(pos)-step,true);finishChange();e.preventDefault();}
      else if(e.key==='ArrowRight'||e.key==='ArrowUp'){apply(Math.round(pos)+step,true);finishChange();e.preventDefault();}
    });

    const inst={
      key:def.key,canvas,resize,draw,
      busy:()=>!!drag||!!anim,
      // Follow the real slider (opening the panel, playhead moved, reset, keyframes...). No emit.
      refresh(){
        if(drag||anim)return;
        const i=inputEl();if(!i)return;
        const [min,max]=range();
        pos=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(i.value)||0));
        canvas.setAttribute('aria-valuemin',String(min));canvas.setAttribute('aria-valuemax',String(max));
        canvas.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(Math.round(pos)));
        canvas.setAttribute('aria-label',def.label);
        draw();
      }
    };
    if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){new ResizeObserver(()=>resize()).observe(canvas);}
    return inst;
  }

  /* ---------- panel wiring ---------- */
  function refreshAll(){instances.forEach(r=>{try{r.refresh();}catch(_e){}});}

  function setTab(root,name){
    root.querySelectorAll('.evcx-tab').forEach(b=>{
      const on=b.dataset.evcxTab===name;b.classList.toggle('active',on);b.setAttribute('aria-selected',on?'true':'false');
    });
    root.querySelectorAll('.evcx-pane').forEach(p=>p.classList.toggle('active',p.dataset.evcxPane===name));
    // Panes that were hidden have no width yet: measure once they are shown.
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{instances.forEach(r=>{r.resize();r.refresh();});});
  }

  function initRoot(root){
    if(!root||root.dataset.evcxReady==='1')return;
    root.dataset.evcxReady='1';
    instances.clear();
    root.querySelectorAll('canvas[data-evcx-ruler]').forEach(c=>{const r=makeRuler(c);if(r)instances.set(r.key,r);});
    root.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const tab=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-evcx-tab]');
      if(tab){setTab(root,tab.dataset.evcxTab);return;}
      const act=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-evcx-act]');
      if(!act)return;
      if(act.dataset.evcxAct==='reset'){
        try{window.resetTransform();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle reset:',err);}
        refreshAll();
      }else if(act.dataset.evcxAct==='ok'){
        try{window.closePanel();}catch(_e){}
      }
    });
    const titleEl=$('panelTitle'),t=$('evcxTitle');
    if(t&&titleEl&&titleEl.textContent.trim())t.textContent=titleEl.textContent.trim();
    // Measure after layout, sync with the real slider values, then once more when the sheet has settled.
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      try{if(typeof window.syncTransformSliders==='function')window.syncTransformSliders();}catch(_e){}
      instances.forEach(r=>{r.resize();r.refresh();});
      setTimeout(()=>{instances.forEach(r=>{r.resize();r.refresh();});},320);
    });
  }

  function watchPanel(){
    const panel=$('toolPanel'),bodyEl=$('panelBody');
    if(!panel||!bodyEl)return;
    const sync=()=>{
      const root=bodyEl.querySelector('.evcx-root');
      panel.classList.toggle('evcx-on',!!root);
      if(root)initRoot(root);else instances.clear();
    };
    new MutationObserver(sync).observe(bodyEl,{childList:true});
    sync();
  }

  // Keep the rulers in step with every place the app re-syncs the sliders.
  const baseSync=window.syncTransformSliders;
  if(typeof baseSync==='function'&&!baseSync.__evcx){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=baseSync.apply(this,arguments);try{refreshAll();}catch(_e){}return r;};
    wrapped.__evcx=true;window.syncTransformSliders=wrapped;
  }

  // The Transform panel now opens with the CapCut layout (always starts on Position, like CapCut).
  if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined'&&panelContent&&panelContent.transform){
    panelContent.transform.body=body();
  }

  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',watchPanel,{once:true});
  else watchPanel();

  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>instances.forEach(r=>r.resize()),{passive:true});
  window.__evcxTransformApi={refresh:refreshAll};
})();
</script>


<style id="ethvelle-v341-anim-tracking-css">
  /* v341: when an ANIMATION clip is selected, the Track button sits right after the fixed
     Delete / Done / Split / Duplicate buttons (order -5) so it is on screen without scrolling the toolbar. */
  #bottomToolsClip.ev-v341-anim-clip #clipTrackingTool{
    display:flex!important;order:-5;flex:0 0 64px;min-width:64px;max-width:64px;
    align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v341-anim-tracking-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__evV341AnimTracking)return;
  window.__evV341AnimTracking=true;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  function animClipOfSelection(){
    try{
      const sel=(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined')?selectedClip:null;
      if(sel==null)return null;
      const key=String(sel);
      if(key!=='main'&&!/^seg\d+$/.test(key))return null;
      const clip=typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(key):null;
      return clip&&typeof clip==='object'?clip:null;
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function syncAnimTrackButton(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row)return;
    const clip=animClipOfSelection();
    row.classList.toggle('ev-v341-anim-clip',!!clip);
    const btn=$('clipTrackingTool');
    if(btn&&clip){
      btn.classList.add('ev-v71-track-visible');
      btn.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
    }
  }
  const prior=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof prior==='function'&&!prior.__evV341){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=prior.apply(this,arguments);try{syncAnimTrackButton();}catch(_e){}return r;};
    wrapped.__evV341=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',syncAnimTrackButton,{once:true});
  else syncAnimTrackButton();
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v342:
     (1) Character-effect "Editing" sliders now use the SAME CapCut tick-ruler sliders as the Transform panel
         (flat rows: label | ruler | value, no card/block boxes).
     (2) A character effect is now hidden underneath any clip that sits ABOVE it in the layer order
         (live preview + export). Clips below the effect never hide it.
     (3) Adjust panel: Perspective now also works on character effects; new "Wide" and "Length" options
         (horizontal / vertical stretch) that work for video, image, background and character-effect clips,
         in the live preview and in the exported video. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v342-css">
  /* ---- (1) flat tick-ruler rows inside the Character Effects panel ---- */
  #evvVisualPanel .evv-fx-adjustments.evx-on{border-top:0!important;margin-top:6px!important;padding:0!important;gap:0!important;}
  .evx-rows{display:grid;grid-template-columns:112px minmax(0,1fr) 56px;grid-auto-rows:44px;align-items:center;padding:2px 2px 12px;
    -webkit-user-select:none;user-select:none;-webkit-touch-callout:none;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent;}
  .evx-rows *{box-sizing:border-box;}
  .evx-lab{align-self:center;font-size:13.5px;color:#D2D2D6;white-space:nowrap;}
  .evx-val{align-self:center;justify-self:end;font-size:14.5px;color:#DADADF;text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;white-space:nowrap;}
  .evx-ruler{display:block;width:100%;height:44px;background:none!important;border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;
    touch-action:pan-y!important;cursor:ew-resize;outline:none;}
  .evx-ruler:focus-visible{box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(47,92,232,.7)!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v342-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV342)return;
  window.__ethvelleV342=true;

  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  const list=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const chars=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(characters)?characters:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const selId=()=>{try{return String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');}catch(_e){return '';}};
  const stageDims=()=>{try{return {W:Number(stageW)||0,H:Number(stageH)||0};}catch(_e){return {W:0,H:0};}};
  const isFxClip=o=>!!(o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&o.ethvelleEffectKind==='character');

  /* ======================================================================
     (3a) Wide / Length stretch  -  shared by every media path.
     The CSS transform suffix is now:  [scale3d(wide,length,1)] [perspective rotateX rotateY]
     ====================================================================== */
  const DIST=900,LIMIT=55;
  const pers=v=>{v=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(v)?Math.max(-LIMIT,Math.min(LIMIT,v)):0;};
  const strPct=v=>{v=Number(v);return(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>0)?Math.max(25,Math.min(300,v)):100;};
  window.ethvelleMediaStretch=function(clip){return {w:strPct(clip&&clip.mediaWide)/100,l:strPct(clip&&clip.mediaLength)/100};};
  const SUFFIX_RE=/(?:\s*scale3d\([^)]*\))?(?:\s*perspective\(\d+(?:\.\d+)?px\)\s*rotateX\([^)]*\)\s*rotateY\([^)]*\))?\s*$/;
  window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix=function(clip){
    const x=pers(clip&&clip.mediaPerspectiveX),y=pers(clip&&clip.mediaPerspectiveY),s=window.ethvelleMediaStretch(clip);
    let out='';
    if(s.w!==1||s.l!==1)out+=` scale3d(${s.w.toFixed(4)}, ${s.l.toFixed(4)}, 1)`;
    if(x||y)out+=` perspective(${DIST}px) rotateX(${y}deg) rotateY(${x}deg)`;
    return out;
  };
  window.ethvelleStripPerspective=function(value){return String(value||'').replace(SUFFIX_RE,'');};

  /* ======================================================================
     Tick ruler (same look + behaviour as the Transform panel's rulers)
     ====================================================================== */
  const SP=7.4,MAJOR=10,ROW_H=44,BLUE='#2F5CE8',IND_TOP=12,IND_H=21,TICK_H=11,BASE_Y=IND_TOP+IND_H;
  function mountRuler(canvas,def){
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    let w=0,dpr=1,pos=0,drag=null,raf=0,pending=null,anim=0,lastEmit=null;
    const clampV=v=>Math.max(def.min,Math.min(def.max,v));
    function rr(x,y,ww,hh,r){
      ctx.beginPath();
      if(ctx.roundRect){ctx.roundRect(x,y,ww,hh,r);}
      else{ctx.moveTo(x+r,y);ctx.arcTo(x+ww,y,x+ww,y+hh,r);ctx.arcTo(x+ww,y+hh,x,y+hh,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y+hh,x,y,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+ww,y,r);ctx.closePath();}
      ctx.fill();
    }
    function resize(){
      const cw=canvas.clientWidth|0;if(!cw)return false;
      dpr=Math.min(3,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
      if(cw!==w||canvas.width!==Math.round(cw*dpr)){w=cw;canvas.width=Math.round(w*dpr);canvas.height=Math.round(ROW_H*dpr);}
      draw();return true;
    }
    function draw(){
      if(!w)return;
      ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,ROW_H);
      const cx=w/2,half=cx/SP;
      const lo=Math.max(Math.ceil(def.min),Math.floor(pos-half)-1);
      const hi=Math.min(Math.floor(def.max),Math.ceil(pos+half)+1);
      for(let v=lo;v<=hi;v++){
        const x=cx+(v-pos)*SP;
        if(x<-2||x>w+2)continue;
        if(Math.abs(x-cx)<1.6)continue;
        const edge=Math.min(x,w-x);
        const fade=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(edge-SP*.4)/(SP*2.6)));
        if(fade<=0)continue;
        const major=(v%MAJOR===0);
        ctx.fillStyle=major?`rgba(255,255,255,${(.93*fade).toFixed(3)})`:`rgba(255,255,255,${(.40*fade).toFixed(3)})`;
        rr(x-.8,BASE_Y-TICK_H,1.6,TICK_H,.8);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle=BLUE;rr(cx-1.3,IND_TOP,2.6,IND_H,1.3);
    }
    function apply(v,emit){
      pos=clampV(v);const iv=Math.round(pos);
      if(emit&&lastEmit!==iv){lastEmit=iv;try{def.input(iv);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle ruler:',err);}}
      canvas.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(iv));draw();
    }
    let dirty=false;
    function finish(){if(lastEmit!==null){try{def.commit(Math.round(pos));}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle ruler:',err);}}lastEmit=null;}
    function stopAnim(){if(anim){cancelAnimationFrame(anim);anim=0;}}
    function settle(){
      stopAnim();const from=pos,to=Math.round(pos);
      if(Math.abs(from-to)<.001){apply(to,true);finish();return;}
      const t0=performance.now(),dur=130;
      const step=now=>{const k=Math.min(1,(now-t0)/dur),e=1-Math.pow(1-k,3);apply(from+(to-from)*e,true);if(k<1)anim=requestAnimationFrame(step);else{anim=0;apply(to,true);finish();}};
      anim=requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }
    function fling(vel){
      stopAnim();let last=performance.now();
      const step=now=>{
        const dt=Math.min(34,now-last);last=now;pos+=vel*dt;vel*=Math.exp(-dt/240);
        if(pos<=def.min||pos>=def.max){apply(pos<=def.min?def.min:def.max,true);anim=0;finish();return;}
        apply(pos,true);
        if(Math.abs(vel)<.0035){anim=0;settle();return;}
        anim=requestAnimationFrame(step);
      };
      anim=requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }
    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(drag||(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0))return;
      stopAnim();drag={id:e.pointerId,x0:e.clientX,p0:pos,s:[{t:e.timeStamp,x:e.clientX}]};lastEmit=null;
      try{canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:true});
    canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      drag.s.push({t:e.timeStamp,x:e.clientX});if(drag.s.length>8)drag.s.shift();
      pending=drag.p0-(e.clientX-drag.x0)/SP;
      if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{raf=0;if(pending!==null){apply(pending,true);pending=null;}});
      if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:false});
    const end=e=>{
      if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      if(raf){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;}
      if(pending!==null){apply(pending,true);pending=null;}
      const s=drag.s,last=s[s.length-1];let vel=0;
      for(let i=s.length-2;i>=0;i--){if(last.t-s[i].t>=70||i===0){const dt=Math.max(1,last.t-s[i].t);vel=-(last.x-s[i].x)/dt/SP;break;}}
      if(e.type==='pointercancel'||e.timeStamp-last.t>90)vel=0;
      drag=null;try{canvas.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      if(Math.abs(vel)>.012)fling(Math.max(-2.2,Math.min(2.2,vel)));else settle();
      e.stopPropagation();
    };
    canvas.addEventListener('pointerup',end);
    canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',end);
    canvas.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
      const step=e.shiftKey?MAJOR:1;
      if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'||e.key==='ArrowDown'){apply(Math.round(pos)-step,true);finish();e.preventDefault();}
      else if(e.key==='ArrowRight'||e.key==='ArrowUp'){apply(Math.round(pos)+step,true);finish();e.preventDefault();}
    });
    pos=clampV(Number(def.value)||0);
    canvas.setAttribute('role','slider');canvas.setAttribute('tabindex','0');canvas.setAttribute('aria-label',def.label||'');
    canvas.setAttribute('aria-valuemin',String(def.min));canvas.setAttribute('aria-valuemax',String(def.max));canvas.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(Math.round(pos)));
    if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){new ResizeObserver(()=>resize()).observe(canvas);}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{resize();setTimeout(resize,320);});
    return {resize,draw};
  }

  /* ======================================================================
     (1) Character-effect Editing sliders -> Transform-style rulers
     ====================================================================== */
  function convertFxSliders(){
    return; /* v412: Effects adjustments keep the normal slider look (same as the Adjust panel) */
    const host=$('evvVisualPanel');if(!host)return;
    const panel=host.querySelector('.evv-fx-adjustments');if(!panel)return;
    const cards=[...panel.querySelectorAll(':scope > .evv-slider-card')];
    if(!cards.length)return;
    const defs=cards.map(card=>{
      const input=card.querySelector('input[type="range"]');if(!input)return null;
      const prop=String(input.id||'').replace(/^evv\d*_fx_/,'');
      const labelEl=card.querySelector('label');
      const outEl=card.querySelector('output');
      const unit=((outEl&&outEl.textContent)||'').replace(/[-\d.\s]/g,'')||'%';
      return {prop,label:(labelEl&&labelEl.textContent)||prop,min:Number(input.min),max:Number(input.max),value:Number(input.value),unit};
    }).filter(Boolean);
    if(!defs.length)return;
    panel.classList.add('evx-on');
    panel.innerHTML='<div class="evx-rows">'+defs.map(d=>
      `<span class="evx-lab">${d.label}</span><canvas class="evx-ruler" data-evx="${d.prop}"></canvas><span class="evx-val" id="evx_${d.prop}_val">${Math.round(d.value)}${d.unit}</span>`
    ).join('')+'</div>';
    defs.forEach(d=>{
      const cv=panel.querySelector(`canvas[data-evx="${d.prop}"]`),valEl=$('evx_'+d.prop+'_val');
      if(!cv)return;
      mountRuler(cv,{min:d.min,max:d.max,value:d.value,label:d.label,
        input:v=>{if(valEl)valEl.textContent=v+d.unit;if(typeof window.setEthvelleEffectSetting==='function')window.setEthvelleEffectSetting(d.prop,v,false);},
        commit:v=>{if(typeof window.setEthvelleEffectSetting==='function')window.setEthvelleEffectSetting(d.prop,v,true);}});
    });
  }

  /* ======================================================================
     (3b) Adjust panel: add "Wide" and "Length" cards (same card style as the rest of that panel)
     ====================================================================== */
  function targetModel(){
    const id=selId();if(!id)return null;
    try{const bg=(Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]).find(b=>b&&String(b.id)===id);if(bg)return {kind:'background',model:bg};}catch(_e){}
    const ov=list().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id&&(o.type==='video'||o.type==='image'));
    return ov?{kind:'overlay',model:ov}:null;
  }
  function repaintFxIfNeeded(){
    const api=window.__ethvelleV244Debug;
    try{if(api&&typeof api.paintEffectPreview==='function')api.paintEffectPreview();else if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
  }
  let persistTimer=0;
  window.ethvelleSetMediaStretch=function(axis,value){
    const tg=targetModel();if(!tg)return;
    const key=axis==='length'?'mediaLength':'mediaWide';
    tg.model[key]=Math.round(clamp(value,25,300));
    const out=$(axis==='length'?'evAdj_lengthVal':'evAdj_wideVal');if(out)out.textContent=tg.model[key]+'%';
    // Re-use the Perspective setter: it re-applies the whole transform suffix on the live element.
    try{if(typeof window.ethvelleSetMediaPerspective==='function')window.ethvelleSetMediaPerspective('x',tg.model.mediaPerspectiveX||0);}catch(_e){}
    if(isFxClip(tg.model))repaintFxIfNeeded();
    clearTimeout(persistTimer);
    persistTimer=setTimeout(()=>{try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){}},420);
  };
  function stretchCard(axis,label,val){
    const id='evAdj_'+axis,pct=((val-25)/275)*100;
    return `<div class="evv-slider-card evlt-adjust-slider evx-stretch-card"><div class="evv-slider-head"><label for="${id}">${label}</label><output id="${id}Val">${val}%</output></div><input type="range" id="${id}" min="25" max="300" step="1" value="${val}" style="--evv-progress:${pct}%" oninput="window.ethvelleSetMediaStretch('${axis}',this.value);this.style.setProperty('--evv-progress',((Number(this.value)-25)/275)*100+'%')"></div>`;
  }
  function enhanceAdjustPanel(){
    const host=$('evltAdjustmentsPanel');if(!host)return;
    const wrap=host.querySelector('.evlt-controls');if(!wrap)return;
    if(wrap.querySelector('.evx-stretch'))return;
    if(!wrap.querySelector('.evlt-persp-heading'))return; // same targets as Perspective (not character animation clips)
    const tg=targetModel();if(!tg)return;
    const w=Math.round(strPct(tg.model.mediaWide)),l=Math.round(strPct(tg.model.mediaLength));
    const box=document.createElement('div');box.className='evx-stretch';box.style.cssText='display:contents';
    box.innerHTML=`<div class="evlt-stretch-heading" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;margin-top:6px"><b style="font-size:13px">Stretch</b></div>${stretchCard('wide','Wide',w)}${stretchCard('length','Length',l)}`;
    const reset=wrap.querySelector('.evlt-secondary');
    if(reset)wrap.insertBefore(box,reset);else wrap.appendChild(box);
  }
  // Reset also resets Wide / Length.
  const prevReset=window.ethvelleResetAdjustments;
  if(typeof prevReset==='function'&&!prevReset.__v342){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const tg=targetModel();
      if(tg){tg.model.mediaWide=100;tg.model.mediaLength=100;}
      const r=prevReset.apply(this,arguments);
      ['wide','length'].forEach(a=>{
        const i=$('evAdj_'+a),o=$('evAdj_'+a+'Val');
        if(i){i.value=100;i.style.setProperty('--evv-progress',((100-25)/275)*100+'%');}if(o)o.textContent='100%';
      });
      if(tg&&isFxClip(tg.model))repaintFxIfNeeded();
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__v342=true;window.ethvelleResetAdjustments=wrapped;
  }
  // Colour / Perspective changes on a character effect must repaint the effect canvas while paused.
  ['ethvelleSetAdjustment','ethvelleSetMediaPerspective'].forEach(name=>{
    const base=window[name];if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__v342)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const r=base.apply(this,arguments);const tg=targetModel();if(tg&&isFxClip(tg.model))repaintFxIfNeeded();return r;};
    wrapped.__v342=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });

  /* Panel watcher: converts the Editing sliders and injects Wide/Length whenever those panels are (re)built. */
  let scan=0;
  function scheduleScan(){if(scan)return;scan=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{scan=0;try{convertFxSliders();}catch(e){console.warn('v342 fx sliders:',e);}try{enhanceAdjustPanel();}catch(e){console.warn('v342 stretch:',e);}});}
  const body=$('panelBody')||$('toolPanel');
  if(body&&typeof MutationObserver==='function')new MutationObserver(scheduleScan).observe(body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  // The Effects tool used to open the panel without the slider block until an effect was picked; render it in full right away.
  const prevOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof prevOpen==='function'&&!prevOpen.__v342){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      const r=prevOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='visualEffects')requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{if(typeof window.renderVisualPanel==='function')window.renderVisualPanel('visualEffects');}catch(_e){}scheduleScan();}));
      else scheduleScan();
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__v342=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('openPanel=window.openPanel');}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* ======================================================================
     (2)+(3c) Character-effect drawing: hide under clips above, filters, Perspective, Wide/Length
     ====================================================================== */
  let opaque=null;
  const opaqueBlock=()=>{if(!opaque){opaque=document.createElement('canvas');opaque.width=2;opaque.height=2;const c=opaque.getContext('2d');c.fillStyle='#000';c.fillRect(0,0,2,2);}return opaque;};
  const scratch=[null,null];
  function scratchCanvas(i,w,h){
    let c=scratch[i];if(!c){c=document.createElement('canvas');scratch[i]=c;}
    if(c.width!==w||c.height!==h){c.width=w;c.height=h;}
    return c;
  }
  function occludersFor(ov,t){
    const arr=list(),idx=arr.indexOf(ov);if(idx<0)return [];
    const out=[];
    for(let i=idx+1;i<arr.length;i++){
      const o=arr[i];
      if(!o||o.ethvelleEffectClip||o.__ethvelleBehindBackground)continue;
      if(o.type!=='image'&&o.type!=='video'&&o.type!=='text')continue;
      if(!(t>=Number(o.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(o.trimEnd||0)))continue;
      out.push(o);
    }
    return out;
  }
  function clipTransform(o,t){
    let keyed=null,tracked=null;
    try{keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(o,'transform',t):null;}catch(_e){}
    try{if(o.clipTracking&&o.clipTracking.enabled&&o.clipTracking.targetId&&typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function')tracked=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(o,t,keyed);}catch(_e){}
    const tr=tracked||keyed||o.transform||{};
    let motion={x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1,opacity:1};
    try{if(typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function')motion=Object.assign(motion,window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(o,t)||{});}catch(_e){}
    const op=Number(motion.opacity);
    return {
      ox:(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion.x)||0),oy:(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion.y)||0),
      sc:Math.max(.0001,(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(motion.scale)||1)),
      rot:(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion.rotation)||0),
      alpha:Number.isFinite(op)?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,op)):1
    };
  }
  function drawTextOccluder(c,o,t,W,H){
    const k=clipTransform(o,t),size=Math.max(8,Number(o.fontSize)||36);
    c.save();
    try{
      c.globalAlpha=k.alpha*(clamp(o.textOverallOpacity==null?100:o.textOverallOpacity,0,100)/100);
      c.translate(W/2+k.ox,H/2+k.oy);if(k.rot)c.rotate(k.rot*Math.PI/180);
      c.scale(k.sc*(o.mirrorX?-1:1),k.sc*(o.mirrorY?-1:1));
      c.font=`${o.fontStyle==='italic'?'italic ':''}${Number(o.fontWeight)||800} ${size}px "${String(o.fontFamily||'Inter').replace(/["\\]/g,'')}", sans-serif`;
      c.textAlign=o.textAlign||'center';c.textBaseline='middle';
      const maxW=W*.82,lines=[];
      String(o.textContent||'').split('\n').forEach(par=>{let line='';String(par).split(/\s+/).forEach(word=>{const test=line?line+' '+word:word;if(line&&c.measureText(test).width>maxW){lines.push(line);line=word;}else line=test;});lines.push(line);});
      const lh=size*1.2,total=lines.length*lh,pad=Math.max(0,Number(o.textBackgroundPadding==null?7:o.textBackgroundPadding));
      const sw=Math.max(0,Number(o.strokeWidth)||0);
      lines.forEach((line,i)=>{
        const y=-total/2+lh*(i+.5),lw=Math.min(maxW,c.measureText(line).width);
        let x=0;if((o.textAlign||'center')==='left')x=-maxW/2;else if(o.textAlign==='right')x=maxW/2;
        if(o.textBackgroundEnabled){const bx=o.textAlign==='left'?x-pad:o.textAlign==='right'?x-lw-pad:x-lw/2-pad;c.fillRect(bx,y-lh/2-pad/2,lw+pad*2,lh+pad);}
        if(sw>0){c.lineWidth=sw*2;c.lineJoin='round';c.strokeText(line,x,y,maxW);}
        c.fillText(line,x,y,maxW);
      });
    }finally{c.restore();}
  }
  function drawOccluder(c,o,t,W,H){
    if(o.type==='text'){drawTextOccluder(c,o,t,W,H);return;}
    const id=String(o.id);let media=null;
    try{media=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;}catch(_e){}
    let iw=Number(o.sourceWidth)||0,ih=Number(o.sourceHeight)||0;
    if(media){iw=Number(media.videoWidth||media.naturalWidth||media.width)||iw;ih=Number(media.videoHeight||media.naturalHeight||media.height)||ih;}
    if(!(iw>0&&ih>0))return;
    let src=null,block=false;
    if(o.type==='video'){
      let ck=null;try{if(o.chromaKey&&o.chromaKey.enabled&&typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases)ck=overlayChromaCanvases[id];}catch(_e){}
      if(ck&&ck.width){src=ck;}else{src=opaqueBlock();block=true;} // a normal video is a solid rectangle: no need to wait for a decoded frame
    }else{
      if(!media||(media.tagName==='IMG'&&!media.complete))return;
      src=media;
    }
    let crop={left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
    try{crop=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(o.crop):(o.crop||crop);}catch(_e){}
    const L=clamp(crop.left,0,.92),R=clamp(crop.right,0,.92),T=clamp(crop.top,0,.92),B=clamp(crop.bottom,0,.92);
    const fit=Math.min(W/iw,H/ih),fw=iw*fit,fh=ih*fit;
    const dx=(W-fw)/2+fw*L,dy=(H-fh)/2+fh*T,dw=Math.max(1,fw*(1-L-R)),dh=Math.max(1,fh*(1-T-B));
    let sx,sy,sw,sh;
    if(block){sx=0;sy=0;sw=2;sh=2;}
    else{const sW=src.videoWidth||src.naturalWidth||src.width||iw,sH=src.videoHeight||src.naturalHeight||src.height||ih;sx=sW*L;sy=sH*T;sw=Math.max(1,sW*(1-L-R));sh=Math.max(1,sH*(1-T-B));}
    const k=clipTransform(o,t),st=window.ethvelleMediaStretch(o);
    const px=pers(o.mediaPerspectiveX),py=pers(o.mediaPerspectiveY);
    c.save();
    try{
      c.globalAlpha=k.alpha;
      c.translate(W/2+k.ox,H/2+k.oy);if(k.rot)c.rotate(k.rot*Math.PI/180);
      c.scale(k.sc*(o.mirrorX?-1:1),k.sc*(o.mirrorY?-1:1));
      if(st.w!==1||st.l!==1)c.scale(st.w,st.l);
      c.translate(-W/2,-H/2);
      if(o.visualMask&&typeof window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip==='function')window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(c,o.visualMask,W,H);
      if((px||py)&&typeof window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage==='function')window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage(c,src,sx,sy,sw,sh,dx,dy,dw,dh,px,py,W/2,H/2,DIST);
      else c.drawImage(src,sx,sy,sw,sh,dx,dy,dw,dh);
    }catch(_e){}
    finally{c.restore();}
  }
  function effectCenter(ov){
    try{
      const ch=chars().find(x=>x&&x.id===ov.ethvelleEffectCharId)||window.__ethvelleCharGhost(ov.ethvelleEffectCharId);
      const f=ch&&typeof window.__ethvelleCharFrame==='function'?window.__ethvelleCharFrame(ch):null;
      if(f&&Number.isFinite(f.x)&&Number.isFinite(f.y)){const tr=ov.transform||{};return {x:f.x+(Number(tr.offsetX)||0),y:f.y+(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)};}
    }catch(_e){}
    const d=stageDims();return {x:d.W/2,y:d.H/2};
  }
  function effectScratchBounds(ov,time,d,cw,ch){
    const character=chars().find(x=>x&&x.id===ov.ethvelleEffectCharId)||window.__ethvelleCharGhost(ov.ethvelleEffectCharId);
    const frame=character&&typeof window.__ethvelleCharFrame==='function'?window.__ethvelleCharFrame(character):null;
    if(!frame||!(Number(frame.r)>0))return null;
    let keyed=null,tracked=null;
    try{keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',time):null;}catch(_e){}
    try{if(ov.clipTracking&&ov.clipTracking.enabled&&ov.clipTracking.targetId&&typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function')tracked=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov,time,keyed);}catch(_e){}
    const tr=tracked||keyed||ov.transform||{};
    const cfg=typeof window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg==='function'?window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg(ov.visualEffects||{}):{size:1,ox:0,oy:0};
    const size=Math.max(.1,Number(cfg.size)||1)*Math.max(.1,Number(tr.scale)||1);
    const radius=Math.max(1,3.2*Number(frame.r)*size);
    const cx=Number(frame.x)+(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(cfg.ox)||0)*Number(frame.r);
    const cy=Number(frame.y)+(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(cfg.oy)||0)*Number(frame.r);
    const left=Math.max(0,cx-radius),top=Math.max(0,cy-radius);
    const right=Math.min(d.W,cx+radius),bottom=Math.min(d.H,cy+radius);
    if(!(right>left&&bottom>top))return null;
    const sx=Math.max(0,Math.floor(left*cw/d.W)),sy=Math.max(0,Math.floor(top*ch/d.H));
    const ex=Math.min(cw,Math.ceil(right*cw/d.W)),ey=Math.min(ch,Math.ceil(bottom*ch/d.H));
    if(!(ex>sx&&ey>sy))return null;
    return {x:left,y:top,w:right-left,h:bottom-top,sx,sy,sw:ex-sx,sh:ey-sy};
  }
  const prevDraw=window.ethvelleDrawCharacterEffectClip;
  if(typeof prevDraw==='function'&&!prevDraw.__v342){
    const wrapped=function(ctx,ov,t){
      if(!ctx||!ov||!isFxClip(ov))return prevDraw.apply(this,arguments);
      const time=Number(t)||0,d=stageDims();
      if(!(d.W>0&&d.H>0))return prevDraw.apply(this,arguments);
      const occ=occludersFor(ov,time),st=window.ethvelleMediaStretch(ov);
      const px=pers(ov.mediaPerspectiveX),py=pers(ov.mediaPerspectiveY);
      let filt='none';try{filt=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter?(window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(ov.adjustments,ov.enhanceAmount||0)||'none'):'none';}catch(_e){}
      const warp=st.w!==1||st.l!==1||!!px||!!py;
      if(!occ.length&&!warp&&(!filt||filt==='none'))return prevDraw.apply(this,arguments);
      const m=ctx.getTransform(),cw=Math.max(1,Math.round(d.W*(Math.abs(m.a)||1))),ch=Math.max(1,Math.round(d.H*(Math.abs(m.d)||1)));
      const region=warp?null:effectScratchBounds(ov,time,d,cw,ch);
      const fresh=(cv,rect)=>{
        const g=cv.getContext('2d');g.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);g.globalAlpha=1;g.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';g.filter='none';
        if(rect)g.clearRect(rect.sx,rect.sy,rect.sw,rect.sh);else g.clearRect(0,0,cw,ch);
        g.setTransform(cw/d.W,0,0,ch/d.H,0,0);return g;
      };
      const A=scratchCanvas(0,cw,ch),ac=fresh(A,region);
      if(region){ac.save();ac.beginPath();ac.rect(region.x,region.y,region.w,region.h);ac.clip();}
      prevDraw.call(this,ac,ov,time);
      if(region)ac.restore();
      let out=A;
      if(warp){
        const B=scratchCanvas(1,cw,ch),bc=fresh(B,null),cen=effectCenter(ov);
        bc.save();
        bc.translate(cen.x,cen.y);bc.scale(st.w,st.l);bc.translate(-cen.x,-cen.y);
        if((px||py)&&typeof window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage==='function')window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage(bc,A,0,0,cw,ch,0,0,d.W,d.H,px,py,cen.x,cen.y,DIST);
        else bc.drawImage(A,0,0,cw,ch,0,0,d.W,d.H);
        bc.restore();
        out=B;
      }
      if(occ.length){
        const oc=out.getContext('2d');
        oc.setTransform(cw/d.W,0,0,ch/d.H,0,0);
        if(region){oc.save();oc.beginPath();oc.rect(region.x,region.y,region.w,region.h);oc.clip();}
        oc.globalCompositeOperation='destination-out';
        occ.forEach(o=>{try{drawOccluder(oc,o,time,d.W,d.H);}catch(err){console.warn('Effect occluder:',err);}});
        if(region)oc.restore();
        oc.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';oc.globalAlpha=1;
      }
      ctx.save();
      try{
        if(filt&&filt!=='none')ctx.filter=filt;
        if(region)ctx.drawImage(out,region.sx,region.sy,region.sw,region.sh,region.x,region.y,region.w,region.h);
        else ctx.drawImage(out,0,0,cw,ch,0,0,d.W,d.H);
      }finally{ctx.restore();}
    };
    wrapped.__v342=true;window.ethvelleDrawCharacterEffectClip=wrapped;
  }

  // Repaint the effect canvas whenever clips are added / reordered / moved so occlusion updates while paused.
  ['renderOverlayLayers','reorderSelectedLayer','duplicateSelectedLayer','addMediaOverlay','deleteSelectedClip'].forEach(name=>{
    const base=window[name];if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__v342Repaint)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const r=base.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(repaintFxIfNeeded);return r;};
    wrapped.__v342Repaint=true;window[name]=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)(name+'=window.'+name);}catch(_e){}
  });

  window.__ethvelleV342={convertFxSliders,enhanceAdjustPanel,occludersFor,mountRuler};
})();
</script>

  }

  @keyframes evca-v364-breathe{0%,100%{transform:scale(1)}50%{transform:scale(1.05)}}

<script id="ethvelle-v364-audio-fx-pack">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* Ethvelle v364: 14 extra audio effects + 14 extra voice filters.
     One graph builder (ethvelleBuildAudioFx) is shared by live preview and by the offline export,
     so what you hear in the editor is what gets rendered. */
  const PI=Math.PI;

  /* ---------- Extra voice filters (EQ profiles) ----------
     Base fields (highpass/lowpass/type/frequency/q/gain) are consumed by the existing live/export chain;
     eq/sat/trim are consumed by the v364 stage that sits after the existing chain. */
  const VOICES={
    podcast:  {highpass:80, lowpass:16000,type:'lowshelf', frequency:130, q:.7, gain:3.5,eq:[['peaking',3200,.9,3],['highshelf',9000,.7,1.5]]},
    broadcast:{highpass:100,lowpass:15000,type:'lowshelf', frequency:200, q:.7, gain:3,  eq:[['peaking',2800,1,4.5],['highshelf',8000,.7,2]]},
    narrator: {highpass:60, lowpass:12000,type:'lowshelf', frequency:150, q:.8, gain:4.5,eq:[['peaking',280,.9,2],['highshelf',6500,.7,-1.5]]},
    rich:     {highpass:50, lowpass:10500,type:'lowshelf', frequency:180, q:.8, gain:6,  eq:[['peaking',650,.8,2],['highshelf',7000,.7,-2]]},
    crisp:    {highpass:120,lowpass:19000,type:'peaking',  frequency:5000,q:.9, gain:5,  eq:[['peaking',2500,1,2.5],['lowshelf',250,.7,-2]]},
    cinematic:{highpass:40, lowpass:18000,type:'lowshelf', frequency:100, q:.8, gain:6,  eq:[['peaking',450,1,-2.5],['highshelf',9000,.7,3]]},
    air:      {highpass:150,lowpass:20000,type:'highshelf',frequency:6000,q:.7, gain:7,  eq:[['peaking',4500,1,3.5],['lowshelf',200,.7,-3]],trim:1},
    smooth:   {highpass:70, lowpass:11000,type:'peaking',  frequency:7000,q:2,  gain:-8, eq:[['lowshelf',200,.7,2],['highshelf',9500,.7,-3]]},
    nasal:    {highpass:220,lowpass:6500, type:'peaking',  frequency:1150,q:1.3,gain:8,  eq:[['lowshelf',250,.7,-5]],trim:1},
    hollow:   {highpass:120,lowpass:9000, type:'peaking',  frequency:900, q:1.1,gain:-9, eq:[['peaking',2200,1,3]]},
    muffled:  {highpass:60, lowpass:1400, type:'highshelf',frequency:1800,q:.7, gain:-9, eq:[['lowshelf',200,.7,2]]},
    walkie:   {highpass:420,lowpass:3000, type:'peaking',  frequency:1700,q:1,  gain:7,  eq:[['peaking',2400,1.2,3]],sat:2.2,trim:.55},
    intercom: {highpass:500,lowpass:2800, type:'peaking',  frequency:1500,q:1.2,gain:8,  eq:[],sat:3,trim:.45},
    vintage:  {highpass:300,lowpass:4800, type:'peaking',  frequency:2100,q:.8, gain:5,  eq:[['highshelf',3500,.7,-3]],sat:1.6,trim:.7}
  };
  const baseProfile=window.ethvelleAudioVoiceProfile;
  window.ethvelleAudioVoiceProfile=function(key){
    const k=String(key||'natural');
    if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(VOICES,k))return Object.assign({},VOICES[k]);
    return typeof baseProfile==='function'?baseProfile.apply(this,arguments):{highpass:30,lowpass:20000,type:'peaking',frequency:1200,q:.55,gain:0};
  };

  /* ---------- node helpers ---------- */
  const newReg=()=>({nodes:[],oscs:[]});
  const track=(reg,n)=>{reg.nodes.push(n);return n;};
  const G=(ctx,reg,v)=>{const g=ctx.createGain();g.gain.value=v;return track(reg,g);};
  const B=(ctx,reg,type,f,q,gain)=>{const b=ctx.createBiquadFilter();b.type=type;b.frequency.value=f;if(q!=null)b.Q.value=q;if(gain!=null)b.gain.value=gain;return track(reg,b);};
  const D=(ctx,reg,t)=>{const d=ctx.createDelay(2);d.delayTime.value=t;return track(reg,d);};
  const P=(ctx,reg,v)=>{if(typeof ctx.createStereoPanner==='function'){const p=ctx.createStereoPanner();p.pan.value=v;return track(reg,p);}return G(ctx,reg,1);};
  const LFO=(ctx,reg,type,hz,depth,param)=>{const o=ctx.createOscillator();o.type=type;o.frequency.value=hz;const g=ctx.createGain();g.gain.value=depth;o.connect(g);g.connect(param);o.start(0);reg.oscs.push(o);track(reg,g);return o;};
  const SHAPE=(ctx,reg,curve,os)=>{const w=ctx.createWaveShaper();w.curve=curve;w.oversample=os||'none';return track(reg,w);};
  const driveCurve=k=>{const n=2048,c=new Float32Array(n),t=Math.tanh(k);for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=i*2/(n-1)-1;c[i]=Math.tanh(x*k)/t;}return c;};
  const crushCurve=l=>{const n=2048,c=new Float32Array(n);for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=i*2/(n-1)-1;c[i]=Math.round(x*l)/l;}return c;};
  /* Deterministic synthetic impulse response: the same noise every time, so preview and export match. */
  function makeIR(ctx,seconds,decay,lpf){
    const sr=ctx.sampleRate,len=Math.max(64,Math.floor(sr*seconds)),buf=ctx.createBuffer(2,len,sr);
    let seed=0x2F6E2B1;
    const rnd=()=>{seed=(Math.imul(seed,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return seed/4294967296*2-1;};
    const attack=Math.max(1,Math.floor(sr*.004));
    for(let c=0;c<2;c++){
      const d=buf.getChannelData(c);let lp=0;
      for(let i=0;i<len;i++){const t=i/len;lp+=(rnd()-lp)*lpf;d[i]=lp*Math.pow(1-t,decay)*(i<attack?i/attack:1);}
    }
    return buf;
  }
  function reverbSend(ctx,reg,i,o,s){
    const dry=G(ctx,reg,s.dry);i.connect(dry);dry.connect(o);
    const conv=ctx.createConvolver();conv.buffer=makeIR(ctx,s.sec,s.decay,s.lp);track(reg,conv);
    const wet=G(ctx,reg,s.wet);
    if(s.pre>0){const pre=D(ctx,reg,s.pre);i.connect(pre);pre.connect(conv);}else i.connect(conv);
    conv.connect(wet);wet.connect(o);
  }

  /* ---------- extra audio effects ---------- */
  const FX={
    reverb:   (c,r,i,o)=>reverbSend(c,r,i,o,{sec:1.2,decay:3,  lp:.55,pre:0,   wet:.5, dry:.9}),
    hall:     (c,r,i,o)=>reverbSend(c,r,i,o,{sec:2.8,decay:2.6,lp:.42,pre:.02, wet:.6, dry:.85}),
    cathedral:(c,r,i,o)=>reverbSend(c,r,i,o,{sec:5,  decay:2.1,lp:.28,pre:.045,wet:.7, dry:.75}),
    slapback:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const dry=G(c,r,1);i.connect(dry);dry.connect(o);
      const d=D(c,r,.11),fb=G(c,r,.18),wet=G(c,r,.55);
      i.connect(d);d.connect(wet);wet.connect(o);d.connect(fb);fb.connect(d);
    },
    pingpong:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const dry=G(c,r,1);i.connect(dry);dry.connect(o);
      const d1=D(c,r,.26),d2=D(c,r,.26),fb=G(c,r,.42),p1=P(c,r,-.85),p2=P(c,r,.85),wet=G(c,r,.65);
      i.connect(d1);d1.connect(p1);p1.connect(wet);d1.connect(d2);d2.connect(p2);p2.connect(wet);d2.connect(fb);fb.connect(d1);wet.connect(o);
    },
    stadium:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const dry=G(c,r,1);i.connect(dry);dry.connect(o);
      const d=D(c,r,.33),lp=B(c,r,'lowpass',2800,.7),fb=G(c,r,.4),echo=G(c,r,.5);
      i.connect(d);d.connect(lp);lp.connect(echo);echo.connect(o);lp.connect(fb);fb.connect(d);
      const conv=c.createConvolver();conv.buffer=makeIR(c,1.6,3,.5);track(r,conv);const rw=G(c,r,.25);i.connect(conv);conv.connect(rw);rw.connect(o);
    },
    chorus:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const dry=G(c,r,1);i.connect(dry);dry.connect(o);
      [{base:.022,hz:.8,depth:.004,pan:-.6},{base:.031,hz:1.1,depth:.005,pan:.6}].forEach(v=>{
        const d=D(c,r,v.base);LFO(c,r,'sine',v.hz,v.depth,d.delayTime);
        const p=P(c,r,v.pan),w=G(c,r,.55);i.connect(d);d.connect(p);p.connect(w);w.connect(o);
      });
    },
    flanger:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const dry=G(c,r,1);i.connect(dry);dry.connect(o);
      const d=D(c,r,.006);LFO(c,r,'sine',.3,.0028,d.delayTime);
      const fb=G(c,r,.55),wet=G(c,r,.7);i.connect(d);d.connect(wet);wet.connect(o);d.connect(fb);fb.connect(d);
    },
    tremolo:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const a=G(c,r,.55);LFO(c,r,'sine',6,.45,a.gain);i.connect(a);a.connect(o);
    },
    robot:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const hp=B(c,r,'highpass',150,.7),m=G(c,r,0);LFO(c,r,'sine',60,1,m.gain);
      const wet=G(c,r,1.1),dry=G(c,r,.2);
      i.connect(hp);hp.connect(m);m.connect(wet);wet.connect(o);i.connect(dry);dry.connect(o);
    },
    underwater:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const lp=B(c,r,'lowpass',650,.9);LFO(c,r,'sine',.35,220,lp.frequency);
      const d=D(c,r,.012);LFO(c,r,'sine',2.6,.0018,d.delayTime);
      const g=G(c,r,1.15);i.connect(lp);lp.connect(d);d.connect(g);g.connect(o);
    },
    megaphone:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const hp=B(c,r,'highpass',500,.8),pk=B(c,r,'peaking',1600,1,9),w=SHAPE(c,r,driveCurve(5)),lp=B(c,r,'lowpass',4200,.7),g=G(c,r,.45);
      i.connect(hp);hp.connect(pk);pk.connect(w);w.connect(lp);lp.connect(g);g.connect(o);
    },
    overdrive:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const hp=B(c,r,'highpass',90,.7),w=SHAPE(c,r,driveCurve(12),'2x'),tone=B(c,r,'lowpass',5500,.7),g=G(c,r,.25);
      i.connect(hp);hp.connect(w);w.connect(tone);tone.connect(g);g.connect(o);
    },
    lofi:(c,r,i,o)=>{
      const hp=B(c,r,'highpass',200,.7),lp=B(c,r,'lowpass',3400,.8),w=SHAPE(c,r,crushCurve(20)),lp2=B(c,r,'lowpass',4200,.7),g=G(c,r,1.1);
      i.connect(hp);hp.connect(lp);lp.connect(w);w.connect(lp2);lp2.connect(g);g.connect(o);
    }
  };

  /* ---------- real-time pitch shifter (keeps clip length) ----------
     Two crossfaded delay lines whose delay time ramps linearly: ramping the delay down raises the pitch,
     ramping it up lowers it. Pure Web Audio nodes, so preview and offline export are identical. */
  const PS_BUF=.1,PS_FADE=.05;
  function pitchShift(ctx,reg,i,o,ratio){
    const sr=ctx.sampleRate,len=Math.round(PS_BUF*sr),fadeLen=Math.round(PS_FADE*sr);
    const ramp=ctx.createBuffer(1,len,sr),fade=ctx.createBuffer(1,len,sr);
    const rp=ramp.getChannelData(0),fp=fade.getChannelData(0);
    for(let k=0;k<len;k++){rp[k]=k/len;fp[k]=k<fadeLen?Math.sqrt(k/fadeLen):(k>=len-fadeLen?Math.sqrt(1-(k-(len-fadeLen))/fadeLen):1);}
    const up=ratio>1,g=Math.abs(ratio-1)*PS_BUF,t0=ctx.currentTime||0;
    [0,PS_BUF-PS_FADE].forEach(offset=>{
      const d=D(ctx,reg,up?g:0);
      const mod=ctx.createBufferSource();mod.buffer=ramp;mod.loop=true;
      const mg=G(ctx,reg,up?-g:g);mod.connect(mg);mg.connect(d.delayTime);
      const fs=ctx.createBufferSource();fs.buffer=fade;fs.loop=true;
      const mix=G(ctx,reg,0);fs.connect(mix.gain);
      i.connect(d);d.connect(mix);mix.connect(o);
      mod.start(t0+offset);fs.start(t0+offset);reg.oscs.push(mod,fs);
    });
  }
  /* ---------- character voices (pitch layers + formant EQ + colour) ---------- */
  const CHARS={
    child:    {gain:1.4,hp:150,pitch:[{st:7,mix:1}],eq:[['highshelf',3500,.7,3],['lowshelf',200,.7,-6]]},
    boy:      {gain:0.8,hp:100,pitch:[{st:4,mix:1}],eq:[['peaking',2600,1,2],['lowshelf',160,.7,-3]]},
    girl:     {hp:120,pitch:[{st:6,mix:1}],eq:[['highshelf',4200,.7,3],['lowshelf',200,.7,-5]]},
    woman:    {gain:1.25,hp:100,pitch:[{st:3.5,mix:1}],eq:[['highshelf',4000,.7,2],['lowshelf',180,.7,-3]]},
    man:      {gain:1.3,pitch:[{st:-3,mix:1}],eq:[['lowshelf',160,.7,4],['highshelf',6000,.7,-2]]},
    oldman:   {gain:1.2,hp:100,pitch:[{st:-2,mix:1}],eq:[['peaking',1100,1,4],['highshelf',4500,.7,-6]],vib:{hz:5.5,depth:.0013},lp:5000},
    giant:    {gain:0.7,pitch:[{st:-7,mix:1}],eq:[['lowshelf',140,.8,6],['highshelf',5000,.7,-4]],rev:{sec:.9,decay:3,lp:.5,wet:.25,dry:1}},
    monster:  {gain:0.32,pitch:[{st:-9,mix:1},{st:-14,mix:.45}],sat:4,eq:[['lowshelf',120,.8,6],['highshelf',4500,.7,-6]],lp:4500,rev:{sec:.7,decay:3,lp:.5,wet:.15,dry:1}},
    demon:    {gain:0.45,pitch:[{st:-10,mix:1},{st:-5,mix:.5},{st:0,mix:.25}],sat:3,eq:[['lowshelf',130,.8,5]],rev:{sec:2.2,decay:2.6,lp:.4,wet:.4,dry:.9}},
    ghost:    {gain:1.6,hp:250,pitch:[{st:0,mix:.55},{st:12,mix:.3}],vib:{hz:4.5,depth:.0035},trem:{hz:3.2,depth:.3},lp:4800,rev:{sec:4,decay:2.2,lp:.3,wet:.8,dry:.35}},
    alien:    {pitch:[{st:4,mix:1}],ring:{hz:95,mix:.45},vib:{hz:7,depth:.003},eq:[['highshelf',3500,.7,3]]},
    chipmunk: {gain:0.75,hp:250,pitch:[{st:12,mix:1}],eq:[['highshelf',5000,.7,2],['lowshelf',300,.7,-8]]},
    fairy:    {hp:200,pitch:[{st:10,mix:1},{st:0,mix:.15}],vib:{hz:6,depth:.001},eq:[['highshelf',6000,.7,4]],rev:{sec:1.4,decay:3,lp:.7,wet:.3,dry:.9}},
    cartoon:  {gain:0.75,hp:120,pitch:[{st:5,mix:1}],eq:[['peaking',1800,1,4],['lowshelf',200,.7,-4]],vib:{hz:6.5,depth:.0012}},
    goblin:   {gain:0.5,hp:150,pitch:[{st:3,mix:1}],sat:2,eq:[['peaking',1300,1.4,6],['lowshelf',250,.7,-5]],vib:{hz:8,depth:.001}},
    dragon:   {gain:0.55,pitch:[{st:-8,mix:1},{st:-3,mix:.35}],sat:3,eq:[['lowshelf',130,.8,5]],rev:{sec:2.4,decay:2.5,lp:.35,wet:.45,dry:.85}},
    villain:  {gain:0.8,pitch:[{st:-5,mix:1}],sat:1.5,eq:[['lowshelf',150,.8,5],['peaking',3000,1,1.5]],rev:{sec:1.6,decay:3,lp:.45,wet:.3,dry:.9}},
    zombie:   {gain:0.55,hp:120,pitch:[{st:-4,mix:1}],sat:2,eq:[['peaking',700,1,3]],vib:{hz:.9,depth:.006},trem:{hz:5,depth:.25},lp:2800}
  };
  function charVoice(ctx,reg,i,o,s){
    let n=i;
    const chain=node=>{n.connect(node);n=node;return node;};
    if(s.hp)chain(B(ctx,reg,'highpass',s.hp,.7));
    const sum=G(ctx,reg,1);
    (s.pitch||[{st:0,mix:1}]).forEach(L=>{
      const lg=G(ctx,reg,L.mix);
      if(!L.st)n.connect(lg);else pitchShift(ctx,reg,n,lg,Math.pow(2,L.st/12));
      lg.connect(sum);
    });
    n=sum;
    (s.eq||[]).forEach(e=>chain(B(ctx,reg,e[0],e[1],e[2],e[3])));
    if(s.sat)chain(SHAPE(ctx,reg,driveCurve(s.sat),'2x'));
    if(s.ring){
      const m=G(ctx,reg,0);LFO(ctx,reg,'sine',s.ring.hz,1,m.gain);
      const rw=G(ctx,reg,s.ring.mix),rd=G(ctx,reg,1-s.ring.mix*.5),jn=G(ctx,reg,1);
      n.connect(m);m.connect(rw);rw.connect(jn);n.connect(rd);rd.connect(jn);n=jn;
    }
    if(s.vib){const d=D(ctx,reg,.008);LFO(ctx,reg,'sine',s.vib.hz,s.vib.depth,d.delayTime);chain(d);}
    if(s.trem){const a=G(ctx,reg,1-s.trem.depth);LFO(ctx,reg,'sine',s.trem.hz,s.trem.depth,a.gain);chain(a);}
    if(s.lp)chain(B(ctx,reg,'lowpass',s.lp,.7));
    const out=G(ctx,reg,s.gain==null?1:s.gain);
    if(s.rev)reverbSend(ctx,reg,n,out,s.rev);else n.connect(out);
    out.connect(o);
  }
  Object.keys(CHARS).forEach(k=>{FX[k]=(c,r,i,o)=>charVoice(c,r,i,o,CHARS[k]);});

  function voiceStage(ctx,reg,i,o,p){
    let n=i;
    (p.eq||[]).forEach(e=>{const b=B(ctx,reg,e[0],e[1],e[2],e[3]);n.connect(b);n=b;});
    if(p.sat){const s=SHAPE(ctx,reg,driveCurve(p.sat),'2x');n.connect(s);n=s;}
    let boost=Math.max(0,Number(p.gain)||0);(p.eq||[]).forEach(e=>{boost=Math.max(boost,Number(e[3])||0);});
    const trim=p.trim!=null?p.trim:Math.pow(10,-(0.4*boost)/20);
    const t=G(ctx,reg,trim);n.connect(t);t.connect(o);
  }

  window.ethvelleDisposeAudioFx=function(reg){
    if(!reg)return;
    (reg.oscs||[]).forEach(o=>{try{o.stop();}catch(_e){}try{o.disconnect();}catch(_e){}});
    (reg.nodes||[]).forEach(n=>{try{n.disconnect();}catch(_e){}});
    reg.oscs=[];reg.nodes=[];
  };
  /* Connects input -> [voice EQ stage] -> [effect] -> output. Never throws: on any failure the
     signal is simply passed straight through. */
  window.ethvelleBuildAudioFx=function(ctx,input,output,cfg){
    const reg=newReg();
    try{
      const p=window.ethvelleAudioVoiceProfile(cfg&&cfg.voice)||{};
      const eff=String((cfg&&cfg.effect)||'none');
      const hasVoice=!!((p.eq&&p.eq.length)||p.sat||p.trim!=null);
      const hasFx=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(FX,eff);
      if(!hasVoice&&!hasFx){input.connect(output);return reg;}
      const mid=hasVoice&&hasFx?G(ctx,reg,1):null;
      if(hasVoice)voiceStage(ctx,reg,input,mid||output,p);
      if(hasFx)FX[eff](ctx,reg,mid||input,output);
    }catch(err){
      console.warn('Ethvelle audio fx build failed; passing audio straight through.',err);
      window.ethvelleDisposeAudioFx(reg);
      try{input.disconnect();}catch(_e){}
      try{input.connect(output);}catch(_e){}
      return newReg();
    }
    return reg;
  };

  window.__EV_AUDIO_EFFECT_KEYS=['none','echo','studio'].concat(Object.keys(FX));

  /* ---------- live preview hook ----------
     Inserts a pass-through pair of gain nodes between the existing "makeup" node and the fade node,
     then (re)builds the chain whenever the clip's effect/voice changes. */
  const KEY='__evcaV364';
  const prior=window.renderAudioOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof prior==='function'&&!prior.__evcaV364){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=prior.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:[];
        const ctx=(typeof audioOverlayAudioCtx!=='undefined')?audioOverlayAudioCtx:null;
        if(!ctx||typeof audioOverlayNodes==='undefined'||!audioOverlayNodes)return result;
        list.forEach(ov=>{
          if(!ov||ov.type!=='audio')return;
          const nodes=audioOverlayNodes[ov.id];if(!nodes)return;
          const d=nodes.__evcaV66,x=nodes.__evcaV67;if(!d||!x||!x.fade)return;
          let st=nodes[KEY];
          if(!st){
            try{d.makeup.disconnect(x.fade);}catch(_e){return;}
            const fxIn=ctx.createGain(),fxOut=ctx.createGain();
            d.makeup.connect(fxIn);fxOut.connect(x.fade);
            st=nodes[KEY]={fxIn,fxOut,sig:null,reg:null};
          }
          const cfg=window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(ov);
          const sig=String(cfg.effect)+'|'+String(cfg.voice);
          if(st.sig!==sig){
            window.ethvelleDisposeAudioFx(st.reg);
            try{st.fxIn.disconnect();}catch(_e){}
            st.reg=window.ethvelleBuildAudioFx(ctx,st.fxIn,st.fxOut,cfg);
            st.sig=sig;
          }
        });
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle v364 live audio fx:',err);}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evcaV364=true;window.renderAudioOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v374-edge-brush">
/* v374 MODEL EDITOR - "Remove black" brush
   Paint over a thin black border / dark outline on any part and the dark pixels under the brush become
   transparent. Works on the real part images, so the result is what gets saved when you press Done.
   Undo / Redo cover brush strokes too. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV374EdgeBrush) return; window.__ethvelleV374EdgeBrush = true;
  if(typeof ED === 'undefined' || !document.getElementById('sheetEd')) return;

  const $ = id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const B = { on:false, size:20, str:35, mode:'black', scope:'all', pt:null, show:false, bonesBefore:null };
  const FEATHER = 36;                                    // soft edge (in 0-255 brightness) so dark halos fade out instead of leaving a hard ring
  const darkLimit = ()=>30 + B.str*1.6;                  // strength 0-100  ->  "black" means brightest channel <= 30..190
  ED.pxVer = {}; ED.pxPatch = {};                        // per-part brush history: art key -> version / { version -> {x,y,w,h,before,after} }

  /* ---------- UI: tool button + brush panel ---------- */
  if(typeof ED_ICON_PATHS === 'object') ED_ICON_PATHS.eraser = '<path d="m7 21-4.3-4.3c-1-1-1-2.5 0-3.4l9.6-9.6c1-1 2.5-1 3.4 0l5.6 5.6c1 1 1 2.5 0 3.4L13 21"/><path d="M22 21H7"/><path d="m5 11 9 9"/>';
  const tools = document.querySelector('#sheetEd .ed-tools');
  if(tools && !$('edBrushToolBtn')){
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'ed-tool'; btn.id = 'edBrushToolBtn'; btn.type = 'button';
    btn.title = 'Paint over a black border to remove it'; btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');
    btn.innerHTML = '<i data-ic="eraser"></i><span>Remove black</span>';
    btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>setBrush(!B.on));
    const after = tools.querySelector('button[onclick="edResetPart()"]');
    if(after) after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', btn); else tools.appendChild(btn);
    try{ btn.querySelector('i').innerHTML = edIcon('eraser'); }catch(_e){}
  }
  const adj = document.querySelector('#sheetEd .ed-adjust');
  if(adj && !$('edBrushPanel')){
    const p = document.createElement('div'); p.id = 'edBrushPanel';
    p.innerHTML =
      '<div class="ed-brush-head"><button type="button" class="ed-brush-x" id="edBrushClose" aria-label="Close brush" title="Close"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M18 6L6 18M6 6l12 12"/></svg></button></div>' +
      '<div class="ed-modes" id="edBrushMode"><button type="button" data-bm="black">Remove black</button><button type="button" data-bm="all">Erase all</button></div>' +
      '<div class="ed-brush-row"><span>Brush size</span><input type="range" id="edBrushSize" min="4" max="90" value="20"><em id="edBrushSizeVal">20px</em></div>' +
      '<div class="ed-brush-row" id="edBrushStrRow"><span>Black level</span><input type="range" id="edBrushStr" min="0" max="100" value="35"><em id="edBrushStrVal">35%</em></div>' +
      '<div class="ed-modes" id="edBrushScope"><button type="button" data-bs="all">All parts</button><button type="button" data-bs="sel">Selected part only</button></div>';
    adj.appendChild(p);
    p.querySelectorAll('[data-bm]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click', ()=>{ B.mode = b.dataset.bm; syncPanel(); }));
    p.querySelectorAll('[data-bs]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click', ()=>{ B.scope = b.dataset.bs; syncPanel(); }));
    $('edBrushSize').addEventListener('input', e=>{ B.size = +e.target.value; syncPanel(); });
    $('edBrushStr').addEventListener('input', e=>{ B.str = +e.target.value; syncPanel(); });
    $('edBrushClose').addEventListener('click', ()=>setBrush(false));
  }
  function syncPanel(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#edBrushMode [data-bm]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('is-on', b.dataset.bm === B.mode));
    document.querySelectorAll('#edBrushScope [data-bs]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('is-on', b.dataset.bs === B.scope));
    const sv = $('edBrushSizeVal'); if(sv) sv.textContent = B.size + 'px';
    const tv = $('edBrushStrVal'); if(tv) tv.textContent = B.str + '%';
    const row = $('edBrushStrRow'); if(row) row.style.display = B.mode === 'black' ? '' : 'none';
    if(ED.open && ED.g) edDraw();
  }
  function setBrush(on){
    on = !!on; if(on === B.on) return;
    B.on = on; S = null; bp.clear(); pinch = null; B.show = false;
    $('sheetEd').classList.toggle('ed-brush-on', on);
    const btn = $('edBrushToolBtn'); if(btn){ btn.classList.toggle('is-on', on); btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on ? 'true' : 'false'); }
    if(on){ B.bonesBefore = ED.bones; ED.bones = false; syncPanel(); try{ showToast('Paint over the black border to remove it', { duration:1800 }); }catch(_e){} }
    else { if(B.bonesBefore != null) ED.bones = B.bonesBefore; B.bonesBefore = null; }
    if(ED.open){ edSyncUI(); edDraw(); }
  }

  /* ---------- pixel work ---------- */
  let S = null;                                          // the stroke being painted
  const bp = new Map(); let pinch = null;                // pointers on the canvas / two-finger pan-zoom state
  const pos = e=>{ const r = ED.cv.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x:e.clientX-r.left, y:e.clientY-r.top }; };

  function buildTargets(){
    const W = edWorld(), list = B.scope === 'sel' ? (ED.sel ? [ED.sel] : []) : ED.order, seen = {}, out = [];
    list.forEach(k=>{
      if(!W[k] || !ED.st[k] || !edKeyVisible(k) || edIsLocked(k)) return;
      const ak = ED.assign[k], art = ED.arts[ak]; if(!art || !art.canvas || seen[ak]) return; seen[ak] = 1;
      const w = W[k], st = ED.st[k];
      out.push({ k, ak, art, st, w, c:Math.cos(w.rot), s:Math.sin(w.rot), sx:w.sx, sy:w.sy, ctx:null, cur:null, orig:null, changed:false, x0:1e9, y0:1e9, x1:-1, y1:-1 });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function load(T){
    if(T.cur) return;
    T.ctx = T.art.canvas.getContext('2d');
    T.cur = T.ctx.getImageData(0, 0, T.art.w, T.art.h);
    T.orig = new Uint8ClampedArray(T.cur.data);          // pixels as they were when the stroke began - every dab is computed from these, so overlapping dabs never compound
  }
  function dab(T, fx, fy, r){
    const dx = fx-T.w.x, dy = fy-T.w.y;
    const lx = T.st.pvx + (dx*T.c + dy*T.s)/T.sx, ly = T.st.pvy + (-dx*T.s + dy*T.c)/T.sy;   // brush centre in the part's own pixels
    const rx = r/Math.abs(T.sx), ry = r/Math.abs(T.sy), aw = T.art.w, ah = T.art.h;
    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ly-ry)), y1 = Math.min(ah-1, Math.ceil(ly+ry));
    const bx0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(lx-rx)), bx1 = Math.min(aw-1, Math.ceil(lx+rx));
    if(y1 < y0 || bx1 < bx0) return false;
    load(T);
    const d = T.cur.data, o = T.orig, all = B.mode === 'all', t = darkLimit();
    let hit = false, ex0 = 1e9, ex1 = -1, ey0 = 1e9, ey1 = -1;
    for(let y = y0; y <= y1; y++){
      const ty = (y+.5-ly)/ry, q = 1-ty*ty; if(q < 0) continue;
      const half = rx*Math.sqrt(q), xs = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(lx-half-.5)), xe = Math.min(aw-1, Math.floor(lx+half-.5));
      for(let x = xs; x <= xe; x++){
        const i = (y*aw+x)*4, a = o[i+3]; if(!a) continue;
        let rem = 1;
        if(!all){ const m = Math.max(o[i], o[i+1], o[i+2]); rem = m <= t ? 1 : m >= t+FEATHER ? 0 : 1-(m-t)/FEATHER; }
        if(rem <= 0) continue;
        let na = Math.round(a*(1-rem)); if(na < 10) na = 0;
        if(na < d[i+3]){ d[i+3] = na; hit = true; if(x<ex0)ex0=x; if(x>ex1)ex1=x; if(y<ey0)ey0=y; if(y>ey1)ey1=y; }
      }
    }
    if(!hit) return false;
    T.ctx.putImageData(T.cur, 0, 0, ex0, ey0, ex1-ex0+1, ey1-ey0+1);
    T.changed = true; T.x0 = Math.min(T.x0, ex0); T.y0 = Math.min(T.y0, ey0); T.x1 = Math.max(T.x1, ex1); T.y1 = Math.max(T.y1, ey1);
    return true;
  }
  function addPoint(p){
    if(!S) return;
    const r = (B.size/2)/ED.cam.z, sp = Math.max(1, B.size*0.2), pts = [];
    if(!S.last) pts.push(p);
    else { const dx = p.x-S.last.x, dy = p.y-S.last.y, n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(dx,dy)/sp)); for(let i = 1; i <= n; i++) pts.push({ x:S.last.x+dx*i/n, y:S.last.y+dy*i/n }); }
    try{
      pts.forEach(q=>{ const fx = (q.x-ED.cam.x)/ED.cam.z, fy = (q.y-ED.cam.y)/ED.cam.z; S.targets.forEach(T=>dab(T, fx, fy, r)); });
    }catch(err){
      console.error('Ethvelle brush:', err); cancelStroke();
      try{ showToast("This part's image can't be edited here", { duration:2200 }); }catch(_e){}
      return;
    }
    S.last = p; edDraw();
  }
  function startStroke(){
    const targets = buildTargets();
    if(!targets.length){
      if(B.scope === 'sel'){ try{ showToast('Select a part first, or switch to All parts', { duration:1800 }); }catch(_e){} }
      S = { targets:[], last:null }; return;
    }
    S = { targets, last:null };
  }
  function cancelStroke(){
    if(!S) return;
    S.targets.forEach(T=>{ if(T.changed && T.ctx) T.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(new Uint8ClampedArray(T.orig), T.art.w, T.art.h), 0, 0); });
    S = null; edDraw();
  }
  function refreshArt(art, x, y, img){
    art.src = null; art.thumb = null;                    // forces Done to re-encode this part from the cleaned canvas
    if(art.alpha) for(let row = 0; row < img.height; row++) for(let col = 0; col < img.width; col++) art.alpha[(y+row)*art.w + x+col] = img.data[(row*img.width+col)*4+3] > 30 ? 1 : 0;
  }
  function commit(){
    const st = S; S = null; if(!st) return;
    const ts = st.targets.filter(T=>T.changed); if(!ts.length) return;
    edPush();                                            // snapshot of the state BEFORE this stroke (tagged with the brush versions below)
    ts.forEach(T=>{
      const x = T.x0, y = T.y0, w = T.x1-T.x0+1, h = T.y1-T.y0+1, aw = T.art.w;
      const before = T.ctx.createImageData(w, h), after = T.ctx.getImageData(x, y, w, h);
      for(let row = 0; row < h; row++){ const so = ((y+row)*aw+x)*4; before.data.set(T.orig.subarray(so, so+w*4), row*w*4); }
      const v = (ED.pxVer[T.ak]||0)+1;
      (ED.pxPatch[T.ak] = ED.pxPatch[T.ak] || {})[v] = { x, y, w, h, before, after };
      ED.pxVer[T.ak] = v;
      refreshArt(T.art, x, y, after);
    });
    edAfterChange(true);
  }

  /* ---------- undo / redo: keep brush versions in step with the editor's own history ---------- */
  const tag = (j, ver)=>{ try{ return j.slice(0, -1) + ',"px":' + JSON.stringify(ver || {}) + '}'; }catch(_e){ return j; } };
  const readPx = j=>{ try{ return JSON.parse(j).px || null; }catch(_e){ return null; } };
  function applyPx(target){
    if(!target) return;
    const keys = new Set(Object.keys(target).concat(Object.keys(ED.pxVer)));
    keys.forEach(ak=>{
      const art = ED.arts[ak]; if(!art || !art.canvas) return;
      const ctx = art.canvas.getContext('2d'), patches = ED.pxPatch[ak] || {}, t = target[ak] || 0; let c = ED.pxVer[ak] || 0;
      while(c > t){ const p = patches[c]; if(p){ ctx.putImageData(p.before, p.x, p.y); refreshArt(art, p.x, p.y, p.before); } c--; }
      while(c < t){ const p = patches[c+1]; if(p){ ctx.putImageData(p.after, p.x, p.y); refreshArt(art, p.x, p.y, p.after); } c++; }
      if(c) ED.pxVer[ak] = c; else delete ED.pxVer[ak];
    });
  }
  const oPush = window.edPush, oUndo = window.edUndo, oRedo = window.edRedo;
  window.edPush = function(){
    const r = oPush.apply(this, arguments);
    const i = ED.undo.length-1; if(i >= 0) ED.undo[i] = tag(ED.undo[i], ED.pxVer);
    return r;
  };
  window.edUndo = function(){
    if(!ED.undo.length) return oUndo.apply(this, arguments);
    const cur = Object.assign({}, ED.pxVer), target = readPx(ED.undo[ED.undo.length-1]);
    const r = oUndo.apply(this, arguments);
    if(ED.redo.length) ED.redo[ED.redo.length-1] = tag(ED.redo[ED.redo.length-1], cur);
    if(target){ applyPx(target); edAfterChange(true); }
    return r;
  };
  window.edRedo = function(){
    if(!ED.redo.length) return oRedo.apply(this, arguments);
    const cur = Object.assign({}, ED.pxVer), target = readPx(ED.redo[ED.redo.length-1]);
    const r = oRedo.apply(this, arguments);
    if(ED.undo.length) ED.undo[ED.undo.length-1] = tag(ED.undo[ED.undo.length-1], cur);
    if(target){ applyPx(target); edAfterChange(true); }
    return r;
  };

  /* ---------- canvas gestures (only while the brush is on) ---------- */
  function bDown(e){
    try{ ED.cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); }catch(_e){}
    const p = pos(e); bp.set(e.pointerId, p); B.pt = p; B.show = true;
    if(e.pointerType === 'mouse' && (e.button === 1 || e.button === 2)){ cancelStroke(); pinch = { type:'pan', p0:p, vx:ED.cam.x, vy:ED.cam.y }; return; }
    if(bp.size >= 2){
      cancelStroke(); const [a, b] = [...bp.values()];
      pinch = { type:'pinch', d:Math.hypot(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y)||1, z:ED.cam.z, c:{ x:(a.x+b.x)/2, y:(a.y+b.y)/2 }, vx:ED.cam.x, vy:ED.cam.y }; return;
    }
    if(pinch) return;
    startStroke(); addPoint(p);
  }
  function bMove(e){
    const p = pos(e);
    if(!bp.has(e.pointerId)){ if(e.pointerType === 'mouse'){ B.pt = p; B.show = true; edDraw(); } return; }
    bp.set(e.pointerId, p); B.pt = p;
    if(pinch){
      if(pinch.type === 'pan'){ ED.cam.x = pinch.vx+p.x-pinch.p0.x; ED.cam.y = pinch.vy+p.y-pinch.p0.y; edDraw(); return; }
      if(bp.size < 2) return;
      const [a, b] = [...bp.values()], d = Math.hypot(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y), c = { x:(a.x+b.x)/2, y:(a.y+b.y)/2 };
      const z = Math.max(0.08, Math.min(8, pinch.z*d/pinch.d)), k = z/pinch.z;
      ED.cam.z = z; ED.cam.x = c.x-(pinch.c.x-pinch.vx)*k; ED.cam.y = c.y-(pinch.c.y-pinch.vy)*k; edSyncZoom(); edDraw(); return;
    }
    if(S) addPoint(p);
  }
  function bUp(e){
    bp.delete(e.pointerId);
    if(!bp.size){ commit(); pinch = null; if(e.pointerType !== 'mouse') B.show = false; edDraw(); }
  }
  function hookCanvas(){
    const cv = ED.cv; if(!cv || cv.__v374) return; cv.__v374 = true;
    const oD = cv.onpointerdown, oM = cv.onpointermove, oU = cv.onpointerup;
    cv.onpointerdown = function(e){ return B.on ? bDown(e) : (oD && oD.call(this, e)); };
    cv.onpointermove = function(e){ return (B.on || bp.size) ? bMove(e) : (oM && oM.call(this, e)); };
    cv.onpointerup = cv.onpointercancel = function(e){ return (B.on || bp.has(e.pointerId)) ? bUp(e) : (oU && oU.call(this, e)); };
    cv.addEventListener('pointerleave', ()=>{ if(B.on && !bp.size){ B.show = false; edDraw(); } });
  }

  /* ---------- brush ring + lifecycle hooks ---------- */
  const oDraw = window.edDraw;
  window.edDraw = function(){
    const r = oDraw.apply(this, arguments);
    if(B.on && B.pt && B.show && ED.g){
      const g = ED.g; g.save(); g.setTransform(ED.dpr, 0, 0, ED.dpr, 0, 0);
      g.beginPath(); g.arc(B.pt.x, B.pt.y, B.size/2, 0, Math.PI*2);
      g.lineWidth = 2.5; g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.6)'; g.stroke(); g.lineWidth = 1.25; g.strokeStyle = '#fff'; g.stroke(); g.restore();
    }
    return r;
  };
  const oOpen = window.edOpen;
  window.edOpen = function(){
    if(B.on) setBrush(false);
    const r = oOpen.apply(this, arguments);
    ED.pxVer = {}; ED.pxPatch = {}; S = null; bp.clear(); pinch = null;
    hookCanvas(); syncPanel();
    return r;
  };
  const oClose = window.edClose;
  window.edClose = function(){ if(B.on) setBrush(false); return oClose.apply(this, arguments); };
  if(ED.cv) hookCanvas();
  syncPanel();
})();
</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v391-requested-ui-polish-css">
  /* Frame Capture: keep the three real buttons visibly pressable instead of flat panels. */
  html body #screen-editor #evcaFrameCaptureSheet button.evca-frame-action{
