<script id="ethvelle-v392-followup-polish-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';

  /* Solid-colour presets use the same real Background clip pipeline as uploaded images. */
  window.ethvelleAddSolidBackground=function(color,label){
    const value=String(color||'').trim();
    if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(value))return;
    if(typeof window.addBackgroundClip==='function')window.addBackgroundClip(value);
    else if(typeof addBackgroundClip==='function')addBackgroundClip(value);
    else {if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Background is not ready yet',{duration:1500});return;}
    if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast((String(label||'Solid colour'))+' background added',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.check,duration:1300});
  };

  /* Profile: approved plans shown in a horizontal, left-right looping carousel. */
  let planNames=[];
  let planResizeObserver=null;
  function getPlanNames(){
    let names=[];
    try{
      const owned=(typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser&&typeof getMyOwnedPlansForBilling==='function')?getMyOwnedPlansForBilling():[];
      names=[...new Set((owned||[]).filter(p=>p&&Number(p.price)>0)
        .map(p=>String(p.name||p.id||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
    }catch(_e){}
    if(!names.length){
      try{names=[String((typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser&&typeof getMyActivePlanName==='function')?getMyActivePlanName():'Free').trim()||'Free'];}
      catch(_e){names=['Free'];}
    }
    return names;
  }
  function sizePlanCarousel(){
    const el=document.getElementById('profileStatPlan');
    const viewport=el&&el.querySelector('.ev-v393-plan-window');
    const track=viewport&&viewport.querySelector('.ev-v393-plan-track');
    if(!viewport||!track)return;
    track.style.animation='none';
    track.style.removeProperty('--ev-v393-distance');
    const distance=Math.max(0,track.scrollWidth-viewport.clientWidth);
    if(distance>3&&planNames.length>1){
      track.style.setProperty('--ev-v393-distance',(-distance)+'px');
      track.style.animationDuration=Math.max(4,Math.min(12,distance/20))+'s';
      track.style.animation='evV393PlanCarousel var(--ev-v393-duration, 6s) ease-in-out infinite alternate';
      track.style.setProperty('--ev-v393-duration',Math.max(4,Math.min(12,distance/20))+'s');
      track.classList.add('is-moving');
    }else{
      track.classList.remove('is-moving');
      track.style.animation='none';
      track.style.justifyContent='center';
    }
  }
  function restartPlanCarousel(){
    const el=document.getElementById('profileStatPlan');if(!el)return;
    planNames=getPlanNames();
    if(planResizeObserver){try{planResizeObserver.disconnect();}catch(_e){}}
    el.classList.remove('ev-v392-plan-slide');
    const viewport=document.createElement('span');viewport.className='ev-v393-plan-window';
    const track=document.createElement('span');track.className='ev-v393-plan-track';
    planNames.forEach(function(name,index){
      if(index){
        const plus=document.createElement('span');plus.className='ev-v393-plan-plus';plus.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
        plus.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.8" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/></svg>';
        track.appendChild(plus);
      }
      const item=document.createElement('span');item.className='ev-v393-plan-item';item.textContent=name;track.appendChild(item);
    });
    viewport.appendChild(track);el.replaceChildren(viewport);
    const measure=function(){sizePlanCarousel();};
    requestAnimationFrame(measure);
    if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
      planResizeObserver=new ResizeObserver(measure);
      planResizeObserver.observe(viewport);
    }else window.addEventListener('resize',measure,{passive:true});
  }
  const oldUpdateProfile=window.updateProfileUI;
  if(typeof oldUpdateProfile==='function'&&!oldUpdateProfile.__evV393PlanCarousel){
    const wrappedUpdateProfile=function(){
      const result=oldUpdateProfile.apply(this,arguments);
      restartPlanCarousel();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedUpdateProfile.__evV393PlanCarousel=true;
    window.updateProfileUI=wrappedUpdateProfile;
  }
  restartPlanCarousel();

  /* Normalize long dash punctuation in dynamic admin-published notification copy too. */
  function cleanNotificationText(value){
    return String(value==null?'':value)
      .replace(/\s*[\u2013\u2014\u2015\u2E3A\u2E3B]\s*/g,' - ')
      .replace(/\s*-{3,}\s*/g,' - ');
  }
  /* Apply the same copy cleanup to notification inbox cards as well as pop-up notices. */
  const previousVisibleNotifications=window.visibleNotifications;
  if(typeof previousVisibleNotifications==='function'&&!previousVisibleNotifications.__evV392DashCopy){
    const wrappedVisibleNotifications=function(){
      return previousVisibleNotifications.apply(this,arguments).map(function(item){
        const copy=Object.assign({},item||{});
        ['title','subject','body','message','description'].forEach(function(key){
          if(copy[key]!=null)copy[key]=cleanNotificationText(copy[key]);
        });
        return copy;
      });
    };
    wrappedVisibleNotifications.__evV392DashCopy=true;
    window.visibleNotifications=wrappedVisibleNotifications;
  }

  const previousNotificationShow=window.maybeShowLocationNotification;
  if(typeof previousNotificationShow==='function'&&!previousNotificationShow.__evV392DashCopy){
    const wrappedNotificationShow=function(){
      const result=previousNotificationShow.apply(this,arguments);
      const title=document.getElementById('notificationPopupTitle');
      const body=document.getElementById('notificationPopupBody');
      const backdrop=document.getElementById('notificationPopupBackdrop');
      if(backdrop&&backdrop.classList.contains('active')){
        if(title)title.textContent=cleanNotificationText(title.textContent);
        if(body)body.textContent=cleanNotificationText(body.textContent);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedNotificationShow.__evV392DashCopy=true;
    window.maybeShowLocationNotification=wrappedNotificationShow;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v393-requested-ui-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';

  /* Keep the homepage capsule synchronized with the live balance, never with plan names. */
  const originalCreditRender=window.renderHomeCreditSummary;
  function fitCreditText(){
    const button=document.getElementById('homeAccountSummary');
    const balance=document.getElementById('homeAccountBalance');
    if(!button||!balance)return;
    let size=18;
    balance.style.fontSize=size+'px';
    const available=Math.max(20,balance.clientWidth||button.clientWidth-18);
    while(size>8.5&&balance.scrollWidth>available){size-=.5;balance.style.fontSize=size+'px';}
    if(balance.scrollWidth<=available&&size<18){
      while(size<18){
        const next=Math.min(18,size+.5);balance.style.fontSize=next+'px';
        if(balance.scrollWidth>available){balance.style.fontSize=size+'px';break;}
        size=next;
      }
    }
  }
  function applyCreditsOnly(){
    const plan=document.getElementById('homeAccountPlan');
    const balance=document.getElementById('homeAccountBalance');
    const button=document.getElementById('homeAccountSummary');
    if(plan)plan.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
    if(!balance||!button)return;
    const signedIn=!!(typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser);
    if(!signedIn){
      balance.textContent='Sign in for credits';
      button.setAttribute('aria-label','Sign in to view credits');
    }else{
      const amount=(window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading)?'...':Math.max(0,Number(typeof myCreditsBalance!=='undefined'?myCreditsBalance:0)||0).toLocaleString();
      balance.innerHTML='<span class="home-credit-available">'+amount+'</span><span class="home-credit-unit"> credits</span>';
      button.setAttribute('aria-label',amount+' credits available. Open billing and plan');
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(fitCreditText);
  }
  if(typeof originalCreditRender==='function'&&!originalCreditRender.__evV393CreditsOnly){
    const wrappedCreditRender=function(){
      const result=originalCreditRender.apply(this,arguments);
      applyCreditsOnly();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedCreditRender.__evV393CreditsOnly=true;
    window.renderHomeCreditSummary=wrappedCreditRender;
  }
  applyCreditsOnly();
  window.addEventListener('resize',fitCreditText,{passive:true});
  if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
    const homeBalance=document.getElementById('homeAccountBalance');
    if(homeBalance){const ro=new ResizeObserver(fitCreditText);ro.observe(homeBalance);}
  }

  /* Frame Capture: show the blue selection edge on the tile being pressed. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(event){
    const button=event.target&&event.target.closest?event.target.closest('#evcaFrameCaptureSheet .evca-frame-action'):null;
    if(!button)return;
    const sheet=button.closest('#evcaFrameCaptureSheet');
    if(sheet)sheet.querySelectorAll('.evca-frame-action').forEach(function(item){
      item.classList.remove('ev-v393-selected');item.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');
    });
    button.classList.add('ev-v393-selected');button.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');
  },true);
  const oldOpenFrame=window.openEthvelleFrameCapture;
  if(typeof oldOpenFrame==='function'&&!oldOpenFrame.__evV393FrameSelection){
    const wrappedOpenFrame=function(){
      const sheet=document.getElementById('evcaFrameCaptureSheet');
      if(sheet)sheet.querySelectorAll('.evca-frame-action').forEach(function(item){item.classList.remove('ev-v393-selected');item.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');});
      return oldOpenFrame.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedOpenFrame.__evV393FrameSelection=true;
    window.openEthvelleFrameCapture=wrappedOpenFrame;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v394-requested-ui-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* The current wallet stores the live available balance. The purchased figure is the
     combined credit allowance of the user's approved paid plans (the source has no separate
     one-time top-up wallet field, so do not invent a second balance). */
  function creditFigures(){
    let available=0,purchased=0;
    try{available=Math.max(0,Number(typeof myCreditsBalance!=='undefined'?myCreditsBalance:0)||0);}catch(_e){}
    try{
      const owned=(typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser&&typeof getMyOwnedPlansForBilling==='function')?getMyOwnedPlansForBilling():[];
      purchased=typeof getMyCombinedCreditAllowance==='function'
        ?Math.max(0,Number(getMyCombinedCreditAllowance(owned))||0):0;
    }catch(_e){}
    return {available,purchased};
  }
  function paintHomeCreditPair(){
    const plan=$('homeAccountPlan'),balance=$('homeAccountBalance'),button=$('homeAccountSummary');
    if(plan)plan.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
    if(!balance||!button)return;
    const signedIn=!!(typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser);
    if(!signedIn){
      balance.classList.remove('ev-v394-credit-loading');
      balance.textContent='Sign in for credits';
      button.setAttribute('aria-label','Sign in to view available and purchased credits');
      return;
    }
    if(window.__ethvelleCreditsLoading){
      balance.classList.add('ev-v394-credit-loading');
      balance.textContent='Loading credits';
      button.setAttribute('aria-label','Loading available and purchased credits');
      return;
    }
    balance.classList.remove('ev-v394-credit-loading');
    const values=creditFigures();
    balance.innerHTML='<span class="ev-v394-credit-pair">'
      +'<b class="ev-v394-available-value">'+values.available.toLocaleString()+'</b>'
      +'<span class="ev-v394-credit-slash" aria-hidden="true">/</span>'
      +'<b class="ev-v394-purchased-value">'+values.purchased.toLocaleString()+'</b>'
      +'<span class="ev-v394-credit-unit">credits</span>'
      +'</span>';
    balance.title='Available credits / Purchased plan credits';
    button.setAttribute('aria-label',values.available.toLocaleString()+' available credits / '+values.purchased.toLocaleString()+' purchased plan credits. Open billing and plan');
    fitNumbers();
  }
  function fitNumbers(){
    const balance=$('homeAccountBalance');if(!balance)return;
    const pair=balance.querySelector('.ev-v394-credit-pair');if(!pair)return;
    let size=12;pair.style.fontSize=size+'px';
    const width=Math.max(40,balance.clientWidth||120);
    while(size>8&&pair.scrollWidth>width){size-=.5;pair.style.fontSize=size+'px';}
  }
  const previousHomeRender=window.renderHomeCreditSummary;
  if(typeof previousHomeRender==='function'&&!previousHomeRender.__evV394CreditPair){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=previousHomeRender.apply(this,arguments);
      paintHomeCreditPair();
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV394CreditPair=true;
    window.renderHomeCreditSummary=wrapped;
  }
  paintHomeCreditPair();
  window.addEventListener('resize',fitNumbers,{passive:true});
  if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
    const account=$('homeAccountSummary');
    if(account){const creditObserver=new ResizeObserver(fitNumbers);creditObserver.observe(account);}
  }

  /* One-at-a-time plan carousel. No plus sign or simultaneous plan names. */
  let planNames=[],planIndex=0,planTimer=0,planAnimationTimer=0;
  function collectPlanNames(){
    let names=[];
    try{
      const owned=(typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser&&typeof getMyOwnedPlansForBilling==='function')?getMyOwnedPlansForBilling():[];
      names=[...new Set((owned||[]).filter(p=>p&&Number(p.price)>0)
        .map(p=>String(p.name||p.id||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
    }catch(_e){}
    if(!names.length){
      try{names=[String((typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser&&typeof getMyActivePlanName==='function')?getMyActivePlanName():'Free').trim()||'Free'];}
      catch(_e){names=['Free'];}
    }
    return names;
  }
  function clearPlanTimers(){
    if(planTimer)clearTimeout(planTimer);
    if(planAnimationTimer)clearTimeout(planAnimationTimer);
    planTimer=0;planAnimationTimer=0;
  }
  function scheduleNextPlan(){
    if(planTimer)clearTimeout(planTimer);
    if(planNames.length<2)return;
    planTimer=setTimeout(advancePlan,2600);
  }
  function advancePlan(){
    const el=$('profileStatPlan');if(!el||planNames.length<2)return;
    el.classList.remove('ev-v394-plan-in','ev-v394-plan-out');
    void el.offsetWidth;
    el.classList.add('ev-v394-plan-out');
    planAnimationTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      planIndex=(planIndex+1)%planNames.length;
      el.textContent=planNames[planIndex];
      el.classList.remove('ev-v394-plan-out');
      void el.offsetWidth;
      el.classList.add('ev-v394-plan-in');
      planAnimationTimer=setTimeout(function(){
        el.classList.remove('ev-v394-plan-in');
        scheduleNextPlan();
      },340);
    },270);
  }
  function refreshPlanCarousel(force){
    const el=$('profileStatPlan');if(!el)return;
    const next=collectPlanNames();
    if(!force&&JSON.stringify(next)===JSON.stringify(planNames))return;
    clearPlanTimers();planNames=next;planIndex=0;
    el.classList.remove('ev-v392-plan-slide','ev-v393-plan-window','ev-v394-plan-out','ev-v394-plan-in');
    el.textContent=planNames[0]||'Free';
    scheduleNextPlan();
  }
  const previousUpdateProfile=window.updateProfileUI;
  if(typeof previousUpdateProfile==='function'&&!previousUpdateProfile.__evV394SinglePlan){
    const wrappedUpdateProfile=function(){
      const result=previousUpdateProfile.apply(this,arguments);
      /* The previous v393 renderer rebuilds its multi-name carousel on each profile update.
         Force a clean one-name render after it so no old plus separator can reappear. */
      refreshPlanCarousel(true);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedUpdateProfile.__evV394SinglePlan=true;
    window.updateProfileUI=wrappedUpdateProfile;
  }
  refreshPlanCarousel(true);

  /* Frame Capture action tiles retain their active blue outline while pressed/selected. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(event){
    const button=event.target&&event.target.closest?event.target.closest('#evcaFrameCaptureSheet .evca-frame-action'):null;
    if(!button)return;
    const sheet=button.closest('#evcaFrameCaptureSheet');
    if(sheet)sheet.querySelectorAll('.evca-frame-action').forEach(function(item){
      item.classList.remove('ev-v393-selected','ev-v394-selected');
      item.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');
    });
    button.classList.add('ev-v394-selected');
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');
  },true);
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v399-requested-ui-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  var LOW_CREDITS=100; /* available credits below this (including 0) = compact capsule */
  function syncCreditCapsule(){
    var button=document.getElementById('homeAccountSummary');
    var balance=document.getElementById('homeAccountBalance');
    if(!button||!balance)return;
    var value=balance.querySelector('.ev-v394-available-value');
    var compact=false;
    if(value){
      var n=parseInt(String(value.textContent).replace(/[^0-9]/g,''),10);
      compact=(isNaN(n)?0:n)<LOW_CREDITS;
    }
    button.classList.toggle('ev-v399-credit-compact',compact);
  }
  var balanceEl=document.getElementById('homeAccountBalance');
  if(balanceEl&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    new MutationObserver(syncCreditCapsule).observe(balanceEl,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});
  }
  syncCreditCapsule();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v421-tap-select">
/* v421: a timeline clip is selected ONLY by a plain tap. Touching a clip while sliding the timeline left/right
   (or scrolling it, or dragging) no longer moves the white selection border onto that clip. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  var CLIP='#timelineInner .anim-clip, #timelineInner .overlay-clip, #timelineInner .track-clip';
  var SKIP='.kf-marker,[data-ov-handle],[data-bg-handle],[data-seg-handle],[data-main-handle],.anim-clip-handle,.sel-handle';
  var SLOP=9, MAX_TAP_MS=900;
  var deferring=false, pending=null, gesture=null, base=null;
  function install(){
    var orig=window.selectClipForChrome;
    if(typeof orig!=='function'||orig.__ev421){return false;}
    base=orig;
    var wrapped=function(which){
      if(deferring&&gesture&&String(which)!==String(window.selectedClip!=null?window.selectedClip:(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:''))){
        pending=which;           // remember it; commit only if this turns out to be a tap
        return;
      }
      return base.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__ev421=true;
    window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('selectClipForChrome=window.selectClipForChrome');}catch(_e){}
    return true;
  }
  function rowsSnapshot(){
    return Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.layer-row.selected'));
  }
  function restoreRows(snap){
    try{
      document.querySelectorAll('.layer-row.selected').forEach(function(r){if(snap.indexOf(r)<0)r.classList.remove('selected');});
      snap.forEach(function(r){if(r.isConnected)r.classList.add('selected');});
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    pending=null;gesture=null;
    var t=e.target;
    if(!t||!t.closest)return;
    if(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0)return;
    if(t.closest(SKIP))return;
    var clip=t.closest(CLIP);
    if(!clip)return;
    if(!base&&!install())return;
    if(clip.classList.contains('selected'))return;       // already selected: nothing to defer
    gesture={id:e.pointerId,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,t:Date.now(),rows:rowsSnapshot(),sel:(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:null),moved:false};
    deferring=true;
    setTimeout(function(){deferring=false;},0);           // only the synchronous pointerdown handlers are deferred
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){
    if(!gesture||e.pointerId!==gesture.id)return;
    if(Math.abs(e.clientX-gesture.x)>SLOP||Math.abs(e.clientY-gesture.y)>SLOP)gesture.moved=true;
  },true);
  function finish(e,cancelled){
    var g=gesture;
    if(!g||e.pointerId!==g.id)return;
    gesture=null;
    var which=pending;pending=null;
    var tap=!cancelled&&!g.moved&&(Date.now()-g.t)<=MAX_TAP_MS&&
      Math.abs(e.clientX-g.x)<=SLOP&&Math.abs(e.clientY-g.y)<=SLOP;
    if(which==null){return;}
    if(tap){
      setTimeout(function(){try{base(which);}catch(_e){}},0);
    }else{
      // a slide / scroll / drag: the old selection (and its row highlight) stays exactly where it was
      setTimeout(function(){if(String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:'')===String(g.sel))restoreRows(g.rows);},0);
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',function(e){finish(e,false);},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',function(e){finish(e,true);},true);
  window.addEventListener('load',function(){install();});
  install();
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v422-scene-effect-selection-frame">
/* v422: the white selection border of a scene effect clip (Rain, Storm, Fog, Blur ...) sat in the wrong place.
   The effect is DRAWN inside the source clip's own contain-rectangle (sourceWidth/sourceHeight, minus crop) at the
   keyed / tracked / animated transform, but the frame was measured from the invisible 512x512 placeholder image and
   ignored tracking and animation. Now the frame is built from exactly the same rectangle and transform the effect uses. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV422SceneFxFrame)return;
  const list=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const selId=()=>{try{return String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');}catch(_e){return '';}};
  const isSceneFx=o=>!!(o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&o.ethvelleEffectKind!=='character'&&o.type==='image');
  const selected=()=>{const id=selId();return list().find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id&&isSceneFx(o))||null;};
  const num=(v,d)=>{v=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(v)?v:d;};
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  function nowT(){try{return Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;}catch(_e){return 0;}}

  /* the rectangle (stage px) the effect is painted into: same maths as drawOverlaySceneFx */
  function effectRect(ov){
    const W=Number(stageW)||0,H=Number(stageH)||0;if(!(W>0&&H>0))return null;
    let iw=num(ov.sourceWidth,0),ih=num(ov.sourceHeight,0);
    try{
      const media=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)]:null;
      if(media){iw=iw||num(media.videoWidth||media.naturalWidth||media.width,0);ih=ih||num(media.videoHeight||media.naturalHeight||media.height,0);}
    }catch(_e){}
    let cw=W,ch=H;
    if(iw>0&&ih>0){const fit=Math.min(W/iw,H/ih);cw=iw*fit;ch=ih*fit;}
    const cx=(W-cw)/2,cy=(H-ch)/2;
    let crop={left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
    try{crop=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(ov.crop):(ov.crop||crop)||crop;}catch(_e){}
    const l=clamp(num(crop.left,0),0,.92),r=clamp(num(crop.right,0),0,.92),t=clamp(num(crop.top,0),0,.92),b=clamp(num(crop.bottom,0),0,.92);
    const vx=cx+l*cw,vy=cy+t*ch,vw=Math.max(1,cw*(1-l-r)),vh=Math.max(1,ch*(1-t-b));
    return {W,H,vx,vy,vw,vh};
  }

  function buildTarget(ov,prev){
    const rect=effectRect(ov);if(!rect)return prev;
    const t=nowT();
    const base=(prev&&prev.obj)||ov.transform;if(!base)return prev;
    let trk=null,mo=null;
    try{trk=typeof window.ethvelleTrackingDelta==='function'?window.ethvelleTrackingDelta(ov,t):null;}catch(_e){}
    try{mo=typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,t):null;}catch(_e){}
    trk=trk||{ds:1,dx:0,dy:0,dr:0};
    mo=mo||{};
    const ms=Math.max(.0001,num(mo.scale,1)),mx=num(mo.x,0),my=num(mo.y,0),mr=num(mo.rotation,0);
    const ds=Math.max(.0001,trk.ds*ms),dx=trk.dx+mx,dy=trk.dy+my,dr=trk.dr+mr;
    let obj=base;
    if(Math.abs(ds-1)>1e-6||Math.abs(dx)>1e-6||Math.abs(dy)>1e-6||Math.abs(dr)>1e-6){
      /* show where the effect is really drawn (authored + tracked/animated delta); edits write back only the authored part */
      obj={};
      Object.defineProperties(obj,{
        scale:{enumerable:true,get:()=>num(base.scale,1)*ds,set:v=>{base.scale=Math.max(.0001,num(v,1)/ds);}},
        offsetX:{enumerable:true,get:()=>num(base.offsetX,0)+dx,set:v=>{base.offsetX=num(v,0)-dx;}},
        offsetY:{enumerable:true,get:()=>num(base.offsetY,0)+dy,set:v=>{base.offsetY=num(v,0)-dy;}},
        rotation:{enumerable:true,get:()=>num(base.rotation,0)+dr,set:v=>{base.rotation=num(v,0)-dr;}}
      });
    }
    return {
      kind:'overlay',obj,
      cB:{x:rect.vx+rect.vw/2,y:rect.vy+rect.vh/2},P0:{x:rect.W/2,y:rect.H/2},
      w0:Math.max(12,rect.vw),h0:Math.max(12,rect.vh),
      canDelete:true,__evvSceneEffectClipId:String(ov.id)
    };
  }

  const prevTarget=window.selBoxTarget;
  if(typeof prevTarget==='function'&&!prevTarget.__evv422SceneFx){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const tg=prevTarget.apply(this,arguments);
      if(!tg)return tg;
      const ov=selected();
      return ov?buildTarget(ov,tg):tg;
    };
    wrapped.__evv422SceneFx=true;window.selBoxTarget=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('selBoxTarget=window.selBoxTarget');}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* a drag of the frame must repaint the effect canvas (the placeholder image itself is invisible) */
  const prevApply=window.selBoxApply;
  if(typeof prevApply==='function'&&!prevApply.__evv422SceneFx){
    const wrapped=function(tg){
      const r=prevApply.apply(this,arguments);
      if(tg&&tg.__evvSceneEffectClipId){
        try{const api=window.__ethvelleV244Debug;if(api&&typeof api.paintEffectPreview==='function')api.paintEffectPreview();}catch(_e){}
      }
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__evv422SceneFx=true;window.selBoxApply=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('selBoxApply=window.selBoxApply');}catch(_e){}
  }

  function refresh(){try{if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}}
  window.__ethvelleV422SceneFxFrame={buildTarget,effectRect,refresh};
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v423-selection-frame-sync">
/* v423 FIX ("the selection border of a tracked clip is right at first, but after I slide the timeline it shows
   in the wrong place"): the white selection frame used to RE-COMPUTE where the clip is (own transform + tracking +
   animation) in a parallel code path, so any difference from what the renderer actually paints - the playhead
   having moved a moment before the frame was repainted, Wide/Length stretch, tracking/animation deltas - left
   the frame beside the picture. For image/video layers the frame is now read from the painted layer itself:
   its real on-screen transform matrix. The frame therefore always hugs the picture, however the clip got there.
   Only the DISPLAY is changed; dragging/resizing still edit the clip's own transform exactly as before. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV423FrameSync)return;
  window.__ethvelleV423FrameSync=true;
  let displaying=false;
  function paintedTarget(tg){
    if(!tg||tg.kind!=='overlay'||tg.__evvSceneEffectClipId||tg.__evvCharacterEffectClipId)return null;
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
    const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:[];
    const ov=list.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id);
    if(!ov||(ov.type!=='image'&&ov.type!=='video')||ov.ethvelleEffectClip)return null;
    const els=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls:null;
    const el=els&&els[ov.id];
    if(!el||!el.isConnected)return null;
    const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
    if(!cs||cs.display==='none'||!cs.transform||cs.transform==='none')return null;
    const m=new DOMMatrix(cs.transform);
    // 3D / perspective tilt cannot be shown as a flat rectangle: keep the regular frame for those.
    if([m.m13,m.m14,m.m23,m.m24,m.m34].some(v=>Math.abs(v)>1e-9))return null;
    const mm=m.scale(ov.mirrorX?-1:1,ov.mirrorY?-1:1);   // the frame itself is never mirrored
    const a=mm.a,b=mm.b,c=mm.c,d=mm.d,e=mm.e,f=mm.f;
    const org=String(cs.transformOrigin||'').split(/\s+/).map(parseFloat);
    const ox=Number.isFinite(org[0])?org[0]:(el.offsetWidth||0)/2,oy=Number.isFinite(org[1])?org[1]:(el.offsetHeight||0)/2;
    const lx=tg.cB.x-(el.offsetLeft||0),ly=tg.cB.y-(el.offsetTop||0);
    const X=ox+a*(lx-ox)+c*(ly-oy)+e+(el.offsetLeft||0);
    const Y=oy+b*(lx-ox)+d*(ly-oy)+f+(el.offsetTop||0);
    const sx=Math.hypot(a,b),sy=Math.hypot(c,d),rot=Math.atan2(b,a)*180/Math.PI;
    if(![X,Y,sx,sy,rot].every(Number.isFinite)||sx<1e-4||sy<1e-4)return null;
    return Object.assign({},tg,{
      obj:{scale:1,offsetX:X-tg.cB.x,offsetY:Y-tg.cB.y,rotation:rot},
      cB:{x:tg.cB.x,y:tg.cB.y},P0:{x:tg.cB.x,y:tg.cB.y},
      w0:tg.w0*sx,h0:tg.h0*sy
    });
  }
  const prevTarget=window.selBoxTarget;
  if(typeof prevTarget==='function'&&!prevTarget.__evv423){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const tg=prevTarget.apply(this,arguments);
      if(!displaying||!tg)return tg;
      try{return paintedTarget(tg)||tg;}catch(_e){return tg;}
    };
    wrapped.__evv423=true;window.selBoxTarget=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('selBoxTarget=window.selBoxTarget');}catch(_e){}
  }
  const prevUpdate=window.updateSelBox;
  if(typeof prevUpdate==='function'&&!prevUpdate.__evv423){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const was=displaying;displaying=true;
      try{return prevUpdate.apply(this,arguments);}finally{displaying=was;}
    };
    wrapped.__evv423=true;window.updateSelBox=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('updateSelBox=window.updateSelBox');}catch(_e){}
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v423-home-first-frame">
/* v423: the example-animation blocks on the Home page now show the FIRST FRAME of their clip as soon as the
   site opens - in EVERY block, not only the ones near the screen. The frame is painted onto a still canvas
   (the same one a paused GIF uses), so it shows even on phones that never draw a frame for a video that has
   only loaded its metadata. Playing/pausing/tapping works exactly as before. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ev423HomeStill)return;
  window.__ev423HomeStill=true;
  function stillCanvas(card,after){
    let cv=card.querySelector('canvas.home-gif-freeze');
    if(!cv){
      cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.className='home-video-img home-gif-freeze';
      (after||card.querySelector('.home-video-img')||card.firstElementChild).after(cv);
    }
    return cv;
  }
  function paintVideo(card,vid){
    try{
      const w=vid.videoWidth,h=vid.videoHeight;
      if(!w||!h||vid.readyState<2)return false;
      const cv=stillCanvas(card,vid);
      cv.width=w;cv.height=h;
      cv.getContext('2d').drawImage(vid,0,0,w,h);
      if(!card.classList.contains('is-playing'))cv.classList.remove('hv-hide');
      card.classList.add('hv-has-still');
      return true;
    }catch(_e){return false;}
  }
  const prevLoad=window.hvLoadStill;
  window.hvLoadStill=function(card){
    const vid=card&&card.querySelector?card.querySelector('video.home-video-img'):null;
    if(!vid)return typeof prevLoad==='function'?prevLoad.apply(this,arguments):undefined;
    if(card.__hvStill)return;
    card.__hvStill=true;
    const paint=()=>{if(!card.classList.contains('is-playing'))paintVideo(card,vid);};
    ['loadeddata','canplay','seeked'].forEach(ev=>vid.addEventListener(ev,paint));
    try{vid.muted=true;vid.preload='auto';}catch(_e){}
    try{if(vid.readyState>=2)paint();else vid.load();}catch(_e){}
    // Some phones will not decode a frame for a paused, never-played video: nudge it once, silently.
    setTimeout(()=>{
      if(card.classList.contains('hv-has-still')||card.classList.contains('is-playing'))return;
      try{
        const p=vid.play();
        const stop=()=>{try{vid.pause();vid.currentTime=0.1;}catch(_e){}setTimeout(paint,120);};
        if(p&&p.then)p.then(stop).catch(()=>{});else stop();
      }catch(_e){}
    },2500);
  };
  const prevSet=window.hvSetPlaying;
  window.hvSetPlaying=function(card,on){
    const r=typeof prevSet==='function'?prevSet.apply(this,arguments):undefined;
    const vid=card&&card.querySelector?card.querySelector('video.home-video-img'):null;
    if(vid){
      const cv=card.querySelector('canvas.home-gif-freeze');
      if(on){
        if(cv){
          const hide=()=>{if(card.classList.contains('is-playing'))cv.classList.add('hv-hide');};
          if(!vid.paused&&vid.currentTime>0&&vid.readyState>=3)hide();
          else vid.addEventListener('playing',()=>requestAnimationFrame(hide),{once:true});
        }
      }else{
        paintVideo(card,vid);
        const c2=card.querySelector('canvas.home-gif-freeze');
        if(c2)c2.classList.remove('hv-hide');
      }
    }
    return r;
  };
  // Load every block's first frame right away (the old code waited until a block was close to the screen).
  function loadAll(){
    try{document.querySelectorAll('#homeVideoScroll .home-video-card[data-hv-media]').forEach(c=>window.hvLoadStill(c));}catch(_e){}
  }
  const prevInit=window.hvInit;
  window.hvInit=function(){
    const r=typeof prevInit==='function'?prevInit.apply(this,arguments):undefined;
    loadAll();
    return r;
  };
  loadAll();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v424-tracked-drag-fix">
/* v424 FIX ("while I drag a clip the selection border is a bit off, on one side only, only while dragging"):
   the on-canvas box edits the DISPLAYED transform of a clip = its own transform + whatever Tracking
   and the clip's animation add on top. Every drag step used to save that displayed value as the clip's
   OWN transform, so the tracking / animation offset, scale and rotation were counted twice: the picture
   jumped on the first move, the frame and the picture disagreed while dragging, and the clip jumped again
   when the finger lifted. Now every drag / resize / rotate step converts the displayed value back to the
   clip's own value before saving, and paints the displayed value, so the picture, the frame and the saved
   clip always agree. Clips without tracking or animation are not touched at all. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV424DragFix)return;
  window.__ethvelleV424DragFix=true;
  const num=(v,d)=>{v=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(v)?v:d;};
  const cloneT=v=>{v=v||{};return {scale:num(v.scale,1),offsetX:num(v.offsetX,0),offsetY:num(v.offsetY,0),rotation:num(v.rotation,0)};};
  const nowT=()=>{try{return typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;}catch(_e){return 0;}};
  function selectedMedia(){
    try{
      const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
      const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:[];
      const c=list.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id);
      return c&&(c.type==='image'||c.type==='video')?c:null;
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  const motionAt=(clip,t)=>{
    try{if(typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'){const m=window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(clip,t);if(m)return {x:num(m.x,0),y:num(m.y,0),rotation:num(m.rotation,0),scale:num(m.scale,1)||1};}}catch(_e){}
    return {x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
  };
  const tracked=(clip,t,own)=>{
    try{if(typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'){const r=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(clip,t,own);if(r)return cloneT(r);}}catch(_e){}
    return cloneT(own);
  };
  function needsConversion(clip,t){
    if(!clip)return false;
    const tr=clip.clipTracking,legacy=clip.characterTracking;
    if((tr&&tr.enabled&&tr.targetId)||(legacy&&legacy.enabled))return true;
    const m=motionAt(clip,t);
    return Math.abs(m.x)>1e-6||Math.abs(m.y)>1e-6||Math.abs(m.rotation)>1e-6||Math.abs(m.scale-1)>1e-6;
  }
  // own (what is saved on the clip)  ->  displayed (what is painted and framed)
  function toDisplay(clip,t,own){
    const k=tracked(clip,t,cloneT(own)),m=motionAt(clip,t);
    return {scale:k.scale*m.scale,offsetX:k.offsetX+m.x,offsetY:k.offsetY+m.y,rotation:k.rotation+m.rotation};
  }
  // displayed  ->  own: undo the animation, then undo tracking (probing the tracker with unit values so the
  // inverse is exact for any scale/rotation/pivot the tracker applies).
  function toOwn(clip,t,disp){
    const m=motionAt(clip,t),d=cloneT(disp);
    const D={scale:d.scale/(m.scale||1),offsetX:d.offsetX-m.x,offsetY:d.offsetY-m.y,rotation:d.rotation-m.rotation};
    const zero={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
    const c=tracked(clip,t,zero),ex=tracked(clip,t,Object.assign({},zero,{offsetX:1})),ey=tracked(clip,t,Object.assign({},zero,{offsetY:1}));
    const a=ex.offsetX-c.offsetX,b=ey.offsetX-c.offsetX,cc=ex.offsetY-c.offsetY,dd=ey.offsetY-c.offsetY;
    const det=a*dd-b*cc;
    if(!Number.isFinite(det)||Math.abs(det)<1e-9||!(c.scale>1e-6))return null;
    const rx=D.offsetX-c.offsetX,ry=D.offsetY-c.offsetY;
    const out={scale:D.scale/c.scale,offsetX:(dd*rx-b*ry)/det,offsetY:(-cc*rx+a*ry)/det,rotation:D.rotation-c.rotation};
    return Object.values(out).every(Number.isFinite)?out:null;
  }
  window.__ev424={needsConversion,toOwn,toDisplay,selectedMedia};

  /* The editor batches drag updates (one paint per display frame) and saves them a frame LATER, so the
     converted value must travel in its own copy of the target - the live target keeps the displayed
     value the finger is editing. */
  const prevApply=window.selBoxApply;
  if(typeof prevApply==='function'&&!prevApply.__evv424){
    const wrapped=function(tg){
      if(tg&&tg.kind==='overlay'&&tg.obj&&!tg.__evv424){
        try{
          const clip=selectedMedia(),t=nowT();
          if(clip&&!clip.ethvelleEffectClip&&needsConversion(clip,t)){
            const own=toOwn(clip,t,tg.obj);
            if(own){
              const proxy=Object.assign({},tg,{obj:Object.assign({},tg.obj,own),__evv424:{clip,t}});
              return prevApply.call(this,proxy);
            }
          }
        }catch(_e){}
      }
      return prevApply.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv424=true;window.selBoxApply=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('selBoxApply=window.selBoxApply');}catch(_e){}
  }

  const prevFast=window.applyOverlayTransformPreviewFast;
  if(typeof prevFast==='function'&&!prevFast.__evv424){
    const wrapped=function(tg){
      const c=tg&&tg.__evv424;
      if(c&&tg.obj){
        try{return prevFast.call(this,Object.assign({},tg,{obj:Object.assign({},tg.obj,toDisplay(c.clip,c.t,tg.obj))}));}catch(_e){}
      }
      return prevFast.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv424=true;window.applyOverlayTransformPreviewFast=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('applyOverlayTransformPreviewFast=window.applyOverlayTransformPreviewFast');}catch(_e){}
  }

  const prevFinish=window.finishSelBoxEdit;
  if(typeof prevFinish==='function'&&!prevFinish.__evv424){
    const wrapped=function(tg){
      let restore=null;
      try{
        if(tg&&tg.kind==='overlay'&&tg.obj){
          const clip=selectedMedia(),t=nowT();
          if(clip&&!clip.ethvelleEffectClip&&needsConversion(clip,t)){
            const own=toOwn(clip,t,tg.obj);
            if(own){restore=tg.obj;tg.obj=Object.assign({},tg.obj,own);}
          }
        }
      }catch(_e){restore=null;}
      try{return prevFinish.apply(this,arguments);}
      finally{if(restore)tg.obj=restore;}
    };
    wrapped.__evv424=true;window.finishSelBoxEdit=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)('finishSelBoxEdit=window.finishSelBoxEdit');}catch(_e){}
  }
})();
</script>


<style id="ethvelle-v427-effect-visual-fixes-css">
  html body #screen-home #homeAccountSummary.ev-v427-signin-compact,
  html body #screen-home .home-account-summary.ev-v427-signin-compact{
    width:clamp(116px,35vw,144px)!important;min-width:116px!important;max-width:144px!important;
    padding:3px 7px!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;
  }
  html body #screen-home #homeAccountBalance.ev-v427-signin-text{
    font-size:13px!important;font-weight:650!important;line-height:1.1!important;
    letter-spacing:-.2px!important;white-space:nowrap!important;
  }

<script id="ethvelle-v427-effect-visual-fixes-js">
/* Ethvelle v427 — canvas-ratio effect clips, richer cloud rendering, true Blur/Mosaic effect layers,
   and a compact signed-out home credits capsule. This patch intentionally wraps the existing
   public render/setter APIs rather than rewriting the editor's core timeline or media pipeline. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV427EffectVisualFixes)return;
  window.__ethvelleV427EffectVisualFixes=true;

  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  const arr=()=>{
    try{if(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))return overlays;}catch(_e){}
    return Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[];
  };
  const stageSize=()=>{
    let w=0,h=0;
    try{w=Number(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?stageW:0);h=Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:0);}catch(_e){}
    const stage=$('canvasStage');
    if(!(w>0))w=Number(stage&&stage.clientWidth)||1;
    if(!(h>0))h=Number(stage&&stage.clientHeight)||1;
    return {w:Math.max(1,w),h:Math.max(1,h)};
  };
  const nowTime=()=>{
    try{return Number(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;}catch(_e){return Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;}
  };
  const cropZero=()=>({left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0});

  /* All scene-effect layers use the project canvas aspect ratio, not the source image's ratio. */
  function syncEffectCanvasRatio(clip){
    if(!clip||!clip.ethvelleEffectClip||clip.ethvelleEffectKind==='character')return false;
    const s=stageSize();
    let changed=false;
    if(Number(clip.sourceWidth)!==s.w){clip.sourceWidth=s.w;changed=true;}
    if(Number(clip.sourceHeight)!==s.h){clip.sourceHeight=s.h;changed=true;}
    const c=clip.crop;
    if(!c||Number(c.left)||Number(c.right)||Number(c.top)||Number(c.bottom)){
      clip.crop=cropZero();changed=true;
    }
    clip.ethvelleCanvasRatioV427=true;
    clip.ethvelleCanvasAspectV427=s.w/s.h;
    return changed;
  }
  function syncAllEffectCanvasRatios(){
    let changed=false;
    arr().forEach(o=>{if(syncEffectCanvasRatio(o))changed=true;});
    return changed;
  }

  /* Catch new effect layers after the existing Blur/Mosaic/weather creation path returns. */
  const oldSetEffect=window.setEthvelleVisualEffect;
  if(typeof oldSetEffect==='function'&&!oldSetEffect.__evv427Ratio){
    const wrappedSetEffect=function(){
      const before=new Set(arr().map(o=>String(o&&o.id)));
      const result=oldSetEffect.apply(this,arguments);
      let changed=false;
      arr().forEach(o=>{
        if(!o||!o.ethvelleEffectClip)return;
        const isNew=!before.has(String(o.id));
        const isSelected=String(o.id)===String((typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?selectedClip:window.selectedClip||'');
        if(isNew||isSelected)changed=syncEffectCanvasRatio(o)||changed;
      });
      if(result&&result.ethvelleEffectClip)changed=syncEffectCanvasRatio(result)||changed;
      if(changed){
        try{if(typeof window.renderOverlayLayers==='function')window.renderOverlayLayers();}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
        try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){}
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedSetEffect.__evv427Ratio=true;
    window.setEthvelleVisualEffect=wrappedSetEffect;
  }

  /* Repair older saved effect clips as soon as they pass through the compositor. */
  function wrapRatioSync(name){
    const base=window[name];
    if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__evv427RatioSync)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=base.apply(this,arguments);
      syncAllEffectCanvasRatios();
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv427RatioSync=true;
    window[name]=wrapped;
    try{(0,eval)(name+'=window.'+name);}catch(_e){}
  }
  wrapRatioSync('renderOverlayLayers');
  wrapRatioSync('renderFrame');

  function effectType(o){return String((o&&o.ethvelleEffectType)||(o&&o.visualEffects&&o.visualEffects.type)||'');}
  function effectActive(o,t){return !!(o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&t>=Number(o.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(o.trimEnd||0));}
  function targetFor(effectClip){
    const id=String(effectClip&&effectClip.ethvelleEffectTargetId||'');
    return arr().find(o=>o&&!o.ethvelleEffectClip&&String(o.id)===id)||null;
  }
  function liveMediaFor(id){
    try{if(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[String(id)])return overlayMediaEls[String(id)];}catch(_e){}
    try{
      const node=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-overlay-id]')).find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId)===String(id));
      if(node)return node.querySelector('video,img,canvas');
    }catch(_e){}
    return null;
  }
  function mediaSize(media){
    if(!media)return null;
    const tag=String(media.tagName||'').toUpperCase();
    const w=tag==='VIDEO'?Number(media.videoWidth):Number(media.naturalWidth||media.width);
    const h=tag==='VIDEO'?Number(media.videoHeight):Number(media.naturalHeight||media.height);
    return w>0&&h>0?{w,h,tag}:null;
  }
  function cropFractions(clip){
    let c=clip&&clip.crop||cropZero();
    try{if(window.ethvelleCropFractions)c=window.ethvelleCropFractions(c)||c;}catch(_e){}
    return {left:clamp(c.left,0,.92),right:clamp(c.right,0,.92),top:clamp(c.top,0,.92),bottom:clamp(c.bottom,0,.92)};
  }
  function displayedTransform(clip,t){
    let keyed=null,tr=null,motion={x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
    try{if(window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue)keyed=window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',t);}catch(_e){}
    try{if(clip.clipTracking&&clip.clipTracking.enabled&&clip.clipTracking.targetId&&window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform)tr=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(clip,t,keyed);}catch(_e){}
    tr=tr||keyed||clip.transform||{};
    try{if(window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState)motion=window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(clip,t)||motion;}catch(_e){}
    return {
      x:(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion.x)||0),
      y:(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion.y)||0),
      rotation:(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion.rotation)||0),
      scale:(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(motion.scale)||1)
    };
  }
  const scratchByKey=Object.create(null);

  /* Draw the actual target media pixels on the effect canvas. This is a real effect layer,
     not a cloned video/image overlay. The target is only used as the pixel source. */
  function drawSourceEffect(ctx,effectClip,t,outW,outH,kind){
    const target=targetFor(effectClip);
    if(!target||!['video','image'].includes(target.type))return;
    if(t<Number(target.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(target.trimEnd||0))return;
    const media=liveMediaFor(target.id),ms=mediaSize(media);
    if(!media||!ms)return;
    if(ms.tag==='VIDEO'&&media.readyState<2)return; // Do not reject a usable previous frame just because seeking is true.

    const s=stageSize(),sx=outW/s.w,sy=outH/s.h;
    const fit=Math.min(s.w/ms.w,s.h/ms.h);
    const cw=ms.w*fit,ch=ms.h*fit,cx=(s.w-cw)/2,cy=(s.h-ch)/2;
    const crop=cropFractions(target);
    const vx=cx+crop.left*cw,vy=cy+crop.top*ch;
    const vw=Math.max(1,cw*(1-crop.left-crop.right)),vh=Math.max(1,ch*(1-crop.top-crop.bottom));
    const tr=displayedTransform(effectClip,t);

    ctx.save();
    ctx.scale(sx,sy);
    ctx.translate(s.w/2+tr.x,s.h/2+tr.y);
    if(tr.rotation)ctx.rotate(tr.rotation*Math.PI/180);
    ctx.scale(Math.max(.0001,tr.scale)*(effectClip.mirrorX?-1:1),Math.max(.0001,tr.scale)*(effectClip.mirrorY?-1:1));
    ctx.translate(-s.w/2,-s.h/2);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(vx,vy,vw,vh);ctx.clip();
    const mask=target.visualMask||effectClip.visualMask;
    if(mask&&window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip){try{window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(ctx,mask,s.w,s.h);}catch(_e){}}

    const fx=Object.assign({intensity:135,density:145,scale:100},effectClip.visualEffects||{});
    const strength=clamp(fx.intensity,0,250)/135;
    if(strength<=0){ctx.restore();return;}
    ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,strength);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;
    if(kind==='mosaic'){
      const density=clamp(fx.density,10,300)/100;
      const size=clamp(fx.scale,25,250)/100;
      const block=Math.max(3,Math.min(100,Math.round((14/Math.max(.1,density))*(100/size))));
      const lowW=Math.max(1,Math.min(900,Math.round(ms.w/block))),lowH=Math.max(1,Math.min(900,Math.round(ms.h/block)));
      const key=String(target.id)+'|'+lowW+'x'+lowH;
      let scratch=scratchByKey[key];
      if(!scratch){scratch=document.createElement('canvas');scratchByKey[key]=scratch;}
      if(scratch.width!==lowW||scratch.height!==lowH){scratch.width=lowW;scratch.height=lowH;}
      const sc=scratch.getContext('2d');
      if(sc){
        sc.clearRect(0,0,lowW,lowH);sc.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;
        try{sc.drawImage(media,0,0,lowW,lowH);}catch(_e){ctx.restore();return;}
        ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
        ctx.drawImage(scratch,0,0,lowW,lowH,cx,cy,cw,ch);
      }
    }else{
      const baseRadius=clamp(fx.intensity,0,250)/30;
      const radius=Math.max(0.6,baseRadius*Math.max(sx,sy));
      const pad=baseRadius*1.35;
      ctx.filter='blur('+radius.toFixed(2)+'px)';
      try{ctx.drawImage(media,cx-pad,cy-pad,cw+pad*2,ch+pad*2);}catch(_e){}
      ctx.filter='none';
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* Route linked Blur through the effect canvas only. This avoids a CSS-filtered source plus
     a second image-like clip, and makes timeline, paused preview and export use the same pixels. */
  const oldBlurLookup=window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect;
  if(typeof oldBlurLookup==='function'&&!oldBlurLookup.__evv427CanvasBlur){
    const wrappedBlurLookup=function(clip,time){
      if(clip&&!clip.ethvelleEffectClip){
        const t=Number(time)||0,id=String(clip.id||'');
        const linked=arr().some(o=>o&&o.ethvelleEffectClip&&effectType(o)==='blur'&&String(o.ethvelleEffectTargetId||'')===id&&effectActive(o,t));
        if(linked)return null;
      }
      return oldBlurLookup.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedBlurLookup.__evv427CanvasBlur=true;
    window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect=wrappedBlurLookup;
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------------------
     Natural cloud silhouettes: every cloud has a different width, height, lobe
     count and drift. The stronger opaque layered gradients create deeper storm,
     cloudy and sunrise looks instead of repeated translucent same-size puffs.
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  function hash(n){const x=Math.sin((n+1)*127.1+37.7)*43758.5453;return x-Math.floor(x);}
  const wrap=(v,m)=>((v%m)+m)%m;
  function cloudPalette(type){
    if(type==='storm')return {top:[103,123,148],mid:[51,69,91],bottom:[13,27,45],rim:[165,185,207],shadow:[5,13,25]};
    if(type==='sunrise')return {top:[241,185,157],mid:[169,91,101],bottom:[58,48,76],rim:[255,190,124],shadow:[47,36,66]};
    return {top:[226,235,245],mid:[139,157,178],bottom:[65,83,105],rim:[244,248,252],shadow:[42,57,76]};
  }
  function color(rgb,a){return 'rgba('+rgb[0]+','+rgb[1]+','+rgb[2]+','+clamp(a,0,1)+')';}
  function drawCloudCluster(ctx,x,y,rx,ry,seed,type,opacity,detail){
    const p=cloudPalette(type);
    const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(x,y-ry*1.1,x,y+ry*1.25);
    grad.addColorStop(0,color(p.top,opacity));
    grad.addColorStop(.46,color(p.mid,Math.min(1,opacity+.015)));
    grad.addColorStop(1,color(p.bottom,Math.min(1,opacity+.025)));
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x,y+ry*.24,rx*.91,ry*.56,0,0,Math.PI*2);
    const lobes=5+Math.floor(hash(seed+31)*5);
    for(let j=0;j<lobes;j++){
      const f=lobes<=1?0:j/(lobes-1);
      const jitter=(hash(seed*13+j*7)-.5)*.16;
      const px=x+(-.72+f*1.44+jitter)*rx;
      const py=y-ry*(.13+hash(seed*17+j*11)*.43);
      const prx=rx*(.18+hash(seed*23+j*19)*.16);
      const pry=ry*(.34+hash(seed*29+j*5)*.43);
      ctx.ellipse(px,py,prx,pry,(hash(seed+j*37)-.5)*.22,0,Math.PI*2);
    }
    // A low, irregular shelf joins the lobes into a believable single cloud mass.
    ctx.ellipse(x-rx*.04,y+ry*.30,rx*.84,ry*.43,0,0,Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fillStyle=grad;
    ctx.shadowColor=color(p.shadow,type==='storm'?.54:.28);
    ctx.shadowBlur=Math.max(1,ry*.10);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;

    // Thin broken light on the upper cloud edges; the storm variant remains cold and subdued.
    ctx.globalAlpha=type==='storm'?.23:(type==='sunrise'?.52:.25);
    ctx.strokeStyle=color(p.rim,1);
    ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.7,ry*.035);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x-rx*.78,y+ry*.02);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-rx*.55,y-ry*.28,x-rx*.46,y-ry*.73,x-rx*.20,y-ry*.56);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+rx*.06,y-ry*.98,x+rx*.29,y-ry*.48,x+rx*.43,y-ry*.57);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+rx*.60,y-ry*.72,x+rx*.68,y-ry*.27,x+rx*.82,y-ry*.08);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawEnhancedCloudScene(ctx,type,t,w,h,settings){
    const fx=settings&&typeof settings==='object'?settings:{};
    const intensity=clamp(fx.intensity==null?145:fx.intensity,0,250);
    if(intensity<=0)return;
    const density=clamp(fx.density==null?145:fx.density,10,300)/100;
    const speed=clamp(fx.speed==null?100:fx.speed,0,300)/100;
    const scale=clamp(fx.scale==null?100:fx.scale,25,250)/100;
    const ox=clamp(fx.x,-100,100)/100*w*.34,oy=clamp(fx.y,-100,100)/100*h*.34;
    const tm=(Number(t)||0)*speed;
    const strength=Math.min(3.5,intensity/70);
    const opacity=type==='storm'?Math.min(.99,.89+strength*.025):Math.min(.985,.86+strength*.035);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(w*.5+ox,h*.5+oy);ctx.scale(scale,scale);ctx.translate(-w*.5,-h*.5);
    if(type==='storm'){
      const shade=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h*.68);
      shade.addColorStop(0,'rgba(4,12,25,.30)');shade.addColorStop(.55,'rgba(10,23,40,.13)');shade.addColorStop(1,'rgba(10,20,35,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=shade;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h*.70);
    }else if(type==='sunrise'){
      ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';
      const sky=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h);
      sky.addColorStop(0,'rgba(74,75,127,.08)');sky.addColorStop(.42,'rgba(189,91,91,.20)');sky.addColorStop(.72,'rgba(255,143,83,.32)');sky.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,185,110,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=sky;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      const sx=w*.72,sy=h*.43,sr=Math.min(w,h)*.20;
      const glow=ctx.createRadialGradient(sx,sy,2,sx,sy,sr*2.6);
      glow.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,248,214,.78)');glow.addColorStop(.25,'rgba(255,184,113,.49)');glow.addColorStop(.62,'rgba(240,103,91,.17)');glow.addColorStop(1,'rgba(240,103,91,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=glow;ctx.fillRect(sx-sr*3,sy-sr*3,sr*6,sr*6);
      ctx.restore();
    }
    const layers=type==='storm'?3:3;
    for(let layer=0;layer<layers;layer++){
      const layerScale=layer===0?.72:(layer===1?1:1.22);
      const baseCount=type==='storm'?6:(type==='sunrise'?7:6);
      const count=Math.max(3,Math.min(15,Math.round(baseCount*density*(layer===2?1.12:1))));
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const seed=layer*701+i*37+11;
        const rx=w*(.075+hash(seed+4)*.105)*layerScale;
        const ry=h*(.045+hash(seed+8)*.075)*layerScale;
        const span=w+rx*2.8;
        const x=wrap(hash(seed+12)*span+tm*(type==='storm'?9:5)*(layer+1)*speed,span)-rx*1.4;
        const y=h*(.075+layer*.145+hash(seed+16)*.105)+Math.sin(tm*.12+i*.47+layer)*h*.009;
        drawCloudCluster(ctx,x,y,rx,ry,seed,type,opacity-(layer===0?.035:0),layer);
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawEnhancedCloudOnOverlay(ctx,clip,type,t,ow,oh){
    const s=stageSize(),sx=ow/s.w,sy=oh/s.h;
    let iw=Number(clip.sourceWidth)||0,ih=Number(clip.sourceHeight)||0;
    const media=liveMediaFor(clip.id);
    if(media){const ms=mediaSize(media);if(ms){iw=iw||ms.w;ih=ih||ms.h;}}
    if(!(iw>0&&ih>0)){iw=s.w;ih=s.h;}
    const fit=Math.min(s.w/iw,s.h/ih),cw=iw*fit,ch=ih*fit,cx=(s.w-cw)/2,cy=(s.h-ch)/2;
    const crop=clip.ethvelleEffectClip?cropZero():cropFractions(clip);
    const vx=cx+crop.left*cw,vy=cy+crop.top*ch,vw=Math.max(1,cw*(1-crop.left-crop.right)),vh=Math.max(1,ch*(1-crop.top-crop.bottom));
    const tr=displayedTransform(clip,t);
    ctx.save();ctx.scale(sx,sy);ctx.translate(s.w/2+tr.x,s.h/2+tr.y);
    if(tr.rotation)ctx.rotate(tr.rotation*Math.PI/180);
    ctx.scale(tr.scale*(clip.mirrorX?-1:1),tr.scale*(clip.mirrorY?-1:1));ctx.translate(-s.w/2,-s.h/2);
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(vx,vy,vw,vh);ctx.clip();
    if(clip.visualMask&&window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip){try{window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(ctx,clip.visualMask,s.w,s.h);}catch(_e){}}
    ctx.translate(vx,vy);
    drawEnhancedCloudScene(ctx,type,t,vw,vh,clip.visualEffects||{});
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawEnhancedClouds(ctx,t,ow,oh){
    const s=stageSize();
    const bg=(()=>{try{return typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(t):null;}catch(_e){return null;}})();
    if(bg&&bg.visualEffects&&['storm','clouds','sunrise'].includes(String(bg.visualEffects.type))){
      ctx.save();ctx.scale(ow/s.w,oh/s.h);
      drawEnhancedCloudScene(ctx,bg.visualEffects.type,t,s.w,s.h,bg.visualEffects);
      ctx.restore();
    }
    arr().forEach(o=>{
      if(!o||!['image','video','text','shape','color'].includes(o.type))return;
      if(t<Number(o.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(o.trimEnd||0))return;
      const type=String(o.visualEffects&&o.visualEffects.type||'');
      if(!['storm','clouds','sunrise'].includes(type))return;
      drawEnhancedCloudOnOverlay(ctx,o,type,t,ow,oh);
    });
  }

  /* The previous renderer's Mosaic routine skipped frames while a video was seeking and
     drew it through a separate source-preview path. Temporarily mask only linked Blur/Mosaic
     effect metadata during the legacy pass, then draw both from their target media here. */
  const oldDrawEffects=window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext;
  if(typeof oldDrawEffects==='function'&&!oldDrawEffects.__evv427Effects){
    const wrappedDrawEffects=function(ctx,tSec,outW,outH){
      const time=Number(tSec)||0;
      const w=Number(outW)||stageSize().w,h=Number(outH)||stageSize().h;
      const muted=[];
      arr().forEach(o=>{
        if(!o||!o.ethvelleEffectClip)return;
        const type=effectType(o);
        if((type==='blur'||type==='mosaic')&&effectActive(o,time)){
          muted.push({o,visualType:o.visualEffects&&o.visualEffects.type,clipType:o.ethvelleEffectType});
          if(o.visualEffects)o.visualEffects.type='none';
          o.ethvelleEffectType='__evv427-muted';
        }
      });
      let result;
      try{result=oldDrawEffects.apply(this,arguments);}
      finally{
        muted.forEach(m=>{if(m.o.visualEffects)m.o.visualEffects.type=m.visualType;m.o.ethvelleEffectType=m.clipType;});
      }
      drawEnhancedClouds(ctx,time,w,h);
      muted.forEach(m=>{
        const type=String(m.clipType||m.visualType||'');
        if(type!=='blur'&&type!=='mosaic')return;
        if(!effectActive(m.o,time))return;
        drawSourceEffect(ctx,m.o,time,w,h,type);
      });
      return result;
    };
    wrappedDrawEffects.__evv427Effects=true;
    window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext=wrappedDrawEffects;
  }

  /* Signed-out home capsule: smaller lettering and a narrower footprint; signed-in credit
     numbers retain the existing v394 sizing. */
  function patchHomeSignInCapsule(){
    const button=$('homeAccountSummary'),balance=$('homeAccountBalance');
    if(!button||!balance)return;
    let signedIn=false;try{signedIn=!!(typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser);}catch(_e){}
    button.classList.toggle('ev-v427-signin-compact',!signedIn);
    balance.classList.toggle('ev-v427-signin-text',!signedIn);
  }
  const oldHomeCreditRender=window.renderHomeCreditSummary;
  if(typeof oldHomeCreditRender==='function'&&!oldHomeCreditRender.__evv427Home){
    const wrappedHomeCreditRender=function(){
      const result=oldHomeCreditRender.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(patchHomeSignInCapsule);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedHomeCreditRender.__evv427Home=true;
    window.renderHomeCreditSummary=wrappedHomeCreditRender;
  }
  function startHomeObserver(){
    patchHomeSignInCapsule();
    const balance=$('homeAccountBalance'),button=$('homeAccountSummary');
    if(balance&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
      const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(patchHomeSignInCapsule));
      observer.observe(balance,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});
    }
    if(button&&typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
      const observer=new ResizeObserver(()=>patchHomeSignInCapsule());
      observer.observe(button);
    }
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',startHomeObserver,{once:true});
  else startHomeObserver();
  window.addEventListener('resize',patchHomeSignInCapsule,{passive:true});

  /* Normalize any old effect clip now present in a restored project, then allow later renders
     to keep it matched to the current canvas aspect ratio. */
  syncAllEffectCanvasRatios();
})();

</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v428-admin-effects-panel">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const FILTERS=[{id:'all',label:'All'},{id:'effect',label:'Effects'},{id:'character',label:'Character effect'}];
  let filter='all',query='',cat='all';
  const CATS={effect:[['water','Water'],['fire','Fire'],['smoke','Smoke'],['light','Light / Sparkle'],['weather','Weather'],['fighting','Fighting'],['magic','Magic'],['other','Other']],character:[['face','Face expression'],['emotion','Emotion & icons'],['aura','Aura & glow'],['power','Power & magic'],['body','Body & outfit'],['other','Other']]};
  const catList=()=>{if(filter==='effect'||filter==='character')return CATS[filter];const seen=new Set();return CATS.effect.concat(CATS.character).filter(c=>seen.has(c[0])?false:(seen.add(c[0]),true));};
  function renderCats(){
    const bar=$('editorAssetFilters');if(!bar)return;
    let row=$('editorEffectCats');
    if(!row){row=document.createElement('div');row.id='editorEffectCats';row.className='editor-asset-filters';bar.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',row);}
    const list=catList();if(cat!=='all'&&!list.some(c=>c[0]===cat))cat='all';
    row.innerHTML=[['all','All categories']].concat(list).map(c=>'<button type="button" class="editor-asset-filter'+(cat===c[0]?' active':'')+'" onclick="ethvelleSetEffectCat(\''+c[0]+'\')">'+c[1]+'</button>').join('');
  }
  window.ethvelleSetEffectCat=function(id){cat=id;renderCats();renderResults();};
  const esc=s=>String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const list=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(siteContent.effects)?siteContent.effects:[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const sectionOf=e=>String(e&&e.section||'effect')==='character'?'character':'effect';
  const sectionLabel=e=>sectionOf(e)==='character'?'Character effect':'Effects';
  try{panelContent.effects={title:'Effects',body:'<div id="editorEffectLibrary"></div>'};}catch(_e){}

  function bodyHtml(){
    return '<div class="editor-asset-toolbar"><div class="editor-asset-search-wrap'+(query?' has-query':'')+'"><svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>'+
      '<input class="editor-asset-search" id="editorEffectSearch" type="search" aria-label="Search effects" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search effects\u2026" value="'+esc(query)+'" oninput="ethvelleFilterEffects(this.value)">'+
      '<button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" id="editorEffectSearchClear" onclick="ethvelleClearEffectSearch()" aria-label="Clear effect search"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button></div>'+
      '<div class="editor-asset-filters" id="editorAssetFilters">'+FILTERS.map(f=>'<button type="button" class="editor-asset-filter'+(filter===f.id?' active':'')+'" data-filter="'+f.id+'" onclick="ethvelleSetEffectFilter(\''+f.id+'\')">'+f.label+'</button>').join('')+'</div></div>'+
      '<div class="editor-asset-results" id="editorEffectResults"></div>';
  }
  function renderResults(){
    const host=$('editorEffectResults');if(!host)return;
    const all=list(),q=query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
    const shown=all.filter(e=>(filter==='all'||sectionOf(e)===filter)&&(cat==='all'||String(e.category||'other')===cat)&&(!q||[e.name,sectionLabel(e),e.category].join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase().includes(q)));
    host.replaceChildren();
    if(!shown.length){const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='editor-asset-empty';empty.textContent=all.length?'No matching effects found.':'No effects published yet.';host.appendChild(empty);return;}
    const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='editor-assets-grid';host.appendChild(grid);
    shown.forEach(fx=>{
      const src=String(fx.src||''),isVideo=String(fx.mediaType||'image')==='video';
      const card=document.createElement('button');card.type='button';card.className='editor-asset-card';
      const thumb=document.createElement('span');thumb.className='editor-asset-thumb';
      if(isVideo&&src){const v=document.createElement('video');v.src=src;v.muted=true;v.playsInline=true;v.loop=true;v.preload='metadata';v.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain';v.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',()=>{if(v.videoWidth>0&&v.videoHeight>0)thumb.style.aspectRatio=v.videoWidth+' / '+v.videoHeight;},{once:true});v.addEventListener('loadeddata',()=>{try{v.currentTime=0.05;v.play().catch(()=>{});}catch(e){}},{once:true});thumb.appendChild(v);}
      else if(src){const img=document.createElement('img');img.addEventListener('load',()=>{if(img.naturalWidth>0&&img.naturalHeight>0)thumb.style.aspectRatio=img.naturalWidth+' / '+img.naturalHeight;},{once:true});img.src=src;img.alt='';img.loading='lazy';thumb.appendChild(img);}
      else thumb.textContent='FX';
      const name=document.createElement('span');name.className='editor-asset-name';name.textContent=fx.name||'Effect';
      const label=document.createElement('span');label.className='editor-asset-category';label.textContent=window.ethvelleFxLabel?window.ethvelleFxLabel(fx,sectionLabel(fx)):sectionLabel(fx);
      card.append(thumb,name,label);
      try{if(window.ethvelleFxLocked&&window.ethvelleFxLocked(fx)){card.classList.add('ev-asset-locked');card.appendChild(evAssetLockBadge());}}catch(_e){}
      try{if(window.ethvelleFxOnDevice&&window.ethvelleFxOnDevice(fx))card.classList.add('on-device');}catch(_e){}
      card.addEventListener('click',()=>window.ethvelleAddEffect(fx.id));
      grid.appendChild(card);
    });
  }
  window.ethvelleRenderEffectsPanel=function(){
    const el=$('editorEffectLibrary');if(!el)return;
    el.innerHTML=bodyHtml();renderCats();renderResults();
  };
  window.ethvelleFilterEffects=function(v){
    query=String(v||'');
    const wrap=document.querySelector('#screen-editor .editor-asset-search-wrap');if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('has-query',!!query);
    renderResults();
  };
  window.ethvelleClearEffectSearch=function(){
    const input=$('editorEffectSearch');if(!input)return;
    input.value='';window.ethvelleFilterEffects('');
    try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){input.focus();}
  };
  window.ethvelleSetEffectFilter=function(id){
    filter=FILTERS.some(f=>f.id===id)?id:'all';
    document.querySelectorAll('#editorAssetFilters .editor-asset-filter').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.filter===filter));
    renderCats();renderResults();
  };
  /* Small still of the effect file, used as the repeated icon inside its timeline clip.
     Animated effects often start empty, so videos are sampled until a visible frame is found.
     Resolves '' (the clip then shows a sparkle icon) if the file cannot be read in time. */
  function effectIconFrom(src,isVideo){
    return new Promise(resolve=>{
      let done=false,media=null;
      const finish=v=>{if(done)return;done=true;clearTimeout(timer);if(isVideo&&media){try{media.removeAttribute('src');media.load();}catch(_e){}}resolve(v||'');};
      const timer=setTimeout(()=>finish(''),4000);
      const snap=(w,h)=>{
        const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=64;c.height=64;
        const x=c.getContext('2d');const k=Math.min(64/w,64/h);
        x.drawImage(media,(64-w*k)/2,(64-h*k)/2,w*k,h*k);
        const d=x.getImageData(0,0,64,64).data;let seen=0;
        for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4)if(d[i+3]>40&&d[i]+d[i+1]+d[i+2]>60)seen++;
        return seen>20?c.toDataURL('image/png'):'';
      };
      try{
        if(!isVideo){
          media=new Image();media.crossOrigin='anonymous';
          media.onload=()=>{try{finish(snap(media.naturalWidth||64,media.naturalHeight||64));}catch(_e){finish('');}};
          media.onerror=()=>finish('');media.src=src;return;
        }
        media=document.createElement('video');media.crossOrigin='anonymous';media.muted=true;media.playsInline=true;media.preload='auto';
        const points=[.4,.65,.2,.85];let n=0,length=1;
        const seek=()=>{media.currentTime=Math.max(.05,length*points[n]);};
        media.onloadeddata=()=>{media.onloadeddata=null;length=Number.isFinite(media.duration)&&media.duration>0?media.duration:1;seek();};
        media.onseeked=()=>{try{const out=snap(media.videoWidth||64,media.videoHeight||64);if(out||++n>=points.length)finish(out);else seek();}catch(_e){finish('');}};
        media.onerror=()=>finish('');media.src=src;
      }catch(_e){finish('');}
    });
  }
  window.ethvelleAddEffect=async function(id){
    const fx=list().find(e=>String(e.id)===String(id));
    if(!fx){showToast('This effect is unavailable',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    const src=String(fx.src||'');
    if(!src){showToast('This effect has no file',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    if(!currentProject){showToast('Open a project first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    const isVideo=String(fx.mediaType||'image')==='video';
    /* The file is downloaded the first time only, kept in the device media store, and every later use plays from there. */
    let useSrc=src,key,info;
    try{
      const r=typeof window.ethvelleFxEnsureLocal==='function'?await window.ethvelleFxEnsureLocal(fx):null;
      if(r&&r.key&&r.blob){useSrc=URL.createObjectURL(r.blob);key=r.key;info={name:fx.name||'Effect',size:r.blob.size,lastModified:Date.now()};}
    }catch(_e){showToast('Could not save this effect to your device. Using it online.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2200});}
    const thumb=await effectIconFrom(useSrc,isVideo);
    addMediaOverlay({assetEffect:{id:fx.id,thumb:thumb},type:isVideo?'video':'image',src:useSrc,name:fx.name||'Effect',duration:5,thumbs:[],peaks:[],deviceMediaKey:key,deviceMediaInfo:info,...(isVideo?{sourceDuration:Number(fx.duration)||5,sourceOffset:0}:{})});
    closePanel();
  };

  /* Same full-height panel fit the Assets panel uses. */
  function fitPanel(){
    const panel=$('toolPanel'),editor=$('screen-editor');
    if(!panel||!editor||!panel.classList.contains('open')||!panel.classList.contains('ev-v114-assets-fit'))return;
    const canvas=editor.querySelector('.canvas-wrap');if(!canvas)return;
    const sr=editor.getBoundingClientRect(),cr=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const top=Math.max(0,Math.min(sr.height-150,cr.bottom-sr.top+1));
    panel.style.setProperty('--ev-v114-assets-top',top+'px');
    panel.style.setProperty('--ev-v114-assets-height',Math.max(150,sr.height-top)+'px');
  }
  const prevOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof prevOpen==='function'&&!prevOpen.__evAdminEffects){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      const result=prevOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='effects'){
        window.ethvelleRenderEffectsPanel();
        const panel=$('toolPanel');if(panel){panel.classList.add('ev-v114-assets-fit');requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitPanel));}
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evAdminEffects=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v429-effect-plan-access">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const CAT={face:'Face expression',emotion:'Emotion & icons',aura:'Aura & glow',power:'Power & magic',body:'Body & outfit',water:'Water',fire:'Fire',smoke:'Smoke',light:'Light / Sparkle',weather:'Weather',fighting:'Fighting',magic:'Magic',other:'Other'};
  const plansOf=raw=>{
    const plans=(typeof getPlans==='function'?getPlans():[])||[];
    const free=plans.find(p=>Number(p.price)===0)||{id:'p_free',name:'Free',price:0};
    const ids=Array.isArray(raw)?raw.map(String).filter(Boolean):(raw==null||raw==='')?[]:[String(raw)];
    if(!ids.length)return [free];
    const r=ids.map(id=>{const n=id.toLowerCase();return plans.find(p=>String(p.id)===id)||plans.find(p=>String(p.name||'').toLowerCase()===n)||((n==='free'||n==='p_free')?free:null);}).filter(Boolean);
    return r.length?r:[free];
  };
  function lockedBy(plans){
    const req=plans.slice().sort((a,b)=>Number(a.price||0)-Number(b.price||0))[0];
    if(!req||Number(req.price)<=0)return null;
    const cur=typeof getMyPlanData==='function'?getMyPlanData():{id:'p_free',price:0};
    if(plans.some(p=>String(p.id)===String(cur.id)))return null;
    return Number(cur.price||0)<Number(req.price||0)?req:null;
  }
  const builtinReq=type=>{
    if(!type||type==='none')return null;
    let raw;try{raw=siteContent.animationAccess&&siteContent.animationAccess['fx:'+type];}catch(_e){}
    return raw?lockedBy(plansOf(raw)):null;
  };
  const effectReq=fx=>lockedBy(plansOf(fx&&fx.accessPlans));
  function upgrade(req){
    try{if(typeof closePanel==='function')closePanel();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof goTo==='function')goTo('payment');}catch(_e){}
    try{showToast('Upgrade to '+((req&&req.name)||'a paid plan')+' to use this effect.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2600});}catch(_e){}
  }
  window.ethvelleFxLabel=function(fx,base){
    const c=fx&&fx.category&&CAT[fx.category]?' \u00b7 '+CAT[fx.category]:'';
    return base+c;
  };
  window.ethvelleFxLocked=function(fx){try{return !!effectReq(fx);}catch(_e){return false;}};
  function install(){
    const s=window.setEthvelleVisualEffect;
    if(typeof s==='function'&&!s.__evFxPlan){
      const w=function(type){const req=builtinReq(type);if(req){upgrade(req);return;}return s.apply(this,arguments);};
      w.__evFxPlan=true;window.setEthvelleVisualEffect=w;
    }
    const a=window.ethvelleAddEffect;
    if(typeof a==='function'&&!a.__evFxPlan){
      const w=function(id){
        let fx=null;try{fx=(siteContent.effects||[]).find(e=>String(e.id)===String(id));}catch(_e){}
        const req=fx&&effectReq(fx);if(req){upgrade(req);return;}
        return a.apply(this,arguments);
      };
      w.__evFxPlan=true;window.ethvelleAddEffect=w;
    }
  }
  install();setTimeout(install,0);setTimeout(install,1500);window.addEventListener('load',install);
})();
</script>
